<script type="application/json" id="sw-data">/*DATA*/</script>
<script>
(() => {
  const DATA = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('sw-data').textContent);
  const KITS = DATA.kits;
  const B64 = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';
  const PROC = SoundwellEngine().enumerate();
  PROC.forEach((e, i) => { e.slug = `${e.g}-${Object.values(e.p).join('-')}`.toLowerCase(); e.n = DATA.proc.n[i]; e.dur = Math.round(e.n / 441) / 100; e.peaks = []; for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) e.peaks.push(Math.round(B64.indexOf(DATA.proc.pk[i * 40 + k]) / 63 * 100)); e.mood = e.mood || ''; e.key = e.key || ''; e.bpm = e.bpm || 0; e.kit = e.kit || ''; e.stem = e.stem || ''; });
  // Generated families that imitate real-world sounds are retired now that real recordings cover them: hidden from
  // browsing, search and packs, but still playable wherever a saved beat, mix or creation already uses them.
  const RETIRED = new Set(['steps', 'knock', 'typing', 'clock', 'phonering', 'doorbell', 'alarmclock', 'appliance', 'drip', 'rain', 'wind', 'ocean', 'night', 'birds', 'fire', 'stream', 'crowd',
    'frog', 'buzz', 'owl', 'howl', 'whale', 'engine', 'passby', 'horn', 'thunder', 'hail', 'gunshot', 'reload', 'glass']);
  for (const e of PROC) if (RETIRED.has(e.g)) e.retired = true;
  const ALL_SOUNDS = [...DATA.sounds, ...PROC];
  const SOUNDS = ALL_SOUNDS.filter((s) => !s.retired);
  const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
  const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  const fmt = (sec) => { sec = Math.max(0, sec || 0); const m = Math.floor(sec / 60); const s = Math.floor(sec % 60); return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`; };
  const fmtDur = (sec) => sec < 1 ? `${sec.toFixed(1)} s` : fmt(sec);
  const store = {
    get(k, d) { try { const v = localStorage.getItem('sw:' + k); return v == null ? d : JSON.parse(v); } catch { return d; } },
    set(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem('sw:' + k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch {} },
  };

  // ---------- search index ----------
  const SYN = { sfx: ['effect'], ui: ['interface', 'button', 'click'], gun: ['shot', 'laser', 'blaster'], beat: ['drums', 'loop'],
    ambience: ['ambient', 'atmosphere'], ambient: ['ambience', 'atmosphere'], bgm: ['music', 'background'], scary: ['horror', 'dark', 'tension', 'creepy'],
    spooky: ['horror', 'creepy', 'haunted'], retro: ['8bit', 'chiptune'], '8bit': ['retro', 'chiptune'], chill: ['calm', 'relaxing', 'lofi'],
    football: ['sports', 'stadium', 'referee'], ghost: ['ghostly', 'haunted', 'spirit'], ghosts: ['ghost', 'haunted'], haunted: ['horror', 'spooky', 'ghost'], halloween: ['spooky', 'horror', 'haunted'], creepy: ['horror', 'spooky'], jumpscare: ['jump scare', 'stinger'], zombie: ['horror', 'moan'], paranormal: ['evp', 'ghost', 'spirit'], orchestral: ['score', 'orchestra'], orchestra: ['orchestral', 'score'], cinematic: ['score', 'orchestral', 'trailer'], film: ['score', 'cinematic'], movie: ['score', 'cinematic'], trailer: ['cinematic', 'braam', 'epic'], strings: ['string', 'violin', 'cello'], violin: ['strings'], stem: ['stems', 'kit'], stems: ['stem', 'kit'], magic: ['fantasy', 'spell'], fantasy: ['magic'], sport: ['sports'], win: ['victory', 'success', 'fanfare'], lose: ['fail', 'game over', 'sad'],
    funny: ['cartoon', 'comedy'], car: ['vehicle', 'engine'], storm: ['thunder', 'rain'], alert: ['alarm', 'notification'], notification: ['alert', 'ping'] };
  const words = (s) => String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/\b([a-g])#/g, '$1sharp').replace(/\b([a-g])b\b/g, '$1flat').normalize('NFKD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').split(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u).filter(Boolean);
  for (const s of ALL_SOUNDS) {
    s.ix = { title: words(s.title), tags: s.tags.flatMap(words), cat: words(s.cat), mood: words(s.mood), desc: words(s.desc) };
    s.kindLabel = s.kind === 'music' ? 'Music' : 'Sound effect';
  }
  const WEIGHT = { title: 10, tags: 6, cat: 5, mood: 3, desc: 1 };
  const DESIGNED = new Set(['Sci-Fi', 'Game', 'Interface', 'Synths', 'Cartoon', 'Fantasy', 'Transitions', 'Cinematic']);
  function score(s, toks) {
    let total = 0;
    for (const t of toks) {
      const alts = [t, ...(SYN[t] || []).flatMap(words)];
      let best = 0;
      for (const [f, w] of Object.entries(WEIGHT)) {
        for (const word of s.ix[f]) {
          for (const a of alts) {
            if (word === a) best = Math.max(best, w * (a === t ? 1.2 : 0.8));
            else if (a.length >= 2 && word.startsWith(a)) best = Math.max(best, w * (a === t ? 0.9 : 0.6));
          }
        }
      }
      if (!best) return 0;
      total += best;
    }
    return s.credit && !DESIGNED.has(s.cat) ? total * 1.15 : total; // real recordings rank first for real-world sounds; designed categories stay neutral
  }

  // ---------- state ----------
  const state = { q: '', kind: 'all', cats: new Set(), loops: false, saved: false, sort: 'best', open: null, edit: null, key: '', bpm: 0, pack: '', view: 'list', browse: false, nav: 'discover' };
  const canon = (slug) => (DATA.alias && DATA.alias[slug]) || slug; // retired early sounds → the recording that replaced them
  let saved = new Set(store.get('saved', []).map(canon));
  let results = [];

  function filtered({ ignoreCats = false } = {}) {
    const toks = words(state.q).slice(0, 8);
    const out = [];
    for (const s of SOUNDS) {
      if (state.kind !== 'all' && s.kind !== state.kind) continue;
      if (!ignoreCats && state.cats.size && !state.cats.has(s.cat)) continue;
      if (state.loops && !s.loop) continue;
      if (state.saved && !saved.has(s.slug)) continue;
      if (state.key && s.key !== state.key) continue;
      if (state.bpm && s.bpm !== state.bpm) continue;
      if (state.pack && s.pack !== state.pack) continue;
      const sc = toks.length ? score(s, toks) : 1;
      if (sc) out.push([s, sc]);
    }
    return { out, toks };
  }

  function compute() {
    const { out, toks } = filtered();
    const by = { short: (a, b) => a[0].dur - b[0].dur, long: (a, b) => b[0].dur - a[0].dur, az: (a, b) => a[0].title.localeCompare(b[0].title) };
    if (by[state.sort]) out.sort(by[state.sort]);
    else if (toks.length) out.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    results = out.map((x) => x[0]);
  }

  // ---------- rendering ----------
  const list = $('#list');
  function statsLine() {
    const LIB = SOUNDS.filter((s) => !s.custom); const sfx = LIB.filter((s) => s.kind === 'sfx').length; const mins = LIB.reduce((a, s) => a + s.dur, 0) / 60;
    const nf = (x) => x.toLocaleString('en-US'); const keys = new Set(SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kind === 'music' && s.key).map((s) => s.key)).size;
    $('#stats').innerHTML = `<li><b>${nf(LIB.length)}</b> sounds</li><li><b>${nf(sfx)}</b> effects</li><li><b>${nf(LIB.length - sfx)}</b> music loops and stems</li><li><b>${keys}</b> keys</li><li><b>${nf(Math.round(mins / 60))}</b> hours of audio</li><li><b>CC0</b> license</li>`;
  }
  function chips() {
    const { out } = filtered({ ignoreCats: true });
    const counts = {}; for (const [s] of out) counts[s.cat] = (counts[s.cat] || 0) + 1;
    const all = [...new Set(SOUNDS.filter((s) => state.kind === 'all' || s.kind === state.kind).map((s) => s.cat))]
      .sort((a, b) => (counts[b] || 0) - (counts[a] || 0) || a.localeCompare(b));
    const nSaved = saved.size;
    $('#chips').innerHTML =
      `<button type="button" class="chip opt" data-opt="loops" aria-pressed="${state.loops}">Seamless loops</button>` +
      `<button type="button" class="chip opt" data-opt="saved" aria-pressed="${state.saved}" ${nSaved || state.saved ? '' : 'disabled'}>Saved <span class="n">${nSaved}</span></button>` +
      `<span class="chip-sep" aria-hidden="true"></span>` +
      all.map((c) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="${esc(c)}" aria-pressed="${state.cats.has(c)}" ${!counts[c] && !state.cats.has(c) ? 'disabled' : ''}>${esc(c)} <span class="n">${counts[c] || 0}</span></button>`).join('');
  }
  function metaHtml(s) {
    const bits = [`<span>${esc(s.cat)}</span>`];
    if (s.bpm) bits.push(`<span class="m">${s.bpm} BPM</span>`);
    if (s.key) bits.push(`<span>${esc(s.key)}</span>`);
    if (s.mood) bits.push(`<span>${esc(s.mood)}</span>`);
    if (s.loop) bits.push(`<span class="loop">Loop</span>`);
    return bits.join('');
  }
  function rowHtml(s) {
    const on = saved.has(s.slug); const tags = s.tags.filter((t) => !/^\d+ bpm$|^loop$/.test(t)).slice(0, 3);
    return `<li class="row${cur === s ? ' playing' : ''}" data-slug="${s.slug}" id="r-${s.slug}">
      <button class="play" type="button" data-act="play" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})" aria-label="${isPlaying(s) ? 'Pause' : 'Play'} ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${isPlaying(s) ? 'pause' : 'play'}"/></svg></button>
      <div class="info"><h3 style="margin:0;font-size:inherit"><button class="ttl" type="button" data-act="open" aria-expanded="${state.open === s.slug}" aria-controls="d-${s.slug}">${esc(s.title)}</button></h3>
        <p class="meta"><span class="pk">${esc(packName(s))}</span>${tags.map((t) => `<button type="button" class="tg" data-tag="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}${s.loop ? '<span class="loop">Loop</span>' : ''}<span class="mobilecols">${[fmtDur(s.dur), shortKey(s.key), s.bpm ? s.bpm + ' BPM' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}</span></p><p class="desc">${esc(s.desc)}</p></div>
      <canvas class="wave" data-act="seek" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
      <span class="cols"><span class="c-dur">${fmtDur(s.dur)}</span><span class="c-key">${esc(shortKey(s.key)) || '–'}</span><span class="c-bpm">${s.bpm || '–'}</span></span>
      <div class="acts"><span class="dur">${fmtDur(s.dur)}</span>
        <button class="ib${inMix(s) ? ' inmix' : ''}" type="button" data-act="mix" aria-pressed="${inMix(s)}" aria-label="${inMix(s) ? 'Remove from mixer' : 'Add to mixer'}: ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg></button>
        <button class="ib" type="button" data-act="save" aria-pressed="${on}" aria-label="${on ? 'Remove from saved' : 'Save'}: ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-heart"/></svg></button>
        <button class="ib dl" type="button" data-act="dl" aria-label="Download ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg></button></div>
      ${state.open === s.slug ? detailHtml(s) : ''}
    </li>`;
  }
  function credit(s) { return s.credit ? `"${s.title}" by ${s.credit.by} on Freesound (CC0 1.0)` : `"${s.title}" by SoundWell (CC0 1.0)`; }
  function similar(s) {
    const tagset = new Set(s.tags);
    return SOUNDS.filter((o) => o !== s && o.kind === s.kind)
      .map((o) => [o, o.tags.filter((t) => tagset.has(t)).length * 2 + (o.cat === s.cat ? 3 : 0)])
      .filter((x) => x[1] > 0).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x[0]);
  }
  function detailHtml(s) {
    const sim = similar(s);
    return `<div class="detail" id="d-${s.slug}">
      <div><p>${esc(s.desc)}</p>${s.credit ? `<p class="recby">🎙 Recorded by <a href="${esc(s.credit.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(s.credit.by)}</a> on Freesound · CC0, no credit required</p>` : ''}${s.custom ? '' : `<p style="margin:0 0 8px;font-size:.86rem">From the pack <button class="clear" type="button" data-pack="${s.pack}">${esc(packName(s))}</button></p>`}
        <dl class="facts"><dt>Type</dt><dd>${s.kindLabel}</dd><dt>Length</dt><dd>${fmtDur(s.dur)}</dd>${s.bpm ? `<dt>Tempo</dt><dd>${s.bpm} BPM</dd>` : ''}${s.key ? `<dt>Key</dt><dd>${esc(s.key)}</dd>` : ''}<dt>Seamless loop</dt><dd>${s.loop ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</dd><dt>Format</dt><dd>${s.custom ? 'Your custom sound · 16-bit WAV' : s.g ? 'Generated in your browser · 16-bit WAV · 44.1 kHz' : s.sp ? 'Recording · downloads as 16-bit WAV' : 'MP3 · 192 kbps · 44.1 kHz'}</dd></dl>
        <div class="tags">${s.tags.map((t) => `<button type="button" data-tag="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        ${s.kit ? `<p style="margin:10px 0 0;font-size:.86rem">Part of the <b>${esc(kitOf(s.kit).name)}</b> stem kit (${kitOf(s.kit).bpm} BPM, ${esc(kitOf(s.kit).key)}). Every stem lines up with the others.</p>` : ''}
        <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:10px">
          <button class="btn" type="button" data-act="seq"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-seq"/></svg>Add to sequencer</button>
          <button class="btn" type="button" data-act="game"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-game"/></svg>+ Game pack</button>
          <button class="btn" type="button" data-act="mix"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg>${inMix(s) ? 'Remove from mixer' : s.loop ? 'Add to mixer' : 'Add to mixer as a pad'}</button>
          ${s.kit ? `<button class="btn" type="button" data-kit="${s.kit}">Open the whole kit</button>` : ''}
          ${s.custom ? `<button class="btn" type="button" data-act="delmine">Delete this creation</button>` : ''}
          <button class="btn" type="button" data-act="edit" aria-expanded="${state.edit === s.slug}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-sliders"/></svg>${state.edit === s.slug ? 'Close editor' : 'Edit and tweak'}</button>
        </div></div>
      <div class="lic"><h4>Can I use this? <span class="badge">CC0</span></h4>
        <ul><li>Yes, for anything: videos, games, podcasts, apps, ads.</li><li>Commercial use is fine.</li><li>No credit required. If you'd like to give one:</li></ul>
        <p class="credit" id="c-${s.slug}">${esc(credit(s))}</p>
        <div class="btnrow"><button class="btn" type="button" data-act="copy"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-copy"/></svg>Copy credit</button>
          <button class="btn primary dl" type="button" data-act="dl"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg>Download</button></div></div>
      ${state.edit === s.slug ? editorHtml(s) : ''}
      ${sim.length ? `<div class="similar"><h4>More like this</h4><ul>${sim.map((o) => `<li><button type="button" data-goto="${o.slug}">${esc(o.title)} <span class="m">${fmtDur(o.dur)}</span></button></li>`).join('')}</ul></div>` : ''}
    </div>`;
  }
  function header() {
    const n = results.length; const label = n === 1 ? 'sound' : 'sounds'; const p = state.pack ? PACKS.get(state.pack) : null;
    const what = state.q ? `Results for “${esc(state.q)}”` : p ? 'In this pack' : state.saved ? 'Saved sounds' : state.cats.has('Your Creations') ? 'Your creations' : navKey() === 'score' ? 'Cinematic score' : navKey() === 'spooky' ? 'Spooky and horror' : state.kind === 'music' ? 'Music' : state.kind === 'sfx' ? 'Sound effects' : state.browse ? 'All sounds' : 'New and notable';
    $('#reshead').innerHTML = `${what}<span>${n.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${label}</span>`;
    $('#clear').hidden = !(state.q || state.cats.size || state.loops || state.saved || state.kind !== 'all' || state.key || state.bpm || state.pack);
    $('#packhead').innerHTML = p ? `<div class="packhero"><span class="cover" style="background-image:url(${packArt(p, 360, false)})"></span><div><span class="eyebrow">Pack · ${esc(p.cat)}</span><h2>${esc(p.name)}</h2><p>${esc(p.desc)}</p><span class="pmeta">${p.count.toLocaleString('en-US')} sounds · CC0</span></div></div>` : '';
    const discover = isDiscover(); $('#mast').classList.toggle('off', !discover); if (discover) requestAnimationFrame(drawHero);
    for (const b2 of document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]')) b2.setAttribute('aria-current', String(b2.dataset.nav === navKey()));
    const set = (k, v) => { for (const el of document.querySelectorAll(`[data-cnt="${k}"]`)) el.textContent = v || ''; };
    set('mine', SOUNDS.filter((x) => x.custom).length); set('saved', saved.size); set('mix', mix.length);
  }
  const isDiscover = () => !state.q && !state.cats.size && !state.loops && !state.saved && state.kind === 'all' && !state.pack && !state.browse && state.view === 'list' && !state.key && !state.bpm;
  function navKey() {
    if (state.view === 'packs') return 'packs'; if (state.view === 'seq') return 'seq'; if (state.view === 'video') return 'video'; if (state.view === 'game') return 'game'; if (state.saved) return 'saved'; if (state.cats.has('Your Creations')) return 'mine'; if (state.cats.has('Stem Kits') && state.cats.size === 1) return 'kits';
    if (state.cats.has('Drum Hits') && state.cats.has('Musical FX')) return 'oneshots'; if (state.cats.has('Score Loops') && state.cats.has('Score One-Shots') && state.cats.size === 2) return 'score'; if (state.cats.has('Horror') && state.cats.size === 1) return 'spooky'; if (state.kind === 'music' && state.loops) return 'loops'; if (isDiscover()) return 'discover'; if (state.browse && !state.q && !state.cats.size && !state.pack) return 'all'; return '';
  }
  function resetFilters() { state.q = ''; $('#q').value = ''; state.cats.clear(); state.kind = 'all'; state.loops = false; state.saved = false; state.key = ''; state.bpm = 0; state.pack = ''; state.browse = false; state.view = 'list'; state.open = null; syncSeg(); }
  function nav(key) {
    if (key === 'mixer') { $('#mixbtn').click(); return; }
    resetFilters();
    if (key === 'all') state.browse = true; else if (key === 'packs') state.view = 'packs'; else if (key === 'seq') state.view = 'seq'; else if (key === 'video') state.view = 'video'; else if (key === 'game') state.view = 'game'; else if (key === 'kits') state.cats.add('Stem Kits');
    else if (key === 'loops') { state.kind = 'music'; state.loops = true; syncSeg(); } else if (key === 'oneshots') { state.cats.add('Drum Hits'); state.cats.add('Musical FX'); }
    else if (key === 'mine') state.cats.add('Your Creations'); else if (key === 'score') { state.cats.add('Score Loops'); state.cats.add('Score One-Shots'); } else if (key === 'spooky') state.cats.add('Horror'); else if (key === 'saved') state.saved = true;
    render(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }
  function openPack(id) { resetFilters(); state.pack = id; render(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0 }); }
  function render() {
    compute(); chips(); header(); renderHome(); musicFilters(); $('#keyf').value = state.key; $('#bpmf').value = state.bpm || '';
    const seqView = state.view === 'seq'; const vidView = state.view === 'video'; const gameView = state.view === 'game'; const tool = seqView || vidView || gameView;
    $('#seqview').hidden = !seqView; $('#vidview').hidden = !vidView; $('#gameview').hidden = !gameView; document.body.classList.toggle('seqmode', seqView); document.body.classList.toggle('vidmode', vidView); list.hidden = tool; document.querySelector('.chips').hidden = tool;
    if (!vidView && VID.playing) vidPause();
    if (tool) { $('#packs').hidden = true; $('#colhead').hidden = true; document.querySelector('.reshead').hidden = true; if (seqView) renderSeq(); else if (vidView) renderVid(); else renderGame(); return; }
    const packsView = state.view === 'packs'; $('#packs').hidden = !packsView; $('#colhead').hidden = packsView; document.querySelector('.reshead').hidden = packsView;
    if (packsView) { list.innerHTML = ''; const all = [...PACKS.values()].sort((a, b) => a.cat.localeCompare(b.cat) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name)); $('#packs').innerHTML = `<div class="secth"><h2>All packs</h2><span class="pmeta" style="font:500 .8rem var(--f-mono);color:var(--muted)">${all.length} packs</span></div>` + all.map(pcardHtml).join(''); return; }
    if (!results.length) {
      list.innerHTML = `<li class="empty"><h3>No sounds match${state.q ? ` “${esc(state.q)}”` : ''}</h3><p>Try fewer words, or one of these:</p>
        <div class="tags">${['whistle', 'rain', 'laser', 'lofi', 'footsteps', 'crowd', 'retro', 'horror'].map((t) => `<button type="button" data-tag="${t}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div></li>`;
      return;
    }
    shown = Math.min(PAGE, results.length);
    list.innerHTML = results.slice(0, shown).map(rowHtml).join('') + moreHtml();
    drawAll(); watchMore();
  }
  const PAGE = 40; let shown = 0;
  const moreHtml = () => (shown < results.length ? `<li class="more" id="more"><button class="btn" type="button" id="morebtn">Show more (${(results.length - shown).toLocaleString('en-US')} left)</button></li>` : '');
  function showMore(upTo) {
    const target = Math.min(results.length, Math.max(shown + PAGE, upTo || 0)); if (target <= shown) return;
    $('#more')?.remove(); const tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = results.slice(shown, target).map(rowHtml).join('') + (target < results.length ? '' : '');
    const added = [...tmp.children]; for (const el of added) list.appendChild(el); shown = target;
    if (shown < results.length) list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', moreHtml());
    for (const el of added) { const c = el.querySelector('canvas.wave'); if (c) { const s = bySlug[el.dataset.slug]; draw(c, s, cur === s ? progressOf() : 0); } }
    watchMore();
  }
  function ensureShown(i) { if (i >= shown) showMore(i + 10); }
  let moreObs = null;
  function watchMore() { moreObs?.disconnect(); const m = $('#more'); if (!m || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return; moreObs = new IntersectionObserver((es) => { if (es.some((x) => x.isIntersecting)) showMore(); }, { rootMargin: '600px' }); moreObs.observe(m); }

  // ---------- waveforms ----------
  let colors = {};
  function readColors() { const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement); colors = { wave: cs.getPropertyValue('--wave').trim(), accent: cs.getPropertyValue('--accent').trim() }; }
  function draw(canvas, s, progress = 0) {
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; const w = canvas.clientWidth; const h = canvas.clientHeight;
    if (!w || !h) return;
    if (canvas.width !== Math.round(w * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(h * dpr)) { canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr); canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr); }
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const n = Math.max(12, Math.min(s.peaks.length, Math.floor(w / 3.5))); const bw = w / n;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const p = s.peaks[Math.floor(i * s.peaks.length / n)] / 100;
      const bh = Math.max(2, p * (h - 4));
      ctx.fillStyle = (i + 0.5) / n <= progress ? colors.accent : colors.wave;
      ctx.fillRect(i * bw + bw * 0.18, (h - bh) / 2, Math.max(1, bw * 0.64), bh);
    }
  }
  function drawAll() { for (const c of list.querySelectorAll('canvas.wave')) { const s = bySlug[c.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; draw(c, s, cur === s ? progressOf() : 0); } }
  const bySlug = Object.fromEntries(ALL_SOUNDS.map((s) => [s.slug, s]));
  for (const [old, now] of Object.entries(DATA.alias || {})) if (!bySlug[old] && bySlug[now]) bySlug[old] = bySlug[now];

    'sc-riser': 'Orchestral Risers', 'sc-timpani': 'Timpani', 'sc-cymbal': 'Cymbals and Gongs', 'sc-gliss': 'Harp Glissandos', 'sc-celesta': 'Celesta', 'sc-choirstab': 'Choir Stabs', 'sc-pizznote': 'Pizzicato Notes', 'sc-solo': 'Solo Strings', 'sc-brassstab': 'Brass Stabs',
    'hr-creak': 'Creaks and Groans', 'hr-whisper': 'Ghostly Whispers', 'hr-drone': 'Dread Drones', 'hr-scare': 'Jump Scares', 'hr-riser': 'Horror Risers', 'hr-musicbox': 'Creepy Music Boxes', 'hr-breath': 'Breathing in the Dark',

    'hr-bell': 'Tolling Bells', 'hr-organ': 'Haunted Organ', 'hr-reverse': 'Reversed and Unsettling', 'hr-place': 'Haunted Places' };
  const recCount = {}; for (const s of SOUNDS) if (s.credit) recCount[s.cat] = (recCount[s.cat] || 0) + 1;
  const packIdOf = (s) => (s.custom ? 'mine' : s.credit ? (recCount[s.cat] >= 5 ? 'rec-' + s.cat.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-') : 'rec-more') : s.g ? s.g : 'orig-' + s.cat.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-'));
  for (const s of ALL_SOUNDS) s.pack = packIdOf(s);
  const PACKS = new Map();
  for (const s of SOUNDS) {
    if (s.custom) continue;
    let p = PACKS.get(s.pack);
    if (!p) { p = { id: s.pack, name: s.credit ? (s.pack === 'rec-more' ? 'More Real Recordings' : `Real ${({ 'Ambience Layers': 'Ambience' })[s.cat] || s.cat} Recordings`) : s.g ? (PACK_NAMES[s.g] || s.cat) : `${s.cat} Originals`, cat: s.cat, kind: s.kind, count: 0, tags: new Map(), first: s }; PACKS.set(s.pack, p); }
    p.count++; for (const t of s.tags.slice(0, 6)) p.tags.set(t, (p.tags.get(t) || 0) + 1);
  }
  for (const p of PACKS.values()) { p.top = [...p.tags.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map((x) => x[0]).filter((t) => t !== 'loop').slice(0, 4); p.desc = `${p.count.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${p.kind === 'music' ? 'music loops and one-shots' : 'sound effects'} in ${p.cat}. ${p.top.length ? 'Tagged ' + p.top.join(', ') + '.' : ''}`; }
  const FEATURED = ['rec-ambience-layers', 'rec-foley', 'rec-nature', 'rec-animals', 'chords', 'drumloop', 'kitstem', 'magic', 'rain', 'laser', 'drumhit', 'padtex', 'gunshot', 'birds', 'hud', 'toon', 'whoosh', 'melody'].filter((id) => PACKS.has(id));
  const SCORE_FEATURED = ['sc-legato', 'sc-ostinato', 'sc-braam', 'sc-horns', 'sc-choir', 'sc-perc', 'sc-hit', 'sc-piano', 'sc-harp', 'sc-tension', 'sc-swell', 'sc-timpani'].filter((id) => PACKS.has(id));
  const SPOOKY_FEATURED = ['hr-scare', 'hr-place', 'hr-whisper', 'hr-creak', 'hr-musicbox', 'hr-ghost', 'hr-drone', 'hr-organ', 'hr-approach', 'hr-evp', 'hr-chains', 'hr-wind'].filter((id) => PACKS.has(id));
  const packName = (s) => (s.custom ? 'Your Creations' : PACKS.get(s.pack)?.name || s.cat);

  // ---------- generative cover art ----------
  const PALETTES = [
    ['#ff6b4a', '#ffb347', '#2b1d4e', '#ff3d7f', '#ffe0b5'], ['#00c2a8', '#0f4c75', '#7ae7ff', '#1b1b3a', '#e7fffb'], ['#8a5cff', '#ff5ca8', '#16112b', '#ffd36e', '#c9b8ff'],
    ['#ffd23f', '#ee4266', '#540d6e', '#3bceac', '#fff1c1'], ['#2d3047', '#93b7be', '#e0ca3c', '#a799b7', '#048a81'], ['#f25f5c', '#ffe066', '#247ba0', '#70c1b3', '#1f2933'],
    ['#0b132b', '#3a506b', '#5bc0be', '#6fffe9', '#1c2541'], ['#3d0c02', '#c1440e', '#f2a65a', '#ffdd99', '#5b2a86'], ['#14213d', '#fca311', '#e5e5e5', '#ff006e', '#8338ec'],
