  ];
  const STYLE_BY_CAT = { 'Ambience Layers': 'waves', Nature: 'waves', Weather: 'waves', Animals: 'waves', 'Pad Textures': 'waves', 'Sci-Fi': 'orbits', Interface: 'orbits', Alarms: 'orbits',
    'Drum Loops': 'bars', 'Drum Hits': 'bars', 'Bass Loops': 'bars', Weapons: 'bars', Impacts: 'bars', 'Chord Loops': 'blobs', 'Melody Loops': 'blobs', 'Arpeggio Loops': 'blobs', Jingles: 'blobs',
    'Stem Kits': 'blobs', 'Musical FX': 'blobs', Fantasy: 'blobs', Game: 'grid', Cartoon: 'grid', Household: 'grid', Foley: 'grid', Vehicles: 'grid', Horror: 'eerie', Transitions: 'sunset', Sports: 'sunset', 'Score Loops': 'staves', 'Score One-Shots': 'staves' };
  function hashStr(str) { let h = 2166136261; for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619); } return h >>> 0; }
  function paint(ctx, W, H, id, style, pal) {
    const r = rng(hashStr(id)); const [a, b, c, d, e] = pal;
    const lg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, H); lg.addColorStop(0, c); lg.addColorStop(1, style === 'grid' ? a : d === '#e5e5e5' ? a : c); ctx.fillStyle = lg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    if (style === 'waves') {
      const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); sky.addColorStop(0, e); sky.addColorStop(1, c); ctx.fillStyle = sky; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      const cols = [b, a, d, c]; for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const y0 = H * (0.3 + k * 0.12); const amp = H * (0.03 + r() * 0.06); const fr = 1 + r() * 2.5; const ph = r() * 6.28;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, H); for (let x = 0; x <= W; x += W / 60) ctx.lineTo(x, y0 + Math.sin(x / W * Math.PI * 2 * fr + ph) * amp); ctx.lineTo(W, H); ctx.closePath(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55 + k * 0.07; ctx.fillStyle = cols[k % 4]; ctx.fill(); }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    } else if (style === 'orbits') {
      ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); const cx = W * (0.35 + r() * 0.3), cy = H * (0.35 + r() * 0.3);
      const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, W * 0.6); glow.addColorStop(0, a); glow.addColorStop(0.35, b + '55'); glow.addColorStop(1, c + '00'); ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, W / 120); for (let k = 1; k < 8; k++) { ctx.strokeStyle = k % 2 ? e + 'aa' : b + '88'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, W * 0.07 * k, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke(); const t = r() * 6.28; ctx.fillStyle = k % 3 ? d : a; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(t) * W * 0.07 * k, cy + Math.sin(t) * W * 0.07 * k, W * 0.018, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); }
    } else if (style === 'bars') {
      ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); const n = 22; const bw = W / n; const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H * 0.15, 0, H * 0.85); g.addColorStop(0, a); g.addColorStop(0.5, b); g.addColorStop(1, d);
      ctx.fillStyle = g; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const env = Math.sin(Math.PI * (i + 0.5) / n); const hgt = H * (0.08 + 0.7 * env * (0.35 + 0.65 * r())); ctx.fillRect(i * bw + bw * 0.18, (H - hgt) / 2, bw * 0.64, hgt); }
      ctx.fillStyle = e + '33'; ctx.fillRect(0, H / 2 - Math.max(1, H / 160), W, Math.max(2, H / 80));
    } else if (style === 'blobs') {
      ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const x = r() * W, y = r() * H, rad = W * (0.25 + r() * 0.45); const col = [a, b, d, e][k % 4]; const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, rad); g.addColorStop(0, col + 'ee'); g.addColorStop(1, col + '00'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
      ctx.strokeStyle = e + '55'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, W / 160); for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { ctx.beginPath(); const y = H * (0.3 + k * 0.2); ctx.moveTo(0, y); for (let x = 0; x <= W; x += W / 40) ctx.lineTo(x, y + Math.sin(x / W * 9 + k) * H * 0.04); ctx.stroke(); }
    } else if (style === 'grid') {
      ctx.fillStyle = a; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); const n = 8; const s = W / n; const ph = r() * 6.28;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) { const v = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(i * 0.9 + j * 0.6 + ph); ctx.fillStyle = (i + j) % 3 === 0 ? c : (i + j) % 3 === 1 ? d : b; const sz = s * (0.2 + 0.55 * v); if ((i * 7 + j) % 4 === 0) ctx.fillRect(i * s + (s - sz) / 2, j * s + (s - sz) / 2, sz, sz); else { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(i * s + s / 2, j * s + s / 2, sz / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); } }
    } else if (style === 'eerie') {
      const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); sky.addColorStop(0, '#05060c'); sky.addColorStop(0.6, c); sky.addColorStop(1, '#020205'); ctx.fillStyle = sky; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      const mx = W * (0.25 + r() * 0.5), my = H * (0.2 + r() * 0.15), mr = W * (0.1 + r() * 0.06); const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(mx, my, mr * 0.6, mx, my, mr * 4); glow.addColorStop(0, e + '66'); glow.addColorStop(1, e + '00'); ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.fillStyle = e; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(mx, my, mr, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(mx + mr * 0.3, my - mr * 0.2, mr * 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { ctx.globalAlpha = 0.18; ctx.fillStyle = b; const y = H * (0.5 + k * 0.1); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); for (let x = 0; x <= W; x += W / 30) ctx.lineTo(x, y + Math.sin(x / W * 7 + k + r()) * H * 0.02); ctx.lineTo(W, y + H * 0.05); ctx.lineTo(0, y + H * 0.05); ctx.fill(); }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = '#020204'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, H); for (let x = 0; x <= W; x += W / 20) ctx.lineTo(x, H * 0.78 - Math.sin(x / W * 3 + r()) * H * 0.05); ctx.lineTo(W, H); ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#020204'; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; const branch = (x, y, len, ang, w, depth) => { if (depth === 0 || len < W * 0.01) return; const x2 = x + Math.cos(ang) * len, y2 = y + Math.sin(ang) * len; ctx.lineWidth = w; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke(); branch(x2, y2, len * 0.72, ang - 0.35 - r() * 0.3, w * 0.68, depth - 1); branch(x2, y2, len * 0.68, ang + 0.3 + r() * 0.35, w * 0.66, depth - 1); };
      const trees = 1 + Math.floor(r() * 2); for (let k = 0; k < trees; k++) { const tx = W * (k ? 0.75 + r() * 0.15 : 0.1 + r() * 0.2); branch(tx, H * 0.82, H * 0.2, -Math.PI / 2 + (r() - 0.5) * 0.2, W * 0.03, 7); }
    } else if (style === 'staves') {
      const g0 = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, H); g0.addColorStop(0, c); g0.addColorStop(1, d); ctx.fillStyle = g0; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * 0.75, H * 0.3, 0, W * 0.75, H * 0.3, W * 0.7); glow.addColorStop(0, a + 'aa'); glow.addColorStop(1, a + '00'); ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, W / 220); for (let g = 0; g < 3; g++) { const y0 = H * (0.18 + g * 0.27) + (r() - 0.5) * H * 0.04; ctx.strokeStyle = e + '55'; for (let l = 0; l < 5; l++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y0 + l * H * 0.022); ctx.lineTo(W, y0 + l * H * 0.022); ctx.stroke(); }
        for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const x = W * (0.1 + k * 0.18 + r() * 0.05), y = y0 + Math.floor(r() * 9) * H * 0.011; ctx.fillStyle = k % 2 ? b : e; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(x, y, W * 0.016, H * 0.011, -0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillRect(x + W * 0.013, y - H * 0.07, Math.max(1, W / 160), H * 0.07); } }
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, W / 60); const rib = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, 0); rib.addColorStop(0, b + '00'); rib.addColorStop(0.5, b + 'cc'); rib.addColorStop(1, a + '00'); ctx.strokeStyle = rib;
      ctx.beginPath(); for (let x = 0; x <= W; x += W / 50) { const y = H * (0.55 + 0.18 * Math.sin(x / W * Math.PI * (1.5 + r() * 0.02) + 1)); x ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y); } ctx.stroke();
    } else {
      const n = 7; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { ctx.fillStyle = [c, d, a, b, e, a, c][k]; ctx.fillRect(0, H * k / n, W, H / n + 1); }
      ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * (0.3 + r() * 0.4), H * 0.55, W * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.clip(); const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H * 0.25, 0, H * 0.85); g.addColorStop(0, e); g.addColorStop(1, a); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.fillStyle = c; for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) ctx.fillRect(0, H * (0.55 + k * 0.06), W, H * 0.012 * (k + 1)); ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  const ART = new Map();
  function artFor(id, cat, size, label) {
    const key = `${id}|${size}|${label ? 1 : 0}`; if (ART.has(key)) return ART.get(key);
    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = size; const ctx = cv.getContext('2d'); const h = hashStr(id);
    const style = STYLE_BY_CAT[cat] || ['waves', 'orbits', 'bars', 'blobs', 'grid', 'sunset'][h % 6]; const SCORE_PAL = [6, 9, 2, 7, 11, 1]; const EERIE = [['#6a2c91', '#9be564', '#2a1840', '#c7f9cc', '#f2f2e9'], ['#7a1c1c', '#e0c097', '#3b0f0f', '#ffb4a2', '#fdf0d5'], ['#1b3a4b', '#90e0ef', '#14213d', '#caf0f8', '#e9f5ff'], ['#3d405b', '#f2cc8f', '#22223b', '#e07a5f', '#fff3d6']];
    const pal = cat === 'Horror' ? EERIE[h % EERIE.length] : /^Score/.test(cat) ? PALETTES[SCORE_PAL[h % SCORE_PAL.length]] : PALETTES[h % PALETTES.length];
    paint(ctx, size, size, id, style, pal);
    if (label) { const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, size * 0.55, 0, size); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, size * 0.5, size, size * 0.5);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = `700 ${Math.round(size * 0.05)}px "JetBrains Mono", monospace`; ctx.fillText('SOUNDWELL', size * 0.07, size * 0.9);
      ctx.font = `800 ${Math.round(size * 0.085)}px "Bricolage Grotesque", sans-serif`; const words2 = String(label).split(' '); let line = '', y = size * 0.78, lines = []; for (const w of words2) { if (ctx.measureText(line + w).width > size * 0.86 && line) { lines.push(line.trim()); line = ''; } line += w + ' '; } lines.push(line.trim()); lines = lines.slice(-2);
      lines.reverse().forEach((ln, i) => ctx.fillText(ln, size * 0.07, y - i * size * 0.095)); }
    let url = ''; try { url = cv.toDataURL('image/png'); } catch {} ART.set(key, url); return url;
  }
  const packArt = (p, size, label) => artFor(p.id, p.cat, size, label ? p.name : '');
  const soundArt = (s) => (s.custom ? artFor('mine', 'Fantasy', 96) : artFor(s.pack, PACKS.get(s.pack)?.cat || s.cat, 96));
  const kitArt = (k, w) => artFor('kit-' + k.id, 'Stem Kits', w);
  function drawHero() {
    const cv = $('#heroart'); if (!cv || !cv.clientWidth) return; const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1); const W = Math.round(cv.clientWidth * dpr), H = Math.round(cv.clientHeight * dpr); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
    paint(ctx, W, H, 'hero-soundwell', 'waves', ['#1f6bff', '#22d3ff', '#050813', '#123a8f', '#0b1530']);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, 0); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); const n = 70; const bw = W / n; const r = rng(7);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = i * bw; const env = 0.3 + 0.7 * Math.abs(Math.sin(i / n * Math.PI * 3)); const hgt = H * 0.5 * env * (0.4 + 0.6 * r()); ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(110,210,255,${0.08 + 0.1 * (i / n)})`; ctx.fillRect(x + bw * 0.25, H * 0.42 - hgt / 2, bw * 0.5, hgt); }
  }
  const shortKey = (k) => (k ? k.replace(' minor', 'm').replace(' major', '') : '');
  function pcardHtml(p) { return `<button type="button" class="pcard" data-pack="${p.id}"><span class="cover" style="background-image:url(${packArt(p, 320, true)})"></span><span class="pn">${esc(p.name)}</span><span class="pm">${p.count.toLocaleString('en-US')} sounds · ${esc(p.cat)}</span></button>`; }


  new ResizeObserver(() => drawAll()).observe(list);
  const themeChanged = () => { readColors(); drawAll(); };
  matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').addEventListener('change', themeChanged);
  new MutationObserver(themeChanged).observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-theme'] });

  // ---------- player (Web Audio: plays MP3 files and sounds generated in the browser) ----------
  let cur = null; let raf = 0; let vol = store.get('vol', 0.8); $('#vol').value = Math.round(vol * 100);
  const P = { buf: null, src: null, t0: 0, off: 0, playing: false, loading: false, gain: null };
  let looping = store.get('loop', false); setLoopUi();
  function pGain() { const c = ac(); if (!P.gain) { P.gain = c.createGain(); P.gain.connect(c.destination); } P.gain.gain.value = vol; return P.gain; }
  function pTime() { if (!P.buf) return 0; if (!P.playing) return P.off; let t = actx.currentTime - P.t0; if (looping) t %= P.buf.duration; return Math.max(0, Math.min(t, P.buf.duration)); }
  const isPlaying = (s) => cur === s && (P.playing || P.loading);
  const progressOf = () => (P.buf ? pTime() / P.buf.duration : 0);
  function pStopSrc() { if (P.src) { P.src.onended = null; try { P.src.stop(); } catch {} P.src.disconnect(); P.src = null; } }
  function pStartAt(off) {
    const c = ac(); pStopSrc(); const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = P.buf; src.loop = looping; src.connect(pGain());
    off = Math.max(0, Math.min(off, P.buf.duration - 0.001)); src.start(0, off); P.t0 = c.currentTime - off; P.src = src; P.playing = true;
    src.onended = () => { if (P.src === src) { P.src = null; P.playing = false; P.off = 0; syncRows(); tick(); } };
    syncRows(); cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  function pPause() { P.off = pTime(); pStopSrc(); P.playing = false; syncRows(); tick(); }
  function pSeek(t) { if (!P.buf) return; if (P.playing) pStartAt(t); else { P.off = Math.max(0, Math.min(t, P.buf.duration)); tick(); } }
  function rowOf(s) { return s && document.getElementById('r-' + s.slug); }
  function syncRows() {
    for (const r of list.querySelectorAll('.row')) {
      const s = bySlug[r.dataset.slug]; const isCur = s === cur; const playing = isPlaying(s);
      r.classList.toggle('playing', isCur);
      const b = r.querySelector('.play'); b.setAttribute('aria-label', `${playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'} ${s.title}`); b.querySelector('use').setAttribute('href', `#i-${playing ? 'pause' : 'play'}`);
      b.classList.toggle('busy', isCur && P.loading);
      if (!isCur) draw(r.querySelector('canvas'), s, 0);
    }
    const on = P.playing || P.loading; $('#pp').setAttribute('aria-label', on ? 'Pause' : 'Play'); $('#pp use').setAttribute('href', on ? '#i-pause' : '#i-play');
  }
  function tick() {
    const r = rowOf(cur); if (r) draw(r.querySelector('canvas'), cur, progressOf());
    $('#cur').textContent = fmt(pTime()); if (!seeking) $('#seek').value = Math.round(progressOf() * 1000);
    if (P.playing) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  async function load(s, at = 0) {
    ac(); cur = s; pStopSrc(); P.buf = null; P.playing = false; P.off = 0; P.loading = true;
    $('#transport').hidden = false; $('#nowt').textContent = s.title; $('#tot').textContent = fmtDur(s.dur); $('#cur').textContent = '0:00';
    syncRows();
    try { const buf = await getBuf(s); if (cur !== s) return; P.buf = buf; P.loading = false; pStartAt(at * buf.duration); $('#live').textContent = `Playing ${s.title}`; }
    catch { if (cur === s) { P.loading = false; syncRows(); toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”. Try again.`); } }
  }
  function toggle(s) {
    if (s && s !== cur) return load(s);
    if (!cur) return results[0] && load(results[0]);
    if (P.loading) return;
    if (P.playing) pPause(); else if (P.buf) pStartAt(P.off); else load(cur);
  }
  function step(d) {
    const pool = results.length ? results : SOUNDS; let i = pool.indexOf(cur);
    i = i < 0 ? 0 : (i + d + pool.length) % pool.length; ensureShown(i); load(pool[i]);
    rowOf(pool[i])?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
  }
  function setLoopUi() { $('#loopbtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(looping)); $('#loopbtn').style.color = looping ? 'var(--meter)' : ''; if (P && P.src) P.src.loop = looping; }
  let seeking = false;
  $('#seek').addEventListener('input', (e) => { seeking = true; if (P.buf) pSeek(e.target.value / 1000 * P.buf.duration); });
  $('#seek').addEventListener('change', () => { seeking = false; });
  $('#vol').addEventListener('input', (e) => { vol = e.target.value / 100; store.set('vol', vol); if (P.gain) P.gain.gain.value = vol; });
  $('#pp').addEventListener('click', () => toggle());
  $('#prev').addEventListener('click', () => step(-1));
  $('#next').addEventListener('click', () => step(1));
  $('#loopbtn').addEventListener('click', () => { looping = !looping; store.set('loop', looping); if (P.playing) { const t = pTime(); setLoopUi(); pStartAt(t); } else setLoopUi(); });

  // ---------- procedural engine: renders generated sounds in a background worker ----------
  let worker = null; let wid = 0; const pending = new Map(); let workerFailed = false; let ENGINE_MAIN = null;
  function getWorker() {
    if (worker || workerFailed) return worker;
    try {
      const src = SoundwellEngine.toString() + '\nconst E = SoundwellEngine();\nonmessage = (e) => { const { id, g, p, m } = e.data; try { const o = E.renderEntry(g, p, m); if (o.stereo) postMessage({ id, l: o.l, r: o.r }, [o.l.buffer, o.r.buffer]); else postMessage({ id, l: o.l, r: null }, [o.l.buffer]); } catch (err) { postMessage({ id, err: String(err) }); } };';
      worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([src], { type: 'text/javascript' })));
      worker.onmessage = (e) => { const r = pending.get(e.data.id); if (!r) return; pending.delete(e.data.id); e.data.err ? r.rej(new Error(e.data.err)) : r.res(e.data); };
      worker.onerror = () => { workerFailed = true; worker = null; for (const [, r] of pending) r.rej(new Error('worker')); pending.clear(); };
    } catch { workerFailed = true; worker = null; }
    return worker;
  }
  function renderProc(s, m = null) {
    const w = getWorker(); const local = () => { ENGINE_MAIN ??= SoundwellEngine(); return ENGINE_MAIN.renderEntry(s.g, s.p, m); };
    if (!w) return new Promise((res, rej) => setTimeout(() => { try { res(local()); } catch (e) { rej(e); } }, 0));
    return new Promise((res, rej) => { const id = ++wid; pending.set(id, { res, rej: (e) => { if (workerFailed) { try { res(local()); } catch (x) { rej(x); } } else rej(e); } }); w.postMessage({ id, g: s.g, p: s.p, m }); });
  }
  // ---------- recipe knobs ("make it yours"): re-render a generated sound with changed ingredients ----------
  const KNOBS = [
    { k: 'pitch', label: 'Pitch', min: -12, max: 12, step: 1, fmt: (v) => (v > 0 ? '+' : '') + v + ' st', help: 'Retunes the oscillators inside the recipe. Unlike speed, the length stays the same, so loops keep their tempo.' },
    { k: 'length', label: 'Length', min: 50, max: 300, step: 5, def: 100, fmt: (v) => (v / 100).toFixed(2) + '×', help: 'How long hits and notes ring.' },
    { k: 'tone', label: 'Tone', min: -100, max: 100, step: 5, fmt: (v) => v === 0 ? 'neutral' : v < 0 ? 'darker ' + -v : 'brighter ' + v },
    { k: 'room', label: 'Room', min: 0, max: 100, step: 5, fmt: (v) => v ? v + '%' : 'dry' },
    { k: 'drive', label: 'Drive', min: 0, max: 100, step: 5, fmt: (v) => v ? v + '%' : 'clean' },
  ];
  const modOf = (m) => { if (!m) return null; const o = {}; for (const { k } of KNOBS) { const v = Number(m[k]); if (k === 'length' ? v && v !== 100 : v) o[k] = k === 'length' ? v / 100 : v; } if (m.seed) o.seed = m.seed | 0; return Object.keys(o).length ? o : null; };
  const modBufs = new Map();
  function modBuf(s, m) {
    const mod = modOf(m); if (!mod || !s.g) return getBuf(s); const key = s.slug + JSON.stringify(mod);
    if (!modBufs.has(key)) { const pr = renderProc(s, mod).then((o) => { const b = ac().createBuffer(2, o.l.length, 44100); b.copyToChannel(o.l, 0); b.copyToChannel(o.r || o.l, 1); return b; }); pr.catch(() => modBufs.delete(key)); modBufs.set(key, pr); while (modBufs.size > 24) modBufs.delete(modBufs.keys().next().value); }
    return modBufs.get(key);
  }
  const modDesc = (m) => { const d = modOf(m); if (!d) return ''; return [d.pitch ? `${d.pitch > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.pitch} semitones` : '', d.length ? `${d.length.toFixed(2)}× length` : '', d.tone ? (d.tone < 0 ? 'darker' : 'brighter') : '', d.room ? `${d.room}% room` : '', d.drive ? `${d.drive}% drive` : '', d.seed ? `take ${d.seed + 1}` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', '); };

  // ---------- downloads (zip with the MP3 + a license note) ----------
  let dlCap = null;
  const use = window.claude && typeof window.claude.use === 'function' ? window.claude.use('downloads') : Promise.resolve(null);
  // Outside claude.ai (a normal website), save with a regular browser download instead.
  const browserSaver = { save: async ({ filename, data }) => { const url = URL.createObjectURL(data instanceof Blob ? data : new Blob([data])); const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = filename; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 30000); return { status: 'saved' }; } };
  const onClaude = !!(window.claude && typeof window.claude.use === 'function');
  Promise.resolve(use).then((d) => { dlCap = d || (onClaude ? null : browserSaver); document.body.classList.toggle('can-dl', !!dlCap); }).catch(() => {});
  const CRC = (() => { const t = new Uint32Array(256); for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) { let c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; t[n] = c >>> 0; } return t; })();
  const crc32 = (u8) => { let c = 0xffffffff; for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i++) c = CRC[(c ^ u8[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8); return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0; };
  function zip(files) {
    const enc = new TextEncoder(); const parts = []; const central = []; let off = 0;
    for (const f of files) {
      const name = enc.encode(f.name); const crc = crc32(f.data); const size = f.data.length;
      const lh = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(30));
      lh.setUint32(0, 0x04034b50, true); lh.setUint16(4, 20, true); lh.setUint16(8, 0, true);
      lh.setUint32(14, crc, true); lh.setUint32(18, size, true); lh.setUint32(22, size, true); lh.setUint16(26, name.length, true);
      parts.push(new Uint8Array(lh.buffer), name, f.data);
      const ch = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(46));
      ch.setUint32(0, 0x02014b50, true); ch.setUint16(4, 20, true); ch.setUint16(6, 20, true);
      ch.setUint32(16, crc, true); ch.setUint32(20, size, true); ch.setUint32(24, size, true); ch.setUint16(28, name.length, true); ch.setUint32(42, off, true);
      central.push(new Uint8Array(ch.buffer), name);
      off += 30 + name.length + size;
    }
    const cdSize = central.reduce((a, b) => a + b.length, 0);
    const end = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(22));
    end.setUint32(0, 0x06054b50, true); end.setUint16(8, files.length, true); end.setUint16(10, files.length, true); end.setUint32(12, cdSize, true); end.setUint32(16, off, true);
    return new Blob([...parts, ...central, new Uint8Array(end.buffer)]);
  }
  const fname = (s) => (s.custom ? s.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') : '') || s.slug;
  async function download(s) {
    if (!dlCap) { toast('Downloads aren’t available in this view.'); return; }
    try {
      let file;
      if (s.g || s.custom || s.sp) { toast(`Preparing “${s.title}”…`); file = { name: `${fname(s)}.wav`, data: toWav(await getBuf(s)) }; }
      else { const res = await fetch(`media/${s.slug}.mp3`); if (!res.ok) throw new Error('fetch'); file = { name: `${s.slug}.mp3`, data: new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer()) }; }
      const note = `${s.title}\n\n${s.desc}\n\nLicense: CC0 1.0 Universal (public domain dedication)\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\n\nYou can use this sound for anything, including commercial work. No credit required.\nOptional credit: ${credit(s)}\n`;
      await dlCap.save({ filename: `${fname(s)}.zip`, data: zip([file, { name: 'LICENSE.txt', data: new TextEncoder().encode(note) }]) });
      toast(`Saved ${fname(s)}.zip`);
    } catch (e) {
      const code = e && e.code;
      if (code === 'declined') return;
      if (code === 'rate_limited') toast('A save is already waiting for your answer.');
      else if (code === 'extension_not_enabled' || code === 'rejected_extension') toast('Zip downloads are turned off in this view.');
      else if (e && e.message === 'fetch') toast(`Couldn't fetch “${s.title}”. Check your connection and try again.`);
      else toast('Download didn’t work in this view.');
    }
  }

  // ---------- small helpers ----------
  let toastT = 0;
  function toast(msg) { const t = $('#toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.hidden = false; clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(() => { t.hidden = true; }, 2600); }
  function copy(s, btn) {
    const text = credit(s);
    const done = () => toast('Credit copied');
    const fallback = () => { const r = document.createRange(); const el = document.getElementById('c-' + s.slug); if (el) { r.selectNodeContents(el); const sel = getSelection(); sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(r); toast('Credit selected. Press Copy on your keyboard.'); } };
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, fallback); } catch { fallback(); }
  }
  function open(slug, { scroll = false } = {}) {
    { const i = results.indexOf(bySlug[slug]); if (i >= 0) ensureShown(i); }
    state.open = state.open === slug ? null : slug;
    try { history.replaceState(null, '', state.open ? '#' + state.open : location.pathname + location.search); } catch {}
    const r = document.getElementById('r-' + slug);
    if (r) { const tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = rowHtml(bySlug[slug]).trim(); const nr = tmp.firstChild; r.replaceWith(nr); draw(nr.querySelector('canvas'), bySlug[slug], cur === bySlug[slug] ? progressOf() : 0); if (scroll) nr.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); nr.querySelector('.ttl').focus({ preventScroll: true }); if (state.edit === slug) drawEdit(bySlug[slug]); }
    // close any other open row
    for (const other of list.querySelectorAll('.detail')) { const row = other.closest('.row'); if (row.dataset.slug !== state.open) { other.remove(); row.querySelector('.ttl').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); } }
  }
  function goto(slug) {
    if (!results.includes(bySlug[slug])) { state.q = ''; state.cats.clear(); state.kind = 'all'; state.loops = false; state.saved = false; state.key = ''; state.bpm = 0; state.pack = ''; state.view = 'list'; state.browse = true; $('#q').value = ''; syncSeg(); state.open = null; render(); }
    state.open = null; open(slug, { scroll: true });
  }
  function syncSeg() { for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('.seg button')) b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(b.dataset.kind === state.kind)); }

  // ---------- audio engine (Web Audio) ----------
  let actx = null, master = null;
  function ac() {
    if (!actx) {
      const C = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext; actx = new C(); try { if (navigator.audioSession) navigator.audioSession.type = 'playback'; } catch {}
      const comp = actx.createDynamicsCompressor(); comp.threshold.value = -14; comp.knee.value = 6; comp.ratio.value = 8;
      master = actx.createGain(); master.gain.value = mixVol; master.connect(comp); comp.connect(actx.destination);
    }
    if (actx.state !== 'running' && actx.state !== 'closed') actx.resume().catch(() => {});
    return actx;
  }
  const bufs = new Map();
  // Recordings live in sound packs (one MP3 holding many sounds, separated by silence). Decode a pack once, then cut
  // each sound out with a 30 ms margin, which covers any MP3 decoder delay difference between browsers.
  const spriteCache = new Map();
  function spriteFile(f) {
    if (spriteCache.has(f)) { const p = spriteCache.get(f); spriteCache.delete(f); spriteCache.set(f, p); return p; }
    const p = fetch(`media/${f}.mp3`).then((r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('fetch'); return r.arrayBuffer(); }).then((ab) => new Promise((res, rej) => ac().decodeAudioData(ab, res, rej)));
    p.catch(() => spriteCache.delete(f)); spriteCache.set(f, p); while (spriteCache.size > 6) spriteCache.delete(spriteCache.keys().next().value); return p;
  }
  async function spriteSlice(s) {
    const big = await spriteFile(s.sp.f); const sr = big.sampleRate; const a = Math.max(0, Math.floor((s.sp.o - 0.03) * sr)); const b = Math.min(big.length, Math.ceil((s.sp.o + s.sp.d + 0.03) * sr));
    const out = ac().createBuffer(big.numberOfChannels, Math.max(1, b - a), sr); for (let c = 0; c < big.numberOfChannels; c++) out.copyToChannel(big.getChannelData(c).subarray(a, b), c); return out;
  }
  function getBuf(s) {
    if (bufs.has(s.slug)) { const p = bufs.get(s.slug); bufs.delete(s.slug); bufs.set(s.slug, p); return p; }
    const p = s.custom
      ? (bySlug[s.custom.base] ? processBuf(bySlug[s.custom.base], s.custom.o, s.custom.m) : Promise.reject(new Error('missing')))
      : s.g
      ? renderProc(s).then((o) => { const b = ac().createBuffer(2, o.l.length, 44100); b.copyToChannel(o.l, 0); b.copyToChannel(o.r || o.l, 1); return b; })
      : s.sp ? spriteSlice(s)
      : fetch(`media/${s.slug}.mp3`).then((r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('fetch'); return r.arrayBuffer(); }).then((ab) => new Promise((res, rej) => ac().decodeAudioData(ab, res, rej)));
    p.catch(() => bufs.delete(s.slug)); bufs.set(s.slug, p);
    // keep memory bounded: drop the oldest buffers that aren't in the mix or playing
    if (bufs.size > 40) for (const k of bufs.keys()) { if (bufs.size <= 40) break; if (k !== cur?.slug && !mix.some((l) => l.slug === k)) bufs.delete(k); }
    return p;
  }
  /** Loop points that skip MP3 encoder padding when a browser doesn't trim it. */
  function loopPoints(s, buf) {
    const n = s.n ? Math.round(s.n * buf.sampleRate / 44100) : Math.round(s.dur * buf.sampleRate);
    const extra = buf.length - n; const st = extra > 500 ? Math.min(Math.round(1105 * buf.sampleRate / 44100), extra) / buf.sampleRate : 0;
    return [st, st + Math.min(n, buf.length) / buf.sampleRate];
  }
  /** 16-bit WAV (stereo when the buffer has two channels) with TPDF dither. */
  function toWav(buf) {
    const n = buf.length; const ch = Math.min(2, buf.numberOfChannels); const sr = buf.sampleRate; const bytes = n * ch * 2; const out = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(44 + bytes));
    const w = (o, str) => { for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) out.setUint8(o + i, str.charCodeAt(i)); };
    w(0, 'RIFF'); out.setUint32(4, 36 + bytes, true); w(8, 'WAVE'); w(12, 'fmt '); out.setUint32(16, 16, true); out.setUint16(20, 1, true); out.setUint16(22, ch, true);
    out.setUint32(24, sr, true); out.setUint32(28, sr * ch * 2, true); out.setUint16(32, ch * 2, true); out.setUint16(34, 16, true); w(36, 'data'); out.setUint32(40, bytes, true);
    const data = []; for (let c = 0; c < ch; c++) data.push(buf.getChannelData(c)); let seed = 22222;
    const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return seed / 4294967296; };
    for (let i = 0, o = 44; i < n; i++) for (let c = 0; c < ch; c++, o += 2) { let v = data[c][i] * 32767 + (rnd() - rnd()); v = Math.max(-32768, Math.min(32767, Math.round(v))); out.setInt16(o, v, true); }
    return new Uint8Array(out.buffer);
  }
  const enc = (str) => new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  async function saveZip(filename, files) {
    if (!dlCap) { toast('Downloads aren’t available in this view.'); return false; }
    try { await dlCap.save({ filename, data: zip(files) }); toast(`Saved ${filename}`); return true; }
    catch (e) {
      const code = e && e.code; if (code === 'declined') return false;
      if (code === 'rate_limited') toast('A save is already waiting for your answer.');
      else if (code === 'extension_not_enabled' || code === 'rejected_extension') toast('Zip downloads are turned off in this view.');
      else toast('Download didn’t work in this view.');
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ---------- mixer ----------
  let mix = store.get('mix', []).map((l) => ({ ...l, slug: canon(l.slug) })).filter((l) => bySlug[l.slug]);
  let mixVol = store.get('mixvol', 0.85); $('#mixvol').value = Math.round(mixVol * 100);
  let mixOn = false, mixT0 = 0; const live = new Map();
  const saveMix = () => store.set('mix', mix);
  const inMix = (s) => mix.some((l) => l.slug === s.slug);
  const kitOf = (id) => KITS.find((k) => k.id === id);
  async function startLayer(l) {
    const s = bySlug[l.slug]; if (!s.loop) return;
    let buf; try { buf = await getBuf(s); } catch { toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); return; }
    if (!mixOn || !mix.includes(l)) return;
    stopLayer(l.slug);
    const c = ac(); const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = l.mute ? 0 : l.vol; g.connect(master);
    const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.loop = true; const [a, b] = loopPoints(s, buf); src.loopStart = a; src.loopEnd = b; src.connect(g);
    const startAt = Math.max(c.currentTime + 0.03, mixT0); const len = b - a; const off = (((startAt - mixT0) % len) + len) % len;
    src.start(startAt, a + off); live.set(l.slug, { src, g });
  }
  function stopLayer(slug) { const x = live.get(slug); if (x) { try { x.src.stop(); } catch {} x.g.disconnect(); live.delete(slug); } }
  async function mixStart() {
    const c = ac(); if (!mix.some((l) => bySlug[l.slug].loop)) { renderMixer(); return; }
    $('#mixplay').disabled = true;
    await Promise.all(mix.map((l) => getBuf(bySlug[l.slug]).catch(() => null)));
    $('#mixplay').disabled = false;
    mixOn = true; mixT0 = c.currentTime + 0.08; for (const l of mix) startLayer(l); renderMixer();
    $('#live').textContent = 'Mixer playing';
  }
  function mixStop() { mixOn = false; for (const k of [...live.keys()]) stopLayer(k); renderMixer(); }
  async function trigger(l) {
    const s = bySlug[l.slug]; let buf; try { buf = await getBuf(s); } catch { return; }
    const c = ac(); const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = l.mute ? 0 : l.vol; g.connect(master);
    const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.connect(g); src.start(); src.onended = () => g.disconnect();
  }
  function addToMix(s) {
    if (inMix(s)) { removeFromMix(s.slug); toast(`Removed “${s.title}” from the mixer`); return; }
    if (mix.length >= 12) { toast('The mixer holds up to 12 layers.'); return; }
    const l = { slug: s.slug, vol: s.loop ? 0.7 : 0.9, mute: false }; mix.push(l); saveMix();
    if (mixOn) startLayer(l);
    renderMixer(); syncMixButtons();
    toast(s.loop ? `Added “${s.title}” to the mixer` : `Added “${s.title}” as a pad`);
  }
  function removeFromMix(slug) { mix = mix.filter((l) => l.slug !== slug); stopLayer(slug); saveMix(); renderMixer(); syncMixButtons(); }
  const KEYLESS = new Set(['Drums', 'Percussion', 'Texture', 'Taiko']);
  const kitKeys = (id) => { const k = KITS.find((x) => x.id === id); const ks = [...new Set(SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kit === id && !KEYLESS.has(s.stem)).map((s) => s.key))]; const order = ['C', 'C#', 'D', 'Eb', 'E', 'F', 'F#', 'G', 'Ab', 'A', 'Bb', 'B']; return ks.sort((a, b) => order.indexOf(a.split(' ')[0]) - order.indexOf(b.split(' ')[0])); };
  function loadKit(id, key) {
    const K = KITS.find((x) => x.id === id); key = key || document.querySelector(`[data-kitkey="${id}"]`)?.value || K.key;
    const stems = SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kit === id && (KEYLESS.has(s.stem) ? !s.g : s.key === key));
    ac(); mixStop(); mix = stems.map((s) => ({ slug: s.slug, vol: 0.55, mute: false }));
    saveMix(); syncMixButtons(); openMixer(); mixStart(); toast(`Loaded ${kitOf(id).name} in ${key}. Mute stems to make it yours.`);
  }
  function openMixer() { $('#mixer').hidden = false; $('#mixbtn').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true'); renderMixer(); }
  function closeMixer() { $('#mixer').hidden = true; $('#mixbtn').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
  function renderMixer() {
    $('#mixcount').textContent = mix.length;
    const pp = $('#mixplay'); pp.innerHTML = `<svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${mixOn ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg>${mixOn ? 'Stop mix' : 'Play mix'}`;
    if ($('#mixer').hidden) return;
    let pad = 0;
    $('#layers').innerHTML = mix.length ? mix.map((l) => {
      const s = bySlug[l.slug]; const kit = s.kit ? kitOf(s.kit) : null; const isPad = !s.loop; if (isPad) pad++;
      const kind = kit ? `Stem · ${kit.bpm} BPM · ${kit.key}` : isPad ? `Pad · ${fmtDur(s.dur)}` : `Loop · ${fmtDur(s.dur)}`;
      return `<li class="layer${l.mute ? ' muted' : ''}" data-slug="${s.slug}"><div class="ln">${esc(s.title)}</div><div class="lk">${esc(kind)}</div>
        <div class="lrow">${isPad ? `<button class="pad" type="button" data-m="trig">Play${pad <= 9 ? `<kbd>${pad}</kbd>` : ''}</button>` : ''}
          <input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(l.vol * 100)}" data-m="vol" aria-label="Volume: ${esc(s.title)}">
          <button class="ib" type="button" data-m="mute" aria-pressed="${l.mute}" aria-label="${l.mute ? 'Unmute' : 'Mute'} ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${l.mute ? 'mute' : 'vol'}"/></svg></button>
          <button class="ib" type="button" data-m="rm" aria-label="Remove ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-x"/></svg></button></div></li>`;
    }).join('') : `<li class="mempty">Your mix is empty. Tap the <svg width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align:-2px"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg> button on any sound, open a stem kit, or describe a scene above.</li>`;
  }
  function syncMixButtons() {
    for (const b of list.querySelectorAll('[data-act="mix"]')) { const s = bySlug[b.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; const on = inMix(s); b.classList.toggle('inmix', on); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on)); b.setAttribute('aria-label', `${on ? 'Remove from mixer' : 'Add to mixer'}: ${s.title}`); }
  }
  async function exportMix() {
    const loops = mix.filter((l) => !l.mute && bySlug[l.slug].loop);
    if (!loops.length) { toast('Add at least one unmuted loop to download a mix.'); return; }
    const btn = $('#mixexport'); btn.disabled = true; const old = btn.innerHTML; btn.textContent = 'Rendering…';
    try {
      const secs = +$('#mixlen').value; const sr = 44100; const oc = new OfflineAudioContext(2, sr * secs, sr);
      const cp = oc.createDynamicsCompressor(); cp.threshold.value = -14; cp.knee.value = 6; cp.ratio.value = 8; const mg = oc.createGain(); mg.gain.value = mixVol; mg.connect(cp); cp.connect(oc.destination);
      for (const l of loops) {
        const s = bySlug[l.slug]; const buf = await getBuf(s); const g = oc.createGain(); g.gain.value = l.vol; g.connect(mg);
        const src = oc.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.loop = true; const [a, b] = loopPoints(s, buf); src.loopStart = a; src.loopEnd = b; src.connect(g); src.start(0, a);
      }
      mg.gain.setValueAtTime(mixVol, secs - 1.5); mg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, secs);
      const out = await oc.startRendering();
      const chs = [out.getChannelData(0), out.getChannelData(1)]; let pk = 0; for (const d of chs) for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) pk = Math.max(pk, Math.abs(d[i])); if (pk > 0.95) { const g = 0.95 / pk; for (const d of chs) for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] *= g; }
      const names = loops.map((l) => `- ${bySlug[l.slug].title} (volume ${Math.round(l.vol * 100)}%)`).join('\n');
      const note = `SoundWell mix, ${secs} seconds\n\nLayers:\n${names}\n\nEvery layer is CC0 1.0 (public domain). Use this mix for anything, commercial work included. No credit required.\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\n`;
      await saveZip('soundwell-mix.zip', [{ name: 'soundwell-mix.wav', data: toWav(out) }, { name: 'LICENSE.txt', data: enc(note) }]);
    } catch { toast('Couldn’t render the mix. Try a shorter length.'); }
    btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = old;
  }

  // ---------- scene builder ----------
  let sampleCap = null;
  Promise.resolve(window.claude && typeof window.claude.use === 'function' ? window.claude.use('sample') : null).then((x) => { sampleCap = x; }).catch(() => {});
  const SCENE = { night: ['crickets', 'owl'], city: ['traffic'], street: ['traffic'], urban: ['traffic'], cafe: ['cafe'], coffee: ['cafe'], restaurant: ['cafe'], space: ['spaceship', 'alien'],
    spaceship: ['spaceship'], forest: ['birds', 'wind', 'trees'], woods: ['birds', 'wind'], park: ['birds', 'wind'], rain: ['rain'], rainy: ['rain'], storm: ['rain', 'thunder'], thunderstorm: ['rain', 'thunder'],
    ocean: ['ocean', 'waves'], beach: ['ocean', 'waves'], sea: ['ocean'], cozy: ['fire', 'campfire'], cabin: ['fire', 'wind'], fireplace: ['fire'], camp: ['campfire', 'crickets'], chase: ['tension', 'taiko', 'ostinato'],
    tense: ['tension', 'ostinato'], suspense: ['tension', 'clock'], underwater: ['underwater'], office: ['room tone', 'keyboard'], study: ['lofi'], studying: ['lofi'], relax: ['lofi', 'calm'], horror: ['whispers', 'drone'],
    haunted: ['whispers', 'drone'], creepy: ['whispers', 'drone'], magic: ['potion'], witch: ['potion'], battle: ['epic', 'taiko'], epic: ['epic'], game: ['crowd', 'stadium'], stadium: ['stadium', 'crowd'],
    football: ['stadium', 'crowd', 'drumline'], party: ['house', 'dance'], winter: ['wind'], snow: ['wind'], river: ['brook', 'stream'], stream: ['brook'], clocks: ['clock'], alley: ['traffic', 'rain'] };
  const scenePool = (s) => s.loop && (!s.g || s.layer || s.g === 'heart' || s.g === 'clock' || (s.g === 'drumloop' && s.bpm === 90));
  function keywordScene(text) {
    const toks = words(text); const extra = toks.flatMap((t) => (SCENE[t] || []).flatMap(words)); const all = [...new Set([...toks, ...extra])];
    const scored = SOUNDS.filter(scenePool).map((s) => { let sc = 0; for (const t of all) { const v = score(s, [t]); sc += v; } if (s.layer) sc *= 1.3; return [s, sc]; }).filter((x) => x[1] > 0).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    const picks = []; const usedKits = new Set(); let music = 0;
    const KITW = { neon: ['neon', 'synth', 'synthpop', 'groove', 'dance', 'retro'], lofi: ['lofi', 'study', 'studying', 'chill', 'relax', 'homework'], epic: ['epic', 'trailer', 'battle', 'cinematic', 'war', 'hero'] };
    for (const [s] of scored) {
      if (picks.length >= 5) break;
      if (s.kit && !KITW[s.kit].some((w) => all.includes(w))) continue;
      if (s.kind === 'music') { if (music >= 1 && !s.kit) continue; if (s.kit) { if (usedKits.has(s.kit) && music >= 2) continue; usedKits.add(s.kit); } music++; }
      picks.push({ slug: s.slug, volume: s.kind === 'music' ? 0.45 : picks.length ? 0.6 : 0.8 });
    }
    return picks;
  }
  async function askClaude(text) {
    const cands = SOUNDS.filter(scenePool).map((s) => `${s.slug} | ${s.title} | ${s.tags.slice(0, 6).join(', ')}`).join('\n');
    const prompt = `You are a sound designer building a layered, looping soundscape for this scene: "${text.slice(0, 300)}".\nChoose 3 to 6 sounds ONLY from this list (format: slug | title | tags). Use ambience layers for places and weather. Add music loops or stems only if the scene implies music or a strong mood. Avoid near-duplicates.\n${cands}\n\nReply with JSON only, in this shape: {"layers":[{"slug":"<slug from the list>","volume":0.6}]}. Volume is 0.2 to 1.0; keep background beds and music lower than the main sound.`;
    const res = await sampleCap.json(prompt, { modelTier: 'quick' });
    const seen = new Set();
    return ((res && res.layers) || []).filter((l) => l && bySlug[l.slug] && scenePool(bySlug[l.slug]) && !seen.has(l.slug) && seen.add(l.slug));
  }
  async function buildScene(text) {
    text = String(text || '').trim();
    if (!text) { toast('Describe a scene first, like “cozy cabin during a thunderstorm”.'); $('#scene').focus(); return; }
    ac(); const hint = $('#scenehint'); const go = $('#scenego'); go.disabled = true; hint.textContent = 'Building your mix…';
    let picks = null; let how = '';
    if (sampleCap) { try { picks = await askClaude(text); how = 'Picked by Helion Sound Labs'; } catch { picks = null; } }
    if (!picks || !picks.length) { picks = keywordScene(text); how = 'Matched by keywords'; }
    go.disabled = false;
    if (!picks.length) { hint.textContent = 'No matching loops. Try naming a place, the weather or a mood.'; return; }
    mixStop(); mix = picks.slice(0, 6).map((p) => ({ slug: p.slug, vol: Math.min(1, Math.max(0.1, Number(p.volume) || 0.6)), mute: false }));
    saveMix(); syncMixButtons(); renderMixer(); mixStart();
    hint.textContent = `${how}: ${mix.length} layers. Adjust the volumes to taste.`;
  }

  // ---------- editor ----------
  const edits = new Map();
  const defaults = () => ({ speed: 1, gain: 0, start: 0, end: 1, fin: 0, fout: 0, rev: false });
  const eo = (s) => { if (!edits.has(s.slug)) edits.set(s.slug, defaults()); return edits.get(s.slug); };
  const knobs = new Map(); const km = (s) => { if (!knobs.has(s.slug)) knobs.set(s.slug, { pitch: 0, length: 100, tone: 0, room: 0, drive: 0, seed: 0 }); return knobs.get(s.slug); };
  function knobsHtml(s) {
    if (!s.g) return '';
    const m = km(s);
    return `<div class="knobs"><h4>Make it yours <span class="tagline">recipe knobs</span></h4>
      <p class="hint">This sound is generated from a recipe, so these change the ingredients themselves. You get a brand-new sound, not an edited copy.</p>
      <div class="egrid">${KNOBS.map((d) => `<label title="${esc(d.help || '')}"><span>${d.label}<output data-ko="${d.k}">${d.fmt(m[d.k])}</output></span><input type="range" data-k="${d.k}" min="${d.min}" max="${d.max}" step="${d.step}" value="${m[d.k]}"></label>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="btnrow"><button class="btn" type="button" data-act="kdice" title="Same recipe, new random take">🎲 New take</button><span class="take" data-ko="seed">${m.seed ? 'Take ' + (m.seed + 1) : 'Original take'}</span>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-act="ksave"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-spark"/></svg>Save to Your creations</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-act="kreset">Reset knobs</button></div></div>`;
  }
  const fadeMax = (s) => Math.min(3, s.dur / 2);
  function editorHtml(s) {
    const o = eo(s); const fm = fadeMax(s);
    const r = (key, label, min, max, val, out) => `<label><span>${label}<output data-o="${key}">${out}</output></span><input type="range" data-e="${key}" min="${min}" max="${max}" value="${val}"></label>`;
    return `<div class="editor" id="e-${s.slug}"><h4>Edit and download</h4>
      <canvas class="epreview" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
      <div class="egrid">
        ${r('speed', 'Speed and pitch', 50, 200, Math.round(o.speed * 100), `${o.speed.toFixed(2)}×`)}
        ${r('gain', 'Volume', -18, 6, o.gain, `${o.gain > 0 ? '+' : ''}${o.gain} dB`)}
        ${r('start', 'Trim start', 0, 1000, Math.round(o.start * 1000), `${(o.start * s.dur).toFixed(2)} s`)}
        ${r('end', 'Trim end', 0, 1000, Math.round(o.end * 1000), `${(o.end * s.dur).toFixed(2)} s`)}
        ${r('fin', 'Fade in', 0, 100, Math.round(o.fin / fm * 100) || 0, `${o.fin.toFixed(1)} s`)}
        ${r('fout', 'Fade out', 0, 100, Math.round(o.fout / fm * 100) || 0, `${o.fout.toFixed(1)} s`)}
        <label class="echeck"><input type="checkbox" data-e="rev" ${o.rev ? 'checked' : ''}> Reverse</label>
      </div>
      <div class="btnrow"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-act="eplay"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-play"/></svg>Preview</button>
        <button class="btn dl" type="button" data-act="edl"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg>Download WAV</button>
        <button class="btn dl" type="button" data-act="evar"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-spark"/></svg>5 variations</button>
        <button class="btn" type="button" data-act="ereset">Reset</button></div>
      <p class="hint" style="margin:0;color:var(--muted);font-size:.8rem">${s.g ? 'Variations are new takes of the recipe (different randomness, tiny pitch and length changes), so a sound you repeat in a game or edit never sounds robotic.' : 'Variations nudge pitch and volume slightly, so a sound you repeat in a game or edit doesn\'t sound robotic.'}</p>${knobsHtml(s)}</div>`;
  }
  function drawEdit(s) {
    const c = document.querySelector(`#e-${s.slug} canvas`); if (!c) return; const o = eo(s);
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; const w = c.clientWidth; const h = c.clientHeight; if (!w) return;
    c.width = Math.round(w * dpr); c.height = Math.round(h * dpr); const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    const pk = o.rev ? [...s.peaks].reverse() : s.peaks; const a = o.rev ? 1 - o.end : o.start; const b = o.rev ? 1 - o.start : o.end; const n = pk.length; const bw = w / n;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const x = (i + 0.5) / n; let amp = pk[i] / 100; const inside = x >= a && x <= b;
      if (inside) { const lt = (x - a) / (b - a) * (s.dur * (b - a)); const total = s.dur * (b - a); if (o.fin && lt < o.fin) amp *= lt / o.fin; if (o.fout && lt > total - o.fout) amp *= Math.max(0, (total - lt) / o.fout); }
      amp *= 10 ** (o.gain / 20);
      const bh = Math.max(2, Math.min(1, amp) * (h - 4)); ctx.fillStyle = inside ? colors.accent : colors.wave; ctx.globalAlpha = inside ? 1 : 0.5;
      ctx.fillRect(i * bw + bw * 0.18, (h - bh) / 2, Math.max(1, bw * 0.64), bh);
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
  async function processBuf(s, o, m = null) {
    const src = await modBuf(s, m); const sr = src.sampleRate; const nch = src.numberOfChannels; const len = src.length;
    const a = Math.floor(Math.min(o.start, o.end) * len); const b = Math.max(a + 256, Math.floor(Math.max(o.start, o.end) * len)); const segLen = Math.min(b, len) - a;
    const inB = ac().createBuffer(nch, segLen, sr);
    for (let c = 0; c < nch; c++) { const seg = src.getChannelData(c).slice(a, a + segLen); if (o.rev) seg.reverse(); inB.copyToChannel(seg, c); }
    const outLen = Math.max(256, Math.ceil(segLen / o.speed)); const oc = new OfflineAudioContext(nch, outLen, sr);
    const node = oc.createBufferSource(); node.buffer = inB; node.playbackRate.value = o.speed; const g = oc.createGain(); const lin = 10 ** (o.gain / 20); const dur = outLen / sr;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(o.fin > 0 ? 0 : lin, 0); if (o.fin > 0) g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(lin, Math.min(o.fin, dur * 0.5));
    if (o.fout > 0) { g.gain.setValueAtTime(lin, Math.max(Math.min(o.fin, dur * 0.5), dur - o.fout)); g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, dur); }
    node.connect(g); g.connect(oc.destination); node.start(); return oc.startRendering();
  }
  let previewSrc = null;
  async function previewEdit(s) {
    try { const out = await processBuf(s, eo(s), s.g ? km(s) : null); const c = ac(); if (previewSrc) try { previewSrc.stop(); } catch {} if (P.playing) pPause();
      previewSrc = c.createBufferSource(); previewSrc.buffer = out; previewSrc.connect(c.destination); previewSrc.start(); }
    catch { toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); }
  }
  async function downloadEdit(s, variations = false) {
    try {
      const o = eo(s); const files = [];
      const m = s.g ? km(s) : null;
      if (!variations) files.push({ name: `${s.slug}-edit.wav`, data: toWav(await processBuf(s, o, m)) });
      else if (s.g) { const base = (m.seed || 0) + 1; for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) files.push({ name: `${s.slug}-take${i}.wav`, data: toWav(await processBuf(s, o, { ...m, seed: base * 10 + i })) }); }
      else { const r = rng(Date.now() % 100000); for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) files.push({ name: `${s.slug}-v${i}.wav`, data: toWav(await processBuf(s, { ...o, speed: o.speed * (1 + (r() - 0.5) * 0.12), gain: o.gain + (r() - 0.5) * 4 })) }); }
      files.push({ name: 'LICENSE.txt', data: enc(`${s.title} (edited)\n\nLicense: CC0 1.0 Universal (public domain)\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\nUse it for anything, commercial work included. No credit required.\n`) });
      await saveZip(`${s.slug}${variations ? '-variations' : '-edit'}.zip`, files);
    } catch { toast(`Couldn't process “${s.title}”.`); }
  }
  function rng(seed) { let a = seed >>> 0; return () => { a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0; let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a); t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t; return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }

  const NOTE_NAMES = ['C', 'C#', 'D', 'Eb', 'E', 'F', 'F#', 'G', 'Ab', 'A', 'Bb', 'B'];
  const PROG_NAMES = [...new Map(SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.g === 'chords').map((s) => [s.p.prog, s.title.split(' · ')[1]])).entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map((x) => x[1]);
  function songStarter() {
    const key = +$('#ss-key').value, prog = +$('#ss-prog').value, bpm = +$('#ss-bpm').value, inst = +$('#ss-inst').value;
    const find = (g, extra = {}) => SOUNDS.find((s) => s.g === g && s.p.key === key && s.p.prog === prog && s.p.bpm === bpm && Object.entries(extra).every(([k, v]) => s.p[k] === v));
    const minor = (SOUNDS.find((s) => s.g === 'chords' && s.p.prog === prog)?.key || '').includes('minor');
    const genre = minor ? (bpm === 90 ? 2 : 11) : (bpm === 90 ? 8 : 0);
    const drums = SOUNDS.find((s) => s.g === 'drumloop' && s.p.g === genre && s.p.bpm === bpm);
    const picks = [[find('chords', { inst }), 0.5], [find('bass', { st: bpm === 120 ? 1 : 0 }), 0.6], [find('arp'), 0.3], [find('melody'), 0.4], [drums, 0.6]].filter((x) => x[0]);
    ac(); mixStop(); mix = picks.map(([s, v]) => ({ slug: s.slug, vol: v, mute: false })); saveMix(); syncMixButtons(); openMixer(); mixStart();
    toast(`Song starter: ${picks[0][0].key}, ${bpm} BPM. Mute or swap parts to taste.`);
  }
  function musicFilters() {
    const box = $('#musicf'); const show = state.kind === 'music' || state.key || state.bpm || [...state.cats].some((c) => /Loops|Kits|Jingles/.test(c)); box.hidden = !show; if (!show || box.dataset.built) return; box.dataset.built = '1';
    const keys = [...new Set(SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kind === 'music' && s.key).map((s) => s.key))].sort((a, b) => (a.includes('minor') - b.includes('minor')) || NOTE_NAMES.indexOf(a.split(' ')[0]) - NOTE_NAMES.indexOf(b.split(' ')[0]));
    $('#keyf').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', keys.map((k) => `<option>${esc(k)}</option>`).join(''));
    const bpms = [...new Set(SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kind === 'music' && s.bpm).map((s) => s.bpm))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    $('#bpmf').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', bpms.map((b) => `<option value="${b}">${b} BPM</option>`).join(''));
  }
  // ---------- describe a sound (custom sounds, saved in this browser) ----------
  const MINE_KEY = 'mine';
  function buildIx(s) { s.ix = { title: words(s.title), tags: s.tags.flatMap(words), cat: words(s.cat), mood: words(s.mood || ''), desc: words(s.desc) }; s.kindLabel = s.kind === 'music' ? 'Music' : 'Sound effect'; }
  function customEntry(c) {
    const base = bySlug[c.base]; if (!base) return null; const o = c.o; const mod = modOf(c.m) || {};
    const plain = !o.rev && !o.fin && !o.fout && o.start <= 0 && o.end >= 1;
    const pk = o.rev ? [...base.peaks].reverse() : base.peaks;
    const s = { slug: c.slug, title: c.title, desc: c.desc, kind: base.kind, cat: 'Your Creations', tags: [...new Set([...words(c.text).filter((w) => w.length > 2).slice(0, 6), ...base.tags.slice(0, 4), 'custom'])],
      mood: base.mood || '', bpm: 0, key: '', loop: !!base.loop && plain, dur: Math.max(0.05, Math.round((base.dur * (base.loop ? 1 : Math.max(1, mod.length || 1)) * (o.end - o.start) / o.speed + (mod.room && !base.loop ? 0.6 + 0.024 * mod.room : 0)) * 100) / 100), peaks: pk, custom: c, kit: '', stem: '', pack: 'mine' };
    buildIx(s); return s;
  }
  function addCustom(c, { front = true } = {}) { const s = customEntry(c); if (!s) return null; if (front) SOUNDS.unshift(s); else SOUNDS.push(s); bySlug[s.slug] = s; return s; }
  function saveMine() { store.set(MINE_KEY, SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.custom).map((s) => s.custom)); }
  function describePool(text) {
    const toks = words(text).slice(0, 10); const pool = SOUNDS.filter((s) => !s.custom && (s.kind === 'sfx' || (!s.loop && s.kind === 'music')));
    const scored = toks.length ? pool.map((s) => [s, toks.reduce((a, t) => a + score(s, [t]), 0)]).filter((x) => x[1] > 0).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 60).map((x) => x[0]) : [];
    const reps = new Map(); for (const s of pool) { const k = s.g || s.cat; if (!reps.has(k)) reps.set(k, s); }
    const out = [...new Set([...scored, ...reps.values()])].slice(0, 130); return { out, matched: scored };
  }
  const clamp = (v, lo, hi, d) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n)) : d; };
  async function createSound(text, { retry = false } = {}) {
    text = String(text || '').trim();
    const out = $('#desc-out'); const go = $('#desc-go');
    if (!text) { toast('Describe the sound first, like “a heavy wooden door slamming in a church”.'); $('#desc-in')?.focus(); return; }
    ac(); go.disabled = true; out.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Designing your sound…</p>';
    const { out: pool, matched } = describePool(text); let pick = null; let how = '';
    if (sampleCap) {
      try {
        const listTxt = pool.map((s) => `${s.slug} | ${s.title} | ${s.tags.slice(0, 6).join(', ')}`).join('\n');
        const prompt = `You are a sound designer. Someone wants this sound: "${text.slice(0, 300)}".\nPick the ONE best starting sound from this list (format: slug | title | tags), then tune it with these settings to match the description as closely as possible:\n- speed: 0.5 to 2 (below 1 is slower and deeper, above 1 is faster and higher)\n- reverse: true or false\n- gain: volume change in dB, -12 to 6\n- fadeIn, fadeOut: seconds, 0 to 3\n- trimStart, trimEnd: the part of the sound to keep, as fractions from 0 to 1\n${retry ? 'Give a clearly different take from the obvious first choice.\n' : ''}\n${listTxt}\n\nReply with JSON only: {"slug":"<slug from the list>","name":"short title for the new sound, at most 6 words","speed":1,"reverse":false,"gain":0,"fadeIn":0,"fadeOut":0,"trimStart":0,"trimEnd":1,"why":"one short sentence"}`;
        const r = await sampleCap.json(prompt, { modelTier: 'quick', cache: !retry });
        if (r && bySlug[r.slug] && pool.includes(bySlug[r.slug])) { pick = r; how = 'Designed by Helion Sound Labs'; }
      } catch { pick = null; }
    }
    if (!pick) { const base = matched[0]; if (!base) { go.disabled = false; out.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">No close match yet. Try naming the object, the action and the place, like “glass bottle breaking in a stone hallway”.</p>'; return; }
      pick = { slug: base.slug, name: text.replace(/^(a|an|the)\s+/i, '').slice(0, 40), speed: 1, reverse: false, gain: 0, fadeIn: 0, fadeOut: 0, trimStart: 0, trimEnd: 1, why: '' }; how = 'Closest keyword match'; }
    const base = bySlug[pick.slug]; const t0 = clamp(pick.trimStart, 0, 0.95, 0); const t1 = clamp(pick.trimEnd, t0 + 0.05, 1, 1);
    const o = { speed: clamp(pick.speed, 0.5, 2, 1), gain: Math.round(clamp(pick.gain, -12, 6, 0)), start: t0, end: t1, fin: clamp(pick.fadeIn, 0, 3, 0), fout: clamp(pick.fadeOut, 0, 3, 0), rev: pick.reverse === true };
    const name = String(pick.name || text).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 60) || 'My sound';
    const tweaks = [o.speed !== 1 ? `${o.speed.toFixed(2)}× speed` : '', o.rev ? 'reversed' : '', o.gain ? `${o.gain > 0 ? '+' : ''}${o.gain} dB` : '', (o.start > 0 || o.end < 1) ? 'trimmed' : '', o.fin ? 'fade in' : '', o.fout ? 'fade out' : ''].filter(Boolean);
    const c = { slug: 'my-' + Date.now().toString(36), title: name, text, base: base.slug, o, desc: `Your custom sound for “${text.slice(0, 120)}”. Based on ${base.title}${tweaks.length ? ` (${tweaks.join(', ')})` : ''}.${pick.why ? ' ' + String(pick.why).slice(0, 160) : ''}` };
    const s = addCustom(c); saveMine(); go.disabled = false;
    out.innerHTML = `<div class="made"><p><b>${esc(s.title)}</b> <span class="hint">· ${esc(how)}</span></p><p class="hint">Based on ${esc(base.title)}${tweaks.length ? ` · ${esc(tweaks.join(', '))}` : ''}</p>
      <div class="btnrow"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-mine-play="${s.slug}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-play"/></svg>Play</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-mine-open="${s.slug}">Open</button><button class="btn" type="button" id="desc-retry">Try a different take</button></div></div>`;
    render(); load(s);
  }

  }

  // ---------- sequencer ----------
  // Modeled on the most-used step sequencers: pattern banks and song chains (Roland TR, Elektron, Maschine), per-step
  // velocity, chance, conditions, ratchets, micro-timing and pitch (Elektron parameter locks, Logic edit modes, FL graph editor),
  // per-track length for polymeter (Logic, Elektron), choke groups (808 hats), track pan/filter/reverb (Drum Rack, Maschine),
  // Euclidean and randomize tools, live pad recording (MPC, Maschine), and stem and MIDI export (Splice Beatmaker, FL Studio).
  const SEQ_MAX_TRACKS = 8; const SEQ_SAVED_KEY = 'seq-saved'; const SEQ_CUR_KEY = 'seq-current';
  const BANKS = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D']; const STEP_OPTS = [16, 32, 64];
  const CONDS = ['', '1:2', '2:2', '1:4', '2:4', '3:4', '4:4', 'fill', '!fill', 'first', '!first'];
  const condLabel = (c) => ({ '': 'Always', fill: 'Fill', '!fill': 'No fill', first: '1st', '!first': 'Not 1st' })[c] ?? c;
  const LANES = {
    steps: { name: 'Steps', help: 'Tap a step for a hit, again for an accent, again to clear. Drag across a row to add many hits at once; start a drag on a hit to erase.' },
    vel: { name: 'Velocity', min: 5, max: 100, step: 5, help: 'Drag up or down on a step to set how hard it hits.', fmt: (v) => v + '%' },
    chance: { name: 'Chance', min: 0, max: 100, step: 5, def: 100, help: 'Drag to set the odds a step plays each time round. Keeps loops from sounding stiff.', fmt: (v) => v + '%' },
    cond: { name: 'Condition', help: 'Tap to cycle: 1:2 plays on the first of every 2 loops, 2:4 on the second of every 4, Fill only while Fill is on, 1st only on the first loop.' },
    rep: { name: 'Repeat', min: 1, max: 4, step: 1, def: 1, help: 'Drag for rolls: up to 4 hits squeezed into one step, like trap hi-hats.', fmt: (v) => v + '×' },
    nudge: { name: 'Nudge', min: -50, max: 50, step: 5, def: 0, bi: true, help: 'Drag to push a step early (down) or late (up) for a looser, human groove.', fmt: (v) => (v > 0 ? '+' : '') + v + '%' },
    tune: { name: 'Pitch', min: -12, max: 12, step: 1, def: 0, bi: true, help: 'Drag to change the pitch of a single step, in semitones. Great for 808 bass lines.', fmt: (v) => (v > 0 ? '+' : '') + v },
  };
  const blankPat = (n) => '.'.repeat(n);
  const num = (v, lo, hi, d) => { v = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(v) ? Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, Math.round(v))) : d; };
  const DEFAULT_TRACKS = [['drumhit-kick-0-0', 'X...x...x...x..x'], ['drumhit-snare-0-0', '....X.......X...'], ['drumhit-hat-0-0', 'x.x.x.x.x.x.x.x.'], ['drumhit-hat-1-0', '..............x.'], ['drumhit-clap-1-0', '............x...'], ['note808-0-1', 'x.........x.....']];
  const newTrack = (slug, steps, o = {}) => ({ slug, vol: 0.8, pitch: 0, pan: 0, tone: 0, send: 0, choke: 0, len: steps, mute: false, solo: false, pats: {}, ...o });
  function defaultPattern() { return cleanPattern({ title: 'My first beat', bpm: 100, swing: 0, steps: 16, tracks: DEFAULT_TRACKS.map(([slug, s]) => ({ slug, pats: { A: { s } }, choke: slug.startsWith('drumhit-hat') ? 1 : 0 })) }); }

  // Genre starting points. Each track: [slug, {A: steps or {s, p}}, track options].
  const H = (s, p) => ({ s, p });
  const TEMPLATES = [
    { name: 'Boom bap', bpm: 90, swing: 28, chain: 'AAAB', tracks: [
      ['drumhit-kick-1-1', { A: 'X......x..X.....', B: 'X......x..X...x.' }],
      ['drumhit-snare-1-0', { A: '....X.......X...', B: H('....X.......X..x', { 15: { vel: 40 } }) }],

      ['drumhit-tom-1-1', { A: '...x......x......x......x..x..x.', B: H('...x......x......x....xxxxxxxxxx', { 22: { vel: 40 }, 23: { vel: 45 }, 24: { vel: 50 }, 25: { vel: 55 }, 26: { vel: 60 }, 27: { vel: 65 }, 28: { vel: 72 }, 29: { vel: 80 }, 30: { vel: 90 }, 31: { vel: 100 } }) }, { send: 40, pan: -20 }],
      ['sc-timpani-2-0', { A: 'X...............X...............', B: 'X...............X...............' }, { send: 30 }],
      ['drumhit-cymbal-0-1', { B: 'X...............................' }, { send: 35, vol: 0.6 }],

  ];
  function templatePattern(tp) {
    const steps = tp.steps || 16;
    return cleanPattern({ title: tp.name + ' beat', bpm: tp.bpm, swing: tp.swing, steps, chain: tp.chain, song: tp.chain.length > 1 && /B/.test(tp.chain), cur: 'A',
      tracks: tp.tracks.map(([slug, pats, o = {}]) => ({ slug, ...o, len: o.len || steps, pats: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(pats).map(([b, v]) => [b, typeof v === 'string' ? { s: v } : v])) })) });
  }

  /** Validate any pattern (from a file, the browser, a template or the community board), version 1 or 2: unknown sounds are dropped, numbers clamped. */
  function cleanPattern(p, { allowCustom = true } = {}) {
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return null; const steps = STEP_OPTS.includes(+p.steps) ? +p.steps : 16;
    const cleanSteps = (src) => {
      let s = String((src && typeof src === 'object' ? src.s : src) || '').replace(/[^.xX]/g, '.').slice(0, steps); s += blankPat(steps - s.length);
      const pp = {}; const raw = src && typeof src === 'object' && src.p && typeof src.p === 'object' ? src.p : {};
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(raw)) {
        const i = +k; if (!(i >= 0 && i < steps) || s[i] === '.' || !v || typeof v !== 'object') continue; const o = {};
        if (v.vel != null) o.vel = num(v.vel, 5, 100, 100); if (v.chance != null && +v.chance < 100) o.chance = num(v.chance, 0, 100, 100);
        if (v.cond && CONDS.includes(v.cond)) o.cond = v.cond; if (+v.rep > 1) o.rep = num(v.rep, 1, 4, 1);
        if (+v.nudge) o.nudge = num(v.nudge, -50, 50, 0); if (+v.tune) o.tune = num(v.tune, -12, 12, 0);
        if (Object.keys(o).length) pp[i] = o;
      }
      return { s, p: pp };
    };
    const tracks = (Array.isArray(p.tracks) ? p.tracks : []).slice(0, SEQ_MAX_TRACKS).map((t) => (t && typeof t.slug === 'string' ? { ...t, slug: canon(t.slug) } : t)).filter((t) => t && typeof t.slug === 'string' && bySlug[t.slug] && (allowCustom || !bySlug[t.slug].custom)).map((t) => {
      const pats = {}; if (t.pats && typeof t.pats === 'object') { for (const b of BANKS) if (t.pats[b]) pats[b] = cleanSteps(t.pats[b]); } else pats.A = cleanSteps({ s: t.pat });
      return { slug: t.slug, vol: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(Number(t.vol)) && t.vol !== '' && t.vol != null ? Number(t.vol) : 0.8)), pitch: num(t.pitch, -12, 12, 0), pan: num(t.pan, -100, 100, 0), tone: num(t.tone, -100, 100, 0),
        send: num(t.send, 0, 100, 0), choke: num(t.choke, 0, 2, 0), len: num(t.len, 1, steps, steps), mute: !!t.mute, solo: !!t.solo, pats };
    });
    const chain = String(p.chain || 'A').toUpperCase().replace(/[^ABCD]/g, '').slice(0, 16) || 'A';
    return { v: 2, title: String(p.title || 'Untitled pattern').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 60) || 'Untitled pattern', bpm: num(p.bpm, 60, 200, 100), swing: num(p.swing, 0, 60, 0), steps,
      cur: BANKS.includes(p.cur) ? p.cur : 'A', chain, song: !!p.song, tracks };
  }
  let pat = cleanPattern(store.get(SEQ_CUR_KEY, null)) || defaultPattern(); if (!pat.tracks.length) pat = defaultPattern();
  let seqSaveT = 0; const seqPersist = () => { clearTimeout(seqSaveT); seqSaveT = setTimeout(() => store.set(SEQ_CUR_KEY, pat), 300); };
  const laneOf = (t, b = pat.cur) => t.pats[b] || (t.pats[b] = { s: blankPat(pat.steps), p: {} });
  const bankUsed = (b) => pat.tracks.some((t) => t.pats[b] && /[xX]/.test(t.pats[b].s));
  const seqBufs = new Map();
  function seqLoad(slug) { const s = bySlug[slug]; if (!s || seqBufs.has(slug)) return; seqBufs.set(slug, null); getBuf(s).then((b) => seqBufs.set(slug, b)).catch(() => { seqBufs.delete(slug); toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); }); }
  const SEQ = { playing: false, next: 0, timer: 0, G: null, marks: [], raf: 0, picker: null, slot: 0, a: 0, abs: {}, bank: 'A', queued: null, fill: false, rec: false, last: null,
    lane: 'steps', menu: -1, undo: [], redo: [], burst: 0, clip: null, taps: [] };

  // undo / redo: snapshots of the whole project
  function snap() { SEQ.undo.push(JSON.stringify(pat)); if (SEQ.undo.length > 80) SEQ.undo.shift(); SEQ.redo = []; }
  function snapSoon() { if (!SEQ.burst) snap(); clearTimeout(SEQ.burst); SEQ.burst = setTimeout(() => { SEQ.burst = 0; }, 700); }
  function seqUndo(redo) { const from = redo ? SEQ.redo : SEQ.undo; if (!from.length) { toast(redo ? 'Nothing to redo' : 'Nothing to undo'); return; } (redo ? SEQ.undo : SEQ.redo).push(JSON.stringify(pat)); pat = JSON.parse(from.pop()); seqPersist(); renderSeq(); }

  // audio graph: tracks → filter → pan → volume → (reverb send) → glue compressor
  function seqIR(ctx) { const sr = ctx.sampleRate, n = Math.floor(sr * 2.2); const b = ctx.createBuffer(2, n, sr); let seed = 7; for (let c = 0; c < 2; c++) { const d = b.getChannelData(c); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647; const t = i / sr; d[i] = (seed / 2147483647 * 2 - 1) * Math.exp(-t * 3.2) * Math.min(1, t / 0.012) * 0.5; } } return b; }
  function seqGraph(ctx, comp = true) {
    const out = ctx.createGain(); let end = ctx.destination;
    if (comp) { const c = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor(); c.threshold.value = -8; c.ratio.value = 6; c.connect(ctx.destination); end = c; }
    out.connect(end); const rev = ctx.createConvolver(); rev.buffer = seqIR(ctx); const rg = ctx.createGain(); rg.gain.value = 0.8; rev.connect(rg); rg.connect(end);
    return { ctx, out, rev, buses: new Map(), voices: new Map() };
  }
  function setBus(b, t, ctx) {
    const ny = ctx.sampleRate / 2 - 100; b.f.Q.value = 0.7;
    if (t.tone < 0) { b.f.type = 'lowpass'; b.f.frequency.value = Math.min(ny, 20000 * Math.pow(250 / 20000, -t.tone / 100)); }
    else if (t.tone > 0) { b.f.type = 'highpass'; b.f.frequency.value = 20 * Math.pow(100, t.tone / 100); }
    else { b.f.type = 'lowpass'; b.f.frequency.value = ny; }
    b.p.pan.value = t.pan / 100; b.v.gain.value = t.vol; b.s.gain.value = t.send / 100 * 0.6;
  }
  function seqBus(G, t) {
    let b = G.buses.get(t);
    if (!b) { const c = G.ctx; b = { in: c.createGain(), f: c.createBiquadFilter(), p: c.createStereoPanner(), v: c.createGain(), s: c.createGain() }; b.in.connect(b.f); b.f.connect(b.p); b.p.connect(b.v); b.v.connect(G.out); b.v.connect(b.s); b.s.connect(G.rev); G.buses.set(t, b); setBus(b, t, c); }
    return b;
  }
  function seqLive() { const c = ac(); if (!SEQ.G) SEQ.G = seqGraph(c); for (const [t, b] of SEQ.G.buses) if (!pat.tracks.includes(t)) { b.v.disconnect(); SEQ.G.buses.delete(t); } return SEQ.G; }
  const liveBus = (t) => { if (SEQ.G && SEQ.G.buses.has(t)) setBus(SEQ.G.buses.get(t), t, SEQ.G.ctx); };
  function seqHit(G, t, when, vel, tune = 0) {
    const buf = seqBufs.get(t.slug); if (!buf) return; const key = t.choke ? 'choke' + t.choke : t; const ctx = G.ctx;
    const prev = G.voices.get(key); if (prev) { try { prev.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, when, 0.006); prev.src.stop(when + 0.06); } catch {} }
    const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = vel; g.connect(seqBus(G, t).in); const src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.playbackRate.value = 2 ** ((t.pitch + tune) / 12); src.connect(g); src.start(when); G.voices.set(key, { src, g });
  }
  const audition = (t, tune = 0, vel = 1) => { const G = seqLive(); seqLoad(t.slug); seqHit(G, t, G.ctx.currentTime + 0.005, vel, tune); };
  const stepLen = (i) => { const d = 60 / pat.bpm / 4; const sw = pat.swing / 100 * 0.5; return d * (i % 2 === 0 ? 1 + sw : 1 - sw); };
  function condOk(c, loop, fill) { if (!c) return true; if (c === 'fill') return fill; if (c === '!fill') return !fill; if (c === 'first') return loop === 0; if (c === '!first') return loop > 0; const [a, b] = c.split(':').map(Number); return loop % b === a - 1; }
  /** The hits one track plays on one step: applies condition, chance, velocity, ratchets, nudge and step pitch. */
  function stepHits(t, bank, a, t0, dur, rng, fill) {
    const L = t.pats[bank]; if (!L) return []; const len = Math.min(t.len, pat.steps); const k = a % len; const c = L.s[k]; if (!c || c === '.') return [];
    const o = L.p[k] || {}; if (!condOk(o.cond, Math.floor(a / len), fill)) return []; if (o.chance != null && rng() * 100 >= o.chance) return [];
    const vel = (o.vel ?? (c === 'X' ? 100 : 62)) / 100; const rep = o.rep || 1; const start = t0 + (o.nudge || 0) / 100 * dur;
    return Array.from({ length: rep }, (_, r) => ({ when: start + r * dur / rep, vel: vel * 0.85 ** r, tune: o.tune || 0, len: dur / rep }));
  }
  const audible = (t) => !t.mute && !(pat.tracks.some((x) => x.solo) && !t.solo);
  function seqTick() {
    const G = SEQ.G;
    while (SEQ.next < actx.currentTime + 0.12) {
      const bank = SEQ.bank; const a = SEQ.abs[bank] || 0; const dur = stepLen(SEQ.a);
      for (const t of pat.tracks) if (audible(t)) for (const h of stepHits(t, bank, a, SEQ.next, dur, Math.random, SEQ.fill)) seqHit(G, t, Math.max(h.when, actx.currentTime), h.vel, h.tune);
      SEQ.marks.push({ bank, a, t: SEQ.next, dur, slot: SEQ.slot }); SEQ.abs[bank] = a + 1; SEQ.next += dur;
      if (++SEQ.a >= pat.steps) { SEQ.a = 0; if (pat.song) { SEQ.slot = (SEQ.slot + 1) % pat.chain.length; SEQ.bank = pat.chain[SEQ.slot]; } else if (SEQ.queued) { SEQ.bank = SEQ.queued; SEQ.queued = null; } }
    }
  }
  function seqDraw() {
    const now = actx.currentTime; let m = null; while (SEQ.marks.length && SEQ.marks[0].t <= now) m = SEQ.marks.shift();
    if (m) {
      SEQ.last = m; if (m.bank !== pat.cur) { pat.cur = m.bank; renderSeq(); }
      for (const row of document.querySelectorAll('#seqview .trk')) { const t = pat.tracks[+row.dataset.t]; if (!t) continue; const k = m.a % Math.min(t.len, pat.steps); row.querySelector('.steps .now')?.classList.remove('now'); row.querySelectorAll('.steps button')[k]?.classList.add('now'); }
      const pos = $('#seq-pos'); if (pos) pos.textContent = pat.song ? `Song ${m.slot + 1}/${pat.chain.length} · ${m.bank}` : `Pattern ${m.bank}${SEQ.queued ? ` → ${SEQ.queued}` : ''}`;
    }
    if (SEQ.playing) SEQ.raf = requestAnimationFrame(seqDraw);
  }
  /** Start playback. Shows Stop at once, loads any sounds that aren't ready, and explains why nothing would play. */
  async function seqPlay() {
    const G = seqLive(); if (P.playing) pPause(); if (pat.song && !pat.chain.length) pat.chain = 'A';
    if (!pat.tracks.length) { toast('Add a track first: press “Add a track” or pick a template.'); return; }
    const banks = pat.song ? [...new Set(pat.chain)] : [pat.cur];
    if (!pat.tracks.some((t) => banks.some((b) => t.pats[b] && /[xX]/.test(t.pats[b].s)))) toast(pat.song ? `Song mode plays the chain “${pat.chain}”, and those patterns are empty. Turn off Song mode or change the chain.` : `Pattern ${pat.cur} has no steps yet. Tap some squares in the grid so it has something to play.`);
    else if (pat.song && !pat.chain.includes(pat.cur)) toast(`Song mode is on, so it plays the chain “${pat.chain}”, not pattern ${pat.cur}. Add ${pat.cur} to the chain or turn Song mode off.`);
    else if (pat.tracks.every((t) => !audible(t))) toast('Every track is muted. Unmute one to hear it.');
    const run = SEQ.run = (SEQ.run || 0) + 1; SEQ.playing = true; seqSyncPlay();
    const missing = pat.tracks.filter((t) => !seqBufs.get(t.slug));
    if (missing.length) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-seq="play"]').forEach((b) => b.classList.add('loading'));
      await Promise.race([Promise.all(missing.map((t) => getBuf(bySlug[t.slug]).then((b) => seqBufs.set(t.slug, b)).catch(() => {}))), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 8000))]);
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-seq="play"]').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('loading'));
      if (!SEQ.playing || run !== SEQ.run) return;
    }
    Object.assign(SEQ, { slot: 0, a: 0, abs: {}, queued: null, marks: [], bank: pat.song ? pat.chain[0] : pat.cur, next: G.ctx.currentTime + 0.08 });
    clearInterval(SEQ.timer); SEQ.timer = setInterval(seqTick, 25); seqTick(); cancelAnimationFrame(SEQ.raf); SEQ.raf = requestAnimationFrame(seqDraw); seqSyncPlay();
  }
  function seqStop() {
    SEQ.playing = false; SEQ.last = null; SEQ.run = (SEQ.run || 0) + 1; clearInterval(SEQ.timer); cancelAnimationFrame(SEQ.raf);
    if (SEQ.G) { for (const v of SEQ.G.voices.values()) { try { v.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, actx.currentTime, 0.01); v.src.stop(actx.currentTime + 0.1); } catch {} } SEQ.G.voices.clear(); }
    document.querySelectorAll('.steps .now').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('now')); const pos = $('#seq-pos'); if (pos) pos.textContent = ''; seqSyncPlay();
  }
  function seqSyncPlay() {
    for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-seq="play"]')) { b.setAttribute('aria-label', SEQ.playing ? 'Stop the sequencer' : 'Play the sequencer'); b.innerHTML = `<svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${SEQ.playing ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg>${b.classList.contains('withlabel') ? `<span>${SEQ.playing ? 'Stop' : 'Play'}</span>` : ''}`; }
    const st = $('#seqdock-state'); if (st) st.textContent = SEQ.playing ? (pat.song ? `Playing song ${pat.chain}` : `Playing pattern ${pat.cur}`) : 'Stopped · Space or the button plays';
  }

  // pads: keys 1–8 play tracks; with Record on they write hits into the playing pattern, quantized to the nearest step
  function seqPad(i, accent) {
    const t = pat.tracks[i]; if (!t) return; audition(t, 0, accent ? 1 : 0.75);
    const row = document.querySelector(`#seqview .trk[data-t="${i}"] .tname`); if (row) { row.classList.add('hitflash'); setTimeout(() => row.classList.remove('hitflash'), 120); }
    if (!SEQ.rec || !SEQ.playing || !SEQ.last) return;
    const m = SEQ.last; const late = actx.currentTime - m.t; const a = m.a + (late > m.dur / 2 ? 1 : 0); const k = a % Math.min(t.len, pat.steps);
    snapSoon(); const L = laneOf(t, m.bank); if (L.s[k] === '.') { L.s = L.s.slice(0, k) + (accent ? 'X' : 'x') + L.s.slice(k + 1); seqPersist(); const cell = document.querySelector(`#seqview .trk[data-t="${i}"] [data-step="${k}"]`); if (cell) seqCell(cell, t, k); }
  }

  function addToSeq(s) {
    if (pat.tracks.some((t) => t.slug === s.slug)) { toast(`“${s.title}” is already in the sequencer.`); return; }
    if (pat.tracks.length >= SEQ_MAX_TRACKS) { toast('The sequencer holds 8 tracks. Remove one first.'); return; }
    snap(); pat.tracks.push(newTrack(s.slug, pat.steps)); seqLoad(s.slug); seqPersist();
    toast(`Added “${s.title}” to the sequencer`); if (state.view === 'seq') renderSeq();
  }
  // Swap a track for the next similar sound (same family and part), like a sample-swap button.
  function seqSimilar(s) {
    if (s.g) { const k0 = Object.keys(s.p || {})[0]; return SOUNDS.filter((x) => x.g === s.g && !x.custom && (k0 == null || typeof s.p[k0] !== 'string' || x.p[k0] === s.p[k0])); }
    return SOUNDS.filter((x) => !x.g && !x.custom && x.cat === s.cat && !x.loop);
  }
  function seqSwap(i, dir) { const t = pat.tracks[i]; const pool = seqSimilar(bySlug[t.slug]); if (pool.length < 2) { toast('No similar sounds to swap in.'); return; } const j = pool.findIndex((x) => x.slug === t.slug); snap(); t.slug = pool[(j + dir + pool.length) % pool.length].slug; seqLoad(t.slug); seqPersist(); renderSeq(); const ready = () => { if (seqBufs.get(t.slug)) audition(t); else setTimeout(ready, 60); }; setTimeout(ready, 0); }

  // step-editing tools
  function euclid(k, n, rot) { const s = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ((i * k) % n) < k && k > 0 ? 'x' : '.'); return s.map((_, i) => s[(i - rot + n * 4) % n]).join(''); }
  function setRow(t, s, keepP = false) { const L = laneOf(t); const full = (s + blankPat(pat.steps)).slice(0, pat.steps); if (!keepP) L.p = {}; else for (const k of Object.keys(L.p)) if (full[k] === '.') delete L.p[k]; L.s = full; }
  function shiftRow(t, dir) {
    const L = laneOf(t); const len = Math.min(t.len, pat.steps); const head = [...L.s.slice(0, len)]; const p2 = {};
