    const s2 = head.map((_, i) => head[(i - dir + len) % len]).join(''); for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(L.p)) if (+k < len) p2[(+k + dir + len) % len] = v; else p2[k] = v;
    L.s = s2 + L.s.slice(len); L.p = p2;
  }
  function randomRow(t) {
    const s = bySlug[t.slug]; const part = (s.p && s.p.part) || ''; const len = Math.min(t.len, pat.steps);
    const prob = (i) => part === 'kick' ? (i % 8 === 0 ? 0.9 : i % 4 === 0 ? 0.35 : i % 2 === 0 ? 0.18 : 0.08) : part === 'snare' || part === 'clap' ? (i % 8 === 4 ? 0.95 : 0.07) : part === 'hat' ? (i % 2 === 0 ? 0.85 : 0.35) : (i % 4 === 0 ? 0.3 : 0.2);
    let out = ''; for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) { const r = Math.random(); out += r < prob(i) ? (r < prob(i) * 0.25 ? 'X' : 'x') : '.'; } setRow(t, out);
  }
  function humanize() { snap(); for (const t of pat.tracks) { const L = laneOf(t); for (let k = 0; k < pat.steps; k++) if (L.s[k] !== '.') { const o = L.p[k] || (L.p[k] = {}); o.vel = num((L.s[k] === 'X' ? 100 : 72) - Math.random() * 22, 5, 100, 80); const n = Math.round((Math.random() - 0.5) * 8); if (n) o.nudge = n; else delete o.nudge; } } seqPersist(); renderSeq(); toast('Humanized: small changes to timing and velocity. Undo to go back.'); }
  function setSteps(n) {
    snap(); const old = pat.steps;
    for (const t of pat.tracks) { for (const L of Object.values(t.pats)) { let s = L.s; const p = { ...L.p }; while (s.length < n) { const l = s.length; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(L.p)) if (+k + l < n) p[+k + l] = { ...v }; s += s; } L.s = s.slice(0, n); for (const k of Object.keys(p)) if (+k >= n) delete p[k]; L.p = p; } t.len = t.len === old ? n : Math.min(t.len, n); }
    pat.steps = n; seqPersist(); renderSeq();
  }

  function laneVal(t, k, lane) {
    const L = laneOf(t); const c = L.s[k]; const o = L.p[k] || {}; if (lane === 'vel') return o.vel ?? (c === 'X' ? 100 : 62);
    if (lane === 'cond') return o.cond || ''; return o[lane] ?? LANES[lane].def;
  }
  function setLane(t, k, lane, v) { const L = laneOf(t); if (L.s[k] === '.') return; const o = L.p[k] || {}; const d = LANES[lane]; if (lane === 'cond') { if (v) o.cond = v; else delete o.cond; } else { v = Math.max(d.min, Math.min(d.max, Math.round(v / d.step) * d.step)); if (lane !== 'vel' && v === d.def) delete o[lane]; else o[lane] = v; } if (Object.keys(o).length) L.p[k] = o; else delete L.p[k]; }
  function seqCell(btn, t, k) {
    const s = bySlug[t.slug]; const L = laneOf(t); const c = L.s[k]; const lane = SEQ.lane; const o = L.p[k] || {};
    btn.dataset.v = c; btn.classList.toggle('out', k >= t.len); btn.classList.toggle('lk', !!(o.chance != null || o.cond || o.rep || o.nudge || o.tune || o.vel != null));
    if (lane === 'steps') { btn.innerHTML = o.rep ? `<b>${o.rep}</b>` : ''; btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(c !== '.')); btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${s.title}, step ${k + 1}: ${c === '.' ? 'off' : c === 'X' ? 'accent' : 'on'}`); return; }
    btn.removeAttribute('aria-pressed');
    if (c === '.') { btn.innerHTML = ''; btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${s.title}, step ${k + 1}: off`); return; }
    const d = LANES[lane]; const v = laneVal(t, k, lane);
    if (lane === 'cond') { btn.innerHTML = `<b>${v ? esc(condLabel(v)) : '·'}</b>`; btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${s.title}, step ${k + 1} condition: ${condLabel(v)}`); return; }
    const f = (v - d.min) / (d.max - d.min);
    btn.innerHTML = d.bi ? `<i class="bi" style="--a:${Math.min(f, 0.5)};--b:${Math.max(f, 0.5)}"></i>` : `<i style="--h:${Math.max(0.04, f)}"></i>`;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${s.title}, step ${k + 1} ${d.name.toLowerCase()}: ${d.fmt(v)}`); btn.title = d.fmt(v);
  }

  function seqTrackHtml(t, i) {
    const s = bySlug[t.slug]; const len = Math.min(t.len, pat.steps); const lane = SEQ.lane; const open = SEQ.menu === i;
    const cells = Array.from({ length: pat.steps }, (_, k) => `<button type="button" data-step="${k}"></button>`).join('');
    const opt = (v, cur, label) => `<option value="${v}"${v === cur ? ' selected' : ''}>${label}</option>`;
    return `<div class="trk${open ? ' open' : ''}" data-t="${i}">
      <button class="tname" type="button" data-seq="pick" title="Change sound (key ${i + 1} plays it)"><span class="art" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})"></span><span>${esc(s.title)}</span><kbd>${i + 1}</kbd></button>
      <div class="tctl"><button class="ib" type="button" data-seq="mute" aria-pressed="${t.mute}" aria-label="Mute ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${t.mute ? 'mute' : 'vol'}"/></svg></button><button class="ib txt" type="button" data-seq="solo" aria-pressed="${t.solo}" aria-label="Solo ${esc(s.title)}">S</button><button class="ib txt" type="button" data-seq="swap" aria-label="Swap ${esc(s.title)} for a similar sound" title="Swap for a similar sound">⇄</button></div>
      <div class="tctl vol"><input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(t.vol * 100)}" data-seq="vol" aria-label="Volume: ${esc(s.title)}"><select data-seq="pitch" aria-label="Pitch: ${esc(s.title)}">${Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, k) => k - 12).map((v) => opt(v, t.pitch, (v > 0 ? '+' : '') + v)).join('')}</select></div>
      <div class="steps${lane !== 'steps' ? ' lanemode' : ''}" role="group" aria-label="Steps for ${esc(s.title)}${len < pat.steps ? `, loops every ${len}` : ''}" style="--n:${pat.steps}">${cells}</div>
      <button class="ib" type="button" data-seq="more" aria-expanded="${open}" aria-label="More for ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${open ? 'x' : 'layers'}"/></svg></button></div>
      ${open ? `<div class="tmore" data-t="${i}" role="group" aria-label="Settings for ${esc(s.title)}">
        <label>Pan <input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${t.pan}" data-seq="pan"><output>${t.pan === 0 ? 'C' : t.pan < 0 ? 'L' + -t.pan : 'R' + t.pan}</output></label>
        <label>Filter <input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${t.tone}" data-seq="tone"><output>${t.tone === 0 ? 'Off' : t.tone < 0 ? 'Low-pass' : 'High-pass'}</output></label>
        <label>Reverb <input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${t.send}" data-seq="send"><output>${t.send}%</output></label>
        <label>Choke <select data-seq="choke">${opt(0, t.choke, 'None')}${opt(1, t.choke, 'Group 1')}${opt(2, t.choke, 'Group 2')}</select></label>
        <label>Length <select data-seq="len">${Array.from({ length: pat.steps }, (_, k) => k + 1).map((v) => opt(v, len, v + (v === pat.steps ? ' (full)' : ''))).join('')}</select></label>
        <span class="eu">Euclid <select data-seq="eu-k" aria-label="Hits">${Array.from({ length: len + 1 }, (_, k) => opt(k, Math.min(4, len), k + ' hits')).join('')}</select><select data-seq="eu-r" aria-label="Rotate">${Array.from({ length: len }, (_, k) => opt(k, 0, 'rotate ' + k)).join('')}</select><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="euclid">Apply</button></span>
        <span class="tools"><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="shl" aria-label="Shift steps left">← Shift</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="shr" aria-label="Shift steps right">Shift →</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="rand">Randomize</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="copy">Copy</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="paste" ${SEQ.clip ? '' : 'disabled'}>Paste</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="dup" ${pat.tracks.length >= SEQ_MAX_TRACKS ? 'disabled' : ''}>Duplicate</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="clrrow">Clear</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="rm">Remove track</button></span>
      </div>` : ''}`;
  }
  function renderSeq() {
    const v = $('#seqview'); if (!v) return; const saved = store.get(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, []); const lane = SEQ.lane;
    const focus = document.activeElement && v.contains(document.activeElement) ? document.activeElement.id : '';
    const pickQ = SEQ.picker != null ? ($('#seq-q')?.value || '') : null;
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="seqhead"><h2>Sequencer</h2><div class="seqtools">
        <select data-seq="tpl" aria-label="Start from a template"><option value="">Templates…</option>${TEMPLATES.map((tp, i) => `<option value="${i}">${esc(tp.name)} · ${tp.bpm}</option>`).join('')}</select>
        <button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="new">New</button><button class="ib" type="button" data-seq="undo" aria-label="Undo" title="Undo (Ctrl+Z)" ${SEQ.undo.length ? '' : 'disabled'}>↶</button><button class="ib" type="button" data-seq="redo" aria-label="Redo" title="Redo (Ctrl+Shift+Z)" ${SEQ.redo.length ? '' : 'disabled'}>↷</button>
        <button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="save">Save in browser</button><button class="btn dl" type="button" data-seq="file">Save pattern file</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="open">Open file</button></div></div>
      <div class="seqbar"><button class="seqplay" id="seqplay" type="button" data-seq="play"></button>
        <label for="seq-title" class="sr">Pattern name</label><input id="seq-title" type="text" maxlength="60" value="${esc(pat.title)}" data-seq="title">
        <label>Tempo <input id="seq-bpm" type="number" min="60" max="200" value="${pat.bpm}" data-seq="bpm"></label><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="tap" title="Tap along to set the tempo">Tap</button>
        <label>Swing <input id="seq-swing" type="range" min="0" max="60" value="${pat.swing}" data-seq="swing"><output>${pat.swing}%</output></label>
        <label>Steps <select id="seq-steps" data-seq="steps">${STEP_OPTS.map((n) => `<option${pat.steps === n ? ' selected' : ''}>${n}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
        <button class="btn tog rec" type="button" data-seq="rec" aria-pressed="${SEQ.rec}" title="Record: play keys 1–8 while the pattern runs">● Rec</button>
        <button class="btn tog" type="button" data-seq="fill" aria-pressed="${SEQ.fill}" title="Fill: plays steps set to the Fill condition (key F)">Fill</button>
        <button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="human" title="Small random changes to timing and velocity">Humanize</button>
        <span class="pos" id="seq-pos" aria-live="off"></span></div>
      <div class="seqbar seqai"><label for="seq-desc" class="lbl">Describe a beat</label><input id="seq-desc" type="text" maxlength="200" value="${esc(SEQ.desc || '')}" placeholder="dusty boom bap at 88 with a lazy snare" autocomplete="off">
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-seq="describe"${SEQ.aiBusy ? ' disabled' : ''}><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-spark"/></svg>Build it</button>
        <button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="bsec"${SEQ.aiBusy ? ' disabled' : ''} title="Writes a variation into the next empty pattern and chains them">Make a B section</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="fill2"${SEQ.aiBusy ? ' disabled' : ''} title="Copies this pattern with a drum fill at the end">Add a fill</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="bbopen" aria-expanded="${!!SEQ.bbOpen}">🎤 Beatbox it</button>
        <span class="hint" id="seq-ai-note" aria-live="polite">${esc(SEQ.aiNote || '')}</span></div>${bbPanelHtml()}
      <div class="seqbar banks"><span class="lbl">Pattern</span><div class="bankbtns" role="group" aria-label="Pattern bank">${BANKS.map((b) => `<button type="button" data-bank="${b}" aria-pressed="${pat.cur === b}" class="${bankUsed(b) ? 'used' : ''}${SEQ.queued === b ? ' queued' : ''}">${b}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        <label>Copy to <select data-seq="copyto"><option value="">—</option>${BANKS.filter((b) => b !== pat.cur).map((b) => `<option>${b}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="clrbank">Clear ${pat.cur}</button>
        <span class="songctl"><button class="btn tog" type="button" data-seq="song" aria-pressed="${pat.song}">Song mode</button><label>Chain <input id="seq-chain" type="text" maxlength="16" value="${pat.chain}" data-seq="chain" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" aria-describedby="chainhelp"></label><span id="chainhelp" class="hint">e.g. AABA</span></span></div>
      <div class="lanes" role="tablist" aria-label="Edit">${Object.entries(LANES).map(([k, d]) => `<button type="button" role="tab" data-lane="${k}" aria-selected="${lane === k}">${d.name}</button>`).join('')}<span class="hint">${LANES[lane].help}</span></div>
      <div class="seqgrid">${pat.tracks.map(seqTrackHtml).join('')}</div>
      <div class="seqtools"><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="add" ${pat.tracks.length >= SEQ_MAX_TRACKS ? 'disabled' : ''}><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg>Add a track</button>
        <span class="hint">Keys 1–8 play each track (Shift for accent) · Space plays · Ctrl+Z undoes · use “Add to sequencer” on any sound.</span></div>
      <div class="picker" id="seqpicker" hidden><div class="pkhead"><label for="seq-q" id="seq-pick-label">Choose a sound</label><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="pickclose">Close</button></div><p class="pknote">Press ▶ to hear a sound first, then tap its name to use it.</p><input id="seq-q" type="search" placeholder="Search kick, snare, 808, braam, glass…" autocomplete="off"><ul id="seq-results"></ul></div>
      <div class="seqbar export dl"><span class="lbl">Export</span><select id="seq-exp" aria-label="Export format"><option value="mix">Mix (WAV)</option><option value="stems">Stems (a WAV per track + mix)</option><option value="midi">MIDI (for any DAW)</option></select>
        <select id="seq-reps" aria-label="Length">${pat.song ? '<option value="1">Song once</option><option value="2" selected>Song twice</option>' : '<option value="1">1 pass</option><option value="2" selected>2 passes</option><option value="4">4 passes</option><option value="8">8 passes</option>'}</select>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-seq="export"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg>Export</button></div>
      <div class="seqcols">
        <section class="plist" aria-label="Community patterns"><h3>Community patterns</h3><p class="note" id="comm-note">${dbCap ? 'Loading…' : 'Community sharing works when SoundWell is opened on claude.ai.'}</p>
          ${dbCap ? `<div class="seqtools"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-seq="share" id="seq-share">Share this pattern</button></div>` : ''}<ul id="comm-list"></ul></section>
        <section class="plist" aria-label="Saved in this browser"><h3>Saved in this browser</h3>${saved.length ? `<ul>${saved.map((p2, i) => `<li><span class="pt">${esc(p2.title)}</span><span class="pm">${p2.bpm} BPM · ${p2.tracks.length} tracks · ${p2.steps} steps</span><span class="pa"><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="loadmine" data-i="${i}">Load</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="delmine" data-i="${i}" aria-label="Delete ${esc(p2.title)}">Delete</button></span></li>`).join('')}</ul>` : '<p class="note">Nothing saved yet. “Save in browser” keeps patterns on this device.</p>'}</section>
      </div>
      <input type="file" id="seq-file" accept=".json,application/json" hidden>
      <div class="seqdock" role="region" aria-label="Sequencer controls"><button class="seqplay withlabel" type="button" data-seq="play"></button>
        <div class="dockinfo"><span class="dt">${esc(pat.title)}</span><span class="ds" id="seqdock-state"></span></div><span class="dbpm">${pat.bpm} BPM</span></div>`;
    for (const row of v.querySelectorAll('.trk')) { const t = pat.tracks[+row.dataset.t]; row.querySelectorAll('[data-step]').forEach((b, k) => seqCell(b, t, k)); }
    if (pickQ != null) { const t = pat.tracks[SEQ.picker]; $('#seqpicker').hidden = false; $('#seq-pick-label').textContent = t ? `Replace “${bySlug[t.slug].title}”` : 'Add a sound'; $('#seq-q').value = pickQ; seqResults(pickQ); }
    seqSyncPlay(); renderCommunity(); for (const t of pat.tracks) seqLoad(t.slug); if (focus) document.getElementById(focus)?.focus();
  }
  function seqPick(trackIndex) {
    SEQ.picker = trackIndex; const box = $('#seqpicker'); box.hidden = false; const t = pat.tracks[trackIndex];
    $('#seq-pick-label').textContent = t ? `Replace “${bySlug[t.slug].title}”` : 'Add a sound'; const q = $('#seq-q'); q.value = ''; seqResults(''); q.focus(); box.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  }
  function seqResults(q) {
    const toks = words(q); const pool = SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.kind === 'sfx' || !s.loop); const verb = pat.tracks[SEQ.picker] ? 'Use' : 'Add';
    const res = (toks.length ? pool.map((s) => [s, score(s, toks)]).filter((x) => x[1]).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map((x) => x[0]) : pool.filter((s) => s.cat === 'Drum Hits' || s.cat === 'Musical FX')).slice(0, 40);
    $('#seq-results').innerHTML = res.length ? res.map((s) => `<li><button class="pv" type="button" data-preview="${s.slug}" aria-label="Preview ${esc(s.title)}" aria-pressed="${PV.slug === s.slug}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${PV.slug === s.slug ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg></button><button class="pick" type="button" data-pickslug="${s.slug}" aria-label="${verb} ${esc(s.title)}"><span class="art" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})"></span><span class="pt">${esc(s.title)}</span><span class="pm">${fmtDur(s.dur)}</span><span class="use">${verb}</span></button></li>`).join('') : '<li class="pm" style="padding:6px">No matching sounds.</li>';
  }
  // Preview a sound in the picker before using it (at the pitch of the track it would replace). One preview at a time.
  const PV = { slug: null, src: null, n: 0 };
  function pvSync() { for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-preview]')) { const on = b.dataset.preview === PV.slug; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on)); b.innerHTML = `<svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${on ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg>`; } }
  function pvStop() { if (PV.src) { try { PV.src.stop(); } catch {} } PV.src = null; PV.slug = null; PV.n++; pvSync(); }
  async function seqPreview(slug) {
    if (PV.slug === slug) { pvStop(); return; } pvStop(); const s = bySlug[slug]; if (!s) return; const n = PV.n; PV.slug = slug; pvSync();
    const btn = document.querySelector(`[data-preview="${slug}"]`); btn?.classList.add('loading');
    let buf = null; try { const c = ac(); buf = await getBuf(s); seqBufs.set(slug, buf); if (n !== PV.n) return; const t = state.view === 'seq' ? pat.tracks[SEQ.picker] : null;
      const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.playbackRate.value = 2 ** ((t ? t.pitch : 0) / 12); const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0.85; src.connect(g); g.connect(c.destination);
      src.onended = () => { if (PV.src === src) { PV.src = null; PV.slug = null; pvSync(); } }; PV.src = src; src.start(); }
    catch { toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); pvStop(); }
    finally { document.querySelector(`[data-preview="${slug}"]`)?.classList.remove('loading'); }
  }
  function seqApplyPick(slug) {
    const s = bySlug[slug]; if (!s) return; pvStop(); snap(); let t;
    if (SEQ.picker == null || SEQ.picker >= pat.tracks.length) { if (pat.tracks.length >= SEQ_MAX_TRACKS) { toast('The sequencer holds 8 tracks.'); return; } t = newTrack(slug, pat.steps); pat.tracks.push(t); }
    else { t = pat.tracks[SEQ.picker]; t.slug = slug; }
    seqLoad(slug); seqPersist(); SEQ.picker = null; renderSeq(); const ready = (n) => { if (seqBufs.get(slug)) audition(t); else if (n < 40) setTimeout(() => ready(n + 1), 60); }; ready(0);
  }

  // ---------- beat-making partner: describe a beat, make a B section, add a fill ----------
  const nextFreeBank = () => BANKS.find((b) => b !== pat.cur && !bankUsed(b));
  const partOf = (t) => { const s = bySlug[t.slug]; const x = (s.slug + ' ' + s.title).toLowerCase(); return /kick/.test(x) ? 'kick' : /snare|clap|rim/.test(x) ? 'snare' : /tom/.test(x) ? 'tom' : /open hi-hat|open hat/.test(x) ? 'ohat' : /hat|shaker|tambourine/.test(x) ? 'hat' : /cymbal|crash|ride/.test(x) ? 'cym' : /808|bass/.test(x) ? 'bass' : 'other'; };
  function aiNote(msg) { SEQ.aiNote = msg; const n = $('#seq-ai-note'); if (n) n.textContent = msg; }
  function aiBusy(on) { SEQ.aiBusy = on; document.querySelectorAll('[data-seq="describe"],[data-seq="bsec"],[data-seq="fill2"]').forEach((b) => { b.disabled = on; }); }
  function beatPool(text) {
    const one = SOUNDS.filter((s) => !s.custom && !s.loop && (s.kind === 'sfx' || s.kind === 'music'));
    const core = one.filter((s) => s.g === 'drumhit' || s.g === 'note808' || s.g === 'notebass' || (s.g === 'stab' && s.p.minor === 1) || s.g === 'sc-hit' || (s.g === 'sc-timpani' && s.p.roll === 0));
    const toks = words(text); const extra = toks.length ? one.map((s) => [s, score(s, toks)]).filter((x) => x[1] > 0).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 30).map((x) => x[0]) : [];
    return [...new Set([...extra, ...core])].slice(0, 200);
  }
  function stepsStr(s, n) { return (String(s || '').replace(/[^.xX]/g, '.') + blankPat(n)).slice(0, n); }
  function paramsFrom(list) { const p = {}; for (const pr of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) { if (!pr || !Number.isFinite(+pr.step)) continue; const o = {}; for (const k of ['vel', 'chance', 'rep', 'nudge', 'tune', 'cond']) if (pr[k] != null) o[k] = pr[k]; p[+pr.step] = o; } return p; }
  function aiToPattern(r, text) {
    const steps = +r.steps === 32 ? 32 : 16;
    const tracks = (Array.isArray(r.tracks) ? r.tracks : []).filter((t) => t && bySlug[t.slug]).slice(0, SEQ_MAX_TRACKS).map((t) => {
      const pats = {}; for (const b of BANKS) if (typeof t[b] === 'string') pats[b] = { s: stepsStr(t[b], steps), p: {} };
      for (const pr of Array.isArray(t.params) ? t.params : []) { const b = BANKS.includes(pr && pr.bank) ? pr.bank : 'A'; if (pats[b]) Object.assign(pats[b].p, paramsFrom([pr])); }
      return { slug: t.slug, vol: t.vol ?? 0.8, pan: t.pan, send: t.send, choke: t.choke, pats };
    });
    const p2 = cleanPattern({ title: r.title || text, bpm: r.bpm, swing: r.swing, steps, cur: 'A', chain: r.chain, song: false, tracks }); if (!p2 || !p2.tracks.length) return null;
    const used = BANKS.filter((b) => p2.tracks.some((t) => t.pats[b] && /[xX]/.test(t.pats[b].s))); p2.chain = [...p2.chain].filter((c) => used.includes(c)).join('') || 'A'; p2.song = p2.chain.length > 1 && new Set(p2.chain).size > 1;
    return p2;
  }
  function keywordBeat(text) {
    const t = text.toLowerCase(); const map = [[/trap|drill/, 'Trap'], [/house|disco|dance/, 'House'], [/techno|industrial|warehouse|rave/, 'Techno'], [/lo-?fi|chill|study|dusty/, 'Lo-fi'], [/reggaeton|dembow|latin/, 'Reggaeton'], [/drum ?(&|and|n) ?bass|jungle|dnb|d&b/, 'Drum & bass'], [/afro|amapiano/, 'Afrobeats'], [/cinematic|epic|trailer|war|battle|toms?\b/, 'Cinematic drums'], [/boom ?bap|hip ?hop|90s|rap/, 'Boom bap']];
    const hit = map.find(([re]) => re.test(t)); const tp = TEMPLATES.find((x) => x.name === (hit ? hit[1] : 'Boom bap')); const p2 = templatePattern(tp);
    const bpm = /\b(\d{2,3})\s*bpm\b/.exec(t) || /\bat\s+(\d{2,3})\b/.exec(t); if (bpm && +bpm[1] >= 60 && +bpm[1] <= 200) p2.bpm = +bpm[1];
    if (/swing|lazy|laid.?back|drunk|loose/.test(t)) p2.swing = Math.max(p2.swing, 35); if (/straight|tight|robotic|quantized/.test(t)) p2.swing = 0;
    p2.title = text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 60) || p2.title; return { p: p2, name: tp.name };
  }
  async function describeBeat() {
    const text = ($('#seq-desc')?.value || '').trim(); SEQ.desc = text;
    if (!text) { toast('Describe the beat first, like “dusty boom bap at 88 with a lazy snare”.'); $('#seq-desc')?.focus(); return; }
    aiBusy(true); let p2 = null; let how = '';
    if (sampleCap) {
      aiNote('Claude is writing your beat…');
      try {
        const pool = beatPool(text);
        const prompt = `You are a beat programmer working in a step sequencer. Build a beat from this description: "${text.slice(0, 300)}".
Rules:
- Use 3 to 8 tracks, and ONLY slugs from the SOUNDS list below. Pick sounds that fit the genre and mood.
- steps is 16 (one bar of 16th notes) unless the description clearly needs 32.
- Every pattern string has exactly that many characters: "." = off, "x" = hit, "X" = accent (louder).
- Pattern "A" is the main groove. Also write "B", a variation for the end of a phrase, and set chain to "AAAB". Use chain "A" only if a variation makes no sense.
- bpm 60 to 200 to suit the style; swing 0 to 60 (0 straight; 20 to 45 for laid-back hip hop or lo-fi).
- Optional per-step extras in "params": vel 5-100, chance 0-100 (odds the step plays), rep 2-4 (rolls, like trap hi-hats), nudge -50..50 (% of a step; positive is late and laid back), tune -12..12 (semitones; use it to write 808 or bass lines).
- Open and closed hi-hats share choke 1 so they cut each other. pan -100..100, send 0-100 (reverb), vol 0-1.
Reply with JSON only, no prose:
{"title":"short name","bpm":90,"swing":25,"steps":16,"chain":"AAAB","tracks":[{"slug":"...","vol":0.8,"pan":0,"send":0,"choke":0,"A":"X.......x.X.....","B":"X.......x.X...x.","params":[{"bank":"A","step":10,"vel":45}]}],"why":"one short sentence"}
SOUNDS (slug | title):
${pool.map((s) => `${s.slug} | ${s.title}`).join('\n')}`;
        const r = await sampleCap.json(prompt, { modelTier: 'quick' }); p2 = r && aiToPattern(r, text); if (p2) how = `Built by Helion Sound Labs${r.why ? `: ${String(r.why).slice(0, 140).replace(/[.\s]+$/, '')}` : ''}`;
      } catch (e) { p2 = null; if (e && e.code === 'not_granted') sampleCap = null; }
    }
    if (!p2) { const k = keywordBeat(text); p2 = k.p; how = `Started from the ${k.name} template${sampleCap ? ' (Claude didn’t answer)' : ''}`; }
    aiBusy(false); seqLoadPattern(p2); aiNote(how + '. Undo goes back.'); seqPlay();
  }
  function variationB(src, n) {
    // offline fallback: same groove with a few classic changes
    const r = seqRng(Date.now() % 1e6); const out = [];
    pat.tracks.forEach((t, i) => {
      const L = src[i]; let s = [...L.s]; const p = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(L.p)); const part = partOf(t); const q = n / 4;
      if (part === 'kick') { const k = [3, 7, 11, 14].map((x) => x * n / 16).find((x) => s[x] === '.' && r() < 0.7); if (k != null) s[k] = 'x'; }
      else if (part === 'hat') { const k = n - (r() < 0.5 ? 1 : 2); if (s[k] !== '.') p[k] = { ...(p[k] || {}), rep: 3, vel: 70 }; else { s[k] = 'x'; p[k] = { rep: 2, vel: 65 }; } }
      else if (part === 'snare') { const k = q * 3 + Math.round(3 * n / 16); if (s[k] === '.') { s[k] = 'x'; p[k] = { vel: 35 }; } }
      else if (part === 'bass') { const hits = s.map((c, k) => c !== '.' ? k : -1).filter((k) => k >= n / 2); for (const k of hits) p[k] = { ...(p[k] || {}), tune: r() < 0.5 ? 5 : -2 }; }
      else if (part === 'ohat') { const k = n - 2; if (s[k] === '.') s[k] = 'x'; }
      out.push({ s: s.join(''), p });
    });
    return out;
  }
  async function makeBSection() {
    const target = nextFreeBank(); if (!target) { toast('All four patterns are in use. Clear one first.'); return; }
    const cur = pat.cur; const n = pat.steps; const src = pat.tracks.map((t) => laneOf(t, cur)); if (!src.some((L) => /[xX]/.test(L.s))) { toast(`Pattern ${cur} is empty. Make a groove first.`); return; }
    aiBusy(true); let lanes = null; let how = '';
    if (sampleCap) {
      aiNote(`Claude is writing pattern ${target}…`);
      try {
        const lines = pat.tracks.map((t, i) => { const L = src[i]; const ex = Object.entries(L.p).map(([k, o]) => `step ${k} ${Object.entries(o).map(([a, b]) => `${a} ${b}`).join(' ')}`).join('; '); return `${i}. ${bySlug[t.slug].title}: ${L.s}${ex ? `   extras: ${ex}` : ''}`; }).join('\n');
        const prompt = `Here is a drum pattern from a step sequencer at ${pat.bpm} BPM, ${n} steps of 16th notes. "." off, "x" hit, "X" accent.
${lines}
Write a second pattern for the end of a phrase: keep the same tracks and feel, but add interest (move or add a kick, a hi-hat roll, ghost snares, a different 808 note, a pickup into the next bar). Don't change everything.
Per-step extras you may use: vel 5-100, chance 0-100, rep 2-4 (rolls), nudge -50..50, tune -12..12 (semitones).
Reply with JSON only: {"tracks":[{"i":0,"s":"exactly ${n} characters","params":[{"step":14,"rep":3,"vel":70}]}],"why":"one short sentence"}`;
        const r = await sampleCap.json(prompt, { modelTier: 'quick' });
        if (r && Array.isArray(r.tracks)) { lanes = src.map((L) => ({ s: L.s, p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(L.p)) })); for (const x of r.tracks) { const i = +x.i; if (lanes[i]) lanes[i] = { s: stepsStr(x.s, n), p: paramsFrom(x.params) }; } how = `Pattern ${target} by Helion Sound Labs${r.why ? `: ${String(r.why).slice(0, 140).replace(/[.\s]+$/, '')}` : ''}`; }
      } catch (e) { lanes = null; if (e && e.code === 'not_granted') sampleCap = null; }
    }
    if (!lanes) { lanes = variationB(src, n); how = `Pattern ${target}: a variation of ${cur}`; }
    snap(); pat.tracks.forEach((t, i) => { t.pats[target] = lanes[i]; }); const tidy = cleanPattern(pat); pat.tracks.forEach((t, i) => { t.pats = tidy.tracks[i].pats; });
    pat.chain = cur + cur + cur + target; pat.song = true; aiBusy(false); seqPersist(); renderSeq(); aiNote(`${how}. Song mode plays ${pat.chain}.`); if (SEQ.playing) { seqStop(); } seqPlay();
  }
  function addFill() {
    const target = nextFreeBank(); if (!target) { toast('All four patterns are in use. Clear one first.'); return; }
    const cur = pat.cur; const n = pat.steps; if (!pat.tracks.some((t) => /[xX]/.test(laneOf(t, cur).s))) { toast(`Pattern ${cur} is empty. Make a groove first.`); return; }
    snap(); const z = n - Math.max(4, n / 8); const zone = Array.from({ length: n - z }, (_, i) => z + i); const ramp = (i) => Math.round(50 + 50 * i / (zone.length - 1));
    let rolled = false; const toms = pat.tracks.filter((t) => partOf(t) === 'tom');
    for (const t of pat.tracks) {
      const L = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(laneOf(t, cur))); const s = [...L.s]; const part = partOf(t);
      if (part === 'snare' && !rolled && !toms.length) { rolled = true; zone.forEach((k, i) => { s[k] = i === zone.length - 1 ? 'X' : 'x'; L.p[k] = { vel: ramp(i), ...(i >= zone.length - 2 ? { rep: i === zone.length - 1 ? 3 : 2 } : {}) }; }); }
      else if (part === 'tom') { const ti = toms.indexOf(t); zone.forEach((k, i) => { const on = Math.floor(i * toms.length / zone.length) === ti; s[k] = on ? 'x' : '.'; if (on) L.p[k] = { vel: ramp(i) }; else delete L.p[k]; }); }
      else if (part === 'kick') zone.slice(1).forEach((k) => { s[k] = '.'; delete L.p[k]; });
      else if (part === 'hat' || part === 'ohat') zone.forEach((k, i) => { if (i === 0 && part === 'hat') { s[k] = 'x'; L.p[k] = { rep: 2, vel: 60 }; } else { s[k] = '.'; delete L.p[k]; } });
      L.s = s.join(''); t.pats[target] = L;
    }
    if (!rolled && !toms.length) { toast('Add a snare, clap or tom track so there’s something to roll on.'); seqUndo(); return; }
    pat.chain = cur + cur + cur + target; pat.song = true; seqPersist(); renderSeq(); aiNote(`Pattern ${target} is ${cur} with a fill at the end. Song mode plays ${pat.chain}.`); if (SEQ.playing) seqStop(); seqPlay();
  }

  // ---------- beatbox to beat: hear kick, snare and hi-hat in a recording and write them as steps ----------
  const BB = { stop: null };
  const BB_SLUG = { kick: 'drumhit-kick-0-0', snare: 'drumhit-snare-0-0', hat: 'drumhit-hat-0-0' };
  const canMic = () => !!(navigator.mediaDevices && navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia);
  function bbMsg(msg) { SEQ.bbMsg = msg; const n = $('#bb-status'); if (n) n.textContent = msg; }
  function fft(re, im) {
    const n = re.length;
    for (let i = 1, j = 0; i < n; i++) { let bit = n >> 1; for (; j & bit; bit >>= 1) j ^= bit; j ^= bit; if (i < j) { [re[i], re[j]] = [re[j], re[i]]; [im[i], im[j]] = [im[j], im[i]]; } }
    for (let len = 2; len <= n; len <<= 1) { const ang = -2 * Math.PI / len; const wr = Math.cos(ang), wi = Math.sin(ang); for (let i = 0; i < n; i += len) { let cr = 1, ci = 0; for (let k = 0; k < len / 2; k++) { const a = i + k, b = a + len / 2; const tr = re[b] * cr - im[b] * ci, ti = re[b] * ci + im[b] * cr; re[b] = re[a] - tr; im[b] = im[a] - ti; re[a] += tr; im[a] += ti; const nr = cr * wr - ci * wi; ci = cr * wi + ci * wr; cr = nr; } } }
  }
  /** Spectral centroid and share of energy below 250 Hz, over ~23 ms from the onset. */
  function bbFeatures(x, sr, at) {
    const N = 1024; const re = new Float64Array(N), im = new Float64Array(N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) re[i] = (x[at + i] || 0) * (0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * i / (N - 1)));
    fft(re, im); let s = 0, w = 0, low = 0; for (let k = 1; k < N / 2; k++) { const m = re[k] * re[k] + im[k] * im[k]; const f = k * sr / N; s += m * f; w += m; if (f < 250) low += m; }
    return { centroid: w ? s / w : 0, low: w ? low / w : 0 };
  }
  /** Find hits: a jump of about 7 dB within 15 ms above the noise floor, at least 70 ms apart. */
  function bbOnsets(x, sr) {
    const hop = Math.max(1, Math.round(sr * 0.005)), win = hop * 4; const env = [];
    for (let i = 0; i + win < x.length; i += hop) { let e = 0; for (let j = i; j < i + win; j++) e += x[j] * x[j]; env.push(Math.sqrt(e / win)); }
    if (!env.length) return []; const sorted = [...env].sort((a, b) => a - b); const noise = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.2)]; const peak = sorted[sorted.length - 1]; if (peak < 0.005) return [];
    const thr = Math.max(noise * 4, peak * 0.1); const out = []; let last = -1e9;
    for (let i = 3; i < env.length; i++) {
      const floor = Math.min(env[i - 1], env[i - 2], env[i - 3]); if (env[i] < thr || env[i] < floor * 2.2 || i - last < 14) continue;
      let pk = env[i]; for (let j = i; j < Math.min(env.length, i + 8); j++) pk = Math.max(pk, env[j]); last = i;
      // the sound itself starts where the signal first gets loud inside this frame: time the hit and analyse from there
      let at = i * hop; const lim = Math.min(x.length, i * hop + win); const gate = pk * 0.5; while (at < lim && Math.abs(x[at]) < gate) at++; if (at >= lim) at = i * hop;
      out.push({ t: at / sr, at: Math.max(0, at - Math.round(sr * 0.002)), amp: pk });
    }
    return out.map((o) => { const f = bbFeatures(x, sr, o.at); return { ...o, ...f, part: f.low > 0.45 || f.centroid < 800 ? 'kick' : f.centroid > 4000 ? 'hat' : 'snare' }; });
  }
  /** Snap hits to 16th notes. `t0` is where step 1 should be; a small phase search absorbs recording latency. */
  function bbQuantize(hits, bpm, t0, nSteps) {
    const step = 60 / bpm / 4; let best = 0, bestErr = Infinity;
    if (hits.length > 2) for (let k = -20; k <= 20; k++) { const off = k / 40 * step; let err = 0; for (const h of hits) { const q = (h.t - t0 - off) / step; err += (q - Math.round(q)) ** 2; } if (err < bestErr) { bestErr = err; best = off; } }
    const grid = { kick: new Map(), snare: new Map(), hat: new Map() }; let dropped = 0;
    for (const h of hits) { const k = Math.round((h.t - t0 - best) / step); if (k < 0 || k >= nSteps) { dropped++; continue; } const g = grid[h.part]; if (!g.has(k) || g.get(k).amp < h.amp) g.set(k, h); }
    return { grid, dropped };
  }
  function bbApply(hits, t0, bars, source) {
    const want = bars * 16; if (want > pat.steps) setSteps(want); const n = pat.steps; const { grid, dropped } = bbQuantize(hits, pat.bpm, t0, Math.min(n, want));
    const counts = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(grid).map(([k, g]) => [k, g.size])); if (!counts.kick && !counts.snare && !counts.hat) { bbMsg('I couldn’t hear any hits. Get closer to the mic and make short, punchy sounds.'); return; }
    snap(); const bank = pat.cur;
    for (const part of ['kick', 'snare', 'hat']) {
      const g = grid[part]; if (!g.size) continue; let t = pat.tracks.find((x) => partOf(x) === part);
      if (!t) { if (pat.tracks.length >= SEQ_MAX_TRACKS) continue; t = newTrack(BB_SLUG[part], n, part === 'hat' ? { choke: 1, vol: 0.6 } : {}); pat.tracks.push(t); seqLoad(t.slug); }
      const max = Math.max(...[...g.values()].map((h) => h.amp)); const s = Array(n).fill('.'); const p = {};
      for (const [k, h] of g) { const v = h.amp / max; s[k] = v > 0.8 ? 'X' : 'x'; if (v <= 0.8) p[k] = { vel: Math.round(Math.max(30, Math.min(95, 35 + v * 60))) }; }
      t.pats[bank] = { s: s.join(''), p }; t.len = n;
    }
    seqPersist(); SEQ.bbOpen = false; renderSeq();
    aiNote(`Beatbox ${source}: heard ${counts.kick} kick${counts.kick === 1 ? '' : 's'}, ${counts.snare} snare${counts.snare === 1 ? '' : 's'} and ${counts.hat} hi-hat${counts.hat === 1 ? '' : 's'}${dropped ? ` (${dropped} outside the ${bars}-bar window)` : ''}. Written into pattern ${bank}. Undo goes back.`); seqPlay();
  }
  const mono = (buf) => { const n = buf.length; const out = new Float32Array(n); for (let c = 0; c < buf.numberOfChannels; c++) { const d = buf.getChannelData(c); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out[i] += d[i] / buf.numberOfChannels; } return out; };
  async function bbFromFile(file) {
    bbMsg('Listening to your recording…');
    try {
      const buf = await new Promise((res, rej) => file.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => ac().decodeAudioData(ab, res, rej)).catch(rej)); const x = mono(buf); const hits = bbOnsets(x, buf.sampleRate);
      if (!hits.length) { bbMsg('I couldn’t hear any hits in that file.'); return; }
      const step = 60 / pat.bpm / 4; const span = hits[hits.length - 1].t - hits[0].t; const bars = span > step * 16 - step / 2 ? 2 : 1;
      bbApply(hits, hits[0].t, bars, 'from your file');
    } catch { bbMsg('That file couldn’t be read. Try an audio file like .m4a, .mp3 or .wav.'); }
  }
  async function bbRecord() {
    if (BB.stop) { BB.stop(); return; }
    const bars = +($('#bb-bars')?.value || 1); const c = ac(); let stream;
    try { stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: { echoCancellation: false, noiseSuppression: false, autoGainControl: false } }); }
    catch { bbMsg('The microphone isn’t available here (the browser or page blocked it). Record a voice memo on your phone and use “Upload a recording” instead.'); return; }
    seqStop(); const sr = c.sampleRate; const src = c.createMediaStreamSource(stream); const proc = c.createScriptProcessor(4096, 1, 1); const mute = c.createGain(); mute.gain.value = 0;
    const chunks = []; const capStart = c.currentTime; proc.onaudioprocess = (e) => chunks.push(new Float32Array(e.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0))); src.connect(proc); proc.connect(mute); mute.connect(c.destination);
    const beat = 60 / pat.bpm; const t1 = c.currentTime + 0.35; const recStart = t1 + beat * 4; const recEnd = recStart + beat * 4 * bars + beat * 0.5;
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const o = c.createOscillator(); const g = c.createGain(); o.frequency.value = i ? 1000 : 1500; g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t1 + i * beat); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t1 + i * beat + 0.06); o.connect(g); g.connect(c.destination); o.start(t1 + i * beat); o.stop(t1 + i * beat + 0.08); }
    const btn = () => document.querySelector('[data-seq="bbrec"]'); let done = false; let timer = 0;
    const finish = (keep) => {
      if (done) return; done = true; clearInterval(timer); BB.stop = null; try { src.disconnect(); proc.disconnect(); mute.disconnect(); } catch {} stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop()); const b = btn(); if (b) b.textContent = '🎤 Record';
      if (!keep) { bbMsg('Stopped.'); return; }
      const all = new Float32Array(chunks.reduce((a, ch) => a + ch.length, 0)); let o = 0; for (const ch of chunks) { all.set(ch, o); o += ch.length; }
      const hits = bbOnsets(all, sr); const t0 = recStart - capStart; const inWin = hits.filter((h) => h.t > t0 - beat / 4);
      if (!inWin.length) { bbMsg('I couldn’t hear any hits. Get closer to the mic and make short, punchy sounds.'); return; }
      bbApply(inWin, t0, bars, 'recording');
    };
    BB.stop = () => finish(false); const b0 = btn(); if (b0) b0.textContent = '■ Stop';
    timer = setInterval(() => { const now = c.currentTime; if (now < recStart) bbMsg(`Get ready… ${Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.floor((now - t1) / beat) + 1))}`); else if (now < recEnd) bbMsg(`Recording… bar ${Math.min(bars, Math.floor((now - recStart) / (beat * 4)) + 1)} of ${bars}. Beatbox now!`); else finish(true); }, 50);
  }
  function bbPanelHtml() {
    if (!SEQ.bbOpen) return '';
    return `<div class="bbox" id="bbox" role="region" aria-label="Beatbox it"><h3>🎤 Beatbox it</h3>
      <p class="note">Make the sounds with your mouth: <b>“b”</b> or <b>“boots”</b> for a kick, <b>“k”</b>, <b>“pf”</b> or <b>“cats”</b> for a snare, <b>“ts”</b> for a hi-hat. SoundWell hears each hit, sorts it and writes it into pattern ${pat.cur} at ${pat.bpm} BPM. ${canMic() ? 'Press Record, wait for the four count-in clicks, then go.' : ''}</p>
      <div class="seqtools"><label class="hint">Length <select id="bb-bars"><option value="1">1 bar</option><option value="2">2 bars</option></select></label>
        ${canMic() ? '<button class="btn primary" type="button" data-seq="bbrec">🎤 Record</button>' : ''}<button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="bbfile">Upload a recording</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-seq="bbclose">Close</button></div>
      <p class="hint" id="bb-status" aria-live="polite">${esc(SEQ.bbMsg || (canMic() ? '' : 'No microphone here: record a voice memo on your phone at about ' + pat.bpm + ' BPM and upload it.'))}</p><input type="file" id="bb-file" accept="audio/*,video/*" hidden></div>`;
  }

  // ---------- export: mix, stems, MIDI ----------
  const patFilename = () => (pat.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') || 'pattern');
  const patJson = () => JSON.stringify({ format: 'soundwell-pattern', ...pat }, null, 2);
  function seqRng(seed) { let a = seed >>> 0 || 1; return () => { a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0; let t = a; t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1); t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61); return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
  /** One fixed "performance" of the pattern or song, so the mix, every stem and the MIDI file agree hit for hit. */
  function seqEvents(reps) {
    const rng = seqRng([...pat.title].reduce((h, c) => Math.imul(h ^ c.charCodeAt(0), 16777619), 2166136261) ^ pat.bpm);
    const slots = pat.song ? Array.from({ length: reps }, () => [...pat.chain]).flat() : Array(reps).fill(pat.cur); const abs = {}; const ev = []; let when = 0;
    for (const bank of slots) for (let i = 0; i < pat.steps; i++) {
      const a = abs[bank] || 0; const dur = stepLen(i);
      pat.tracks.forEach((t, ti) => { if (audible(t)) for (const h of stepHits(t, bank, a, when, dur, rng, false)) ev.push({ ti, ...h, when: Math.max(0, h.when) }); });
      abs[bank] = a + 1; when += dur;
    }
    return { ev, total: when };
  }
  async function seqRender({ ev, total }, only = null, comp = true) {
    const sr = 44100; const oc = new OfflineAudioContext(2, Math.ceil(sr * (total + 2.5)), sr); const G = seqGraph(oc, comp);
    for (const e of ev) seqHit(G, pat.tracks[e.ti], e.when, only == null || e.ti === only ? e.vel : 0, e.tune);
    return oc.startRendering();
  }
  const peakOf = (audio) => { let pk = 0; for (let c = 0; c < audio.numberOfChannels; c++) { const d = audio.getChannelData(c); for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) pk = Math.max(pk, Math.abs(d[i])); } return pk; };
  const scaleBuf = (audio, g) => { if (g !== 1) for (let c = 0; c < audio.numberOfChannels; c++) { const d = audio.getChannelData(c); for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] *= g; } return audio; };
  function gmNote(t) {
    const s = bySlug[t.slug]; const ttl = s.title.toLowerCase(); const m = /^(note808|notebass|notepiano|notepluck|stab|sc-timpani|sc-hit)-(\d+)-(\d+)/.exec(s.slug);
    if (m) { const a = +m[2], b = +m[3]; const base = { note808: 36 + a, notebass: 36 + a, notepiano: 48 + 12 * a + b, notepluck: 60 + 12 * a + b, stab: 60 + a, 'sc-timpani': 36 + a, 'sc-hit': 48 + a }[m[1]]; return { ch: 0, note: base + t.pitch }; }
    const map = [[/kick/, 36], [/rim/, 37], [/snare/, 38], [/clap/, 39], [/open hi-hat|open hat/, 46], [/pedal/, 44], [/hi-hat|hat/, 42], [/floor tom/, 41], [/low tom/, 45], [/mid tom/, 47], [/high tom|tom/, 50], [/crash/, 49], [/ride/, 51], [/splash/, 55], [/cowbell/, 56], [/shaker/, 70], [/tambourine/, 54], [/woodblock/, 76], [/conga/, 63], [/bongo/, 60], [/cymbal/, 49]];
    for (const [re, n] of map) if (re.test(ttl)) return { ch: 9, note: n }; return { ch: 0, note: 60 + t.pitch };
  }
  function midiFile({ ev }) {
    const PPQ = 96; const beat = 60 / pat.bpm; const u32 = (n) => [n >>> 24 & 255, n >>> 16 & 255, n >>> 8 & 255, n & 255]; const u16 = (n) => [n >> 8 & 255, n & 255];
    const vlq = (n) => { const o = [n & 127]; while ((n >>>= 7)) o.unshift((n & 127) | 128); return o; }; const chunk = (id, d) => [...[...id].map((c) => c.charCodeAt(0)), ...u32(d.length), ...d];
    const tempo = Math.round(60000000 / pat.bpm); const head = [0, 0xFF, 0x51, 3, tempo >> 16 & 255, tempo >> 8 & 255, tempo & 255, 0, 0xFF, 0x58, 4, 4, 2, 24, 8, 0, 0xFF, 0x2F, 0];
    const trs = pat.tracks.map((t, ti) => {
      const { ch, note } = gmNote(t); const msgs = [];
      for (const e of ev) if (e.ti === ti) { const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(127, note + e.tune)); const tk = Math.round(e.when / beat * PPQ); const ln = Math.max(6, Math.round(e.len / beat * PPQ * (ch === 9 ? 0.5 : 0.95))); msgs.push([tk, 0x90 | ch, n, Math.max(1, Math.min(127, Math.round(e.vel * 127)))], [tk + ln, 0x80 | ch, n, 0]); }
      msgs.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1] - b[1]); const name = [...enc(bySlug[t.slug].title)].slice(0, 60); const d = [0, 0xFF, 0x03, ...vlq(name.length), ...name]; let last = 0;
      for (const [tk, st, n, v] of msgs) { d.push(...vlq(tk - last), st, n, v); last = tk; } d.push(0, 0xFF, 0x2F, 0); return chunk('MTrk', d);
    });
    return new Uint8Array([...chunk('MThd', [...u16(1), ...u16(trs.length + 1), ...u16(PPQ)]), ...chunk('MTrk', head), ...trs.flat()]);
  }
  async function seqExport() {
    if (!pat.tracks.length) { toast('Add a track first.'); return; } const reps = +$('#seq-reps').value || 2; const kind = $('#seq-exp').value; const btn = document.querySelector('[data-seq="export"]'); btn.disabled = true;
    const name = patFilename(); const names = pat.tracks.map((t) => `- ${bySlug[t.slug].title}`).join('\n');
    const license = enc(`${pat.title}: ${pat.song ? `a song (${pat.chain})` : 'a pattern'} made with SoundWell, ${pat.bpm} BPM\n\nSounds:\n${names}\n\nEvery sound is CC0 1.0 (public domain). Use this audio for anything, commercial work included. No credit required.\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\n`);
    try {
      const bufs = await Promise.all(pat.tracks.map((t) => getBuf(bySlug[t.slug]))); pat.tracks.forEach((t, i) => seqBufs.set(t.slug, bufs[i]));
      const perf = seqEvents(reps); const files = [];
      if (kind === 'midi') { files.push({ name: `${name}.mid`, data: midiFile(perf) }); toast('Building the MIDI file…'); }
      else {
        toast(kind === 'stems' ? 'Rendering the mix and stems…' : 'Rendering…');
        const mix = await seqRender(perf); const pk = peakOf(mix); scaleBuf(mix, pk > 0.95 ? 0.95 / pk : 1); files.push({ name: `${name}.wav`, data: toWav(mix) });
        if (kind === 'stems') {
          const dry = await seqRender(perf, null, false); const g = Math.min(1, 0.95 / (peakOf(dry) || 1));
          for (let i = 0; i < pat.tracks.length; i++) { if (!audible(pat.tracks[i])) continue; const st = scaleBuf(await seqRender(perf, i, false), g); const tn = bySlug[pat.tracks[i].slug].title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ''); files.push({ name: `stems/${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${tn}.wav`, data: toWav(st) }); }
        }
      }
      files.push({ name: `${name}.json`, data: enc(patJson()) }, { name: 'LICENSE.txt', data: license });
      await saveZip(`${name}${kind === 'stems' ? '-stems' : kind === 'midi' ? '-midi' : ''}.zip`, files);
    } catch { toast('Couldn’t export. Try fewer passes.'); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  async function seqSaveFile() {
    if (!dlCap) { toast('Saving files isn’t available in this view.'); return; }
    try { await dlCap.save({ filename: `${patFilename()}.json`, data: patJson() }); toast(`Saved ${patFilename()}.json`); } catch (e) { if (e && e.code !== 'declined') toast('Couldn’t save the file in this view.'); }
  }
  function seqLoadPattern(p2, msg) { seqStop(); snap(); pat = p2; seqPersist(); SEQ.menu = -1; renderSeq(); if (msg) toast(msg); }
  function seqOpenFile(file) {
    const rd = new FileReader(); rd.onload = () => { let p2 = null; try { p2 = cleanPattern(JSON.parse(String(rd.result))); } catch {} if (!p2 || !p2.tracks.length) { toast('That file isn’t a SoundWell pattern with sounds from this library.'); return; } seqLoadPattern(p2, `Opened “${p2.title}”`); }; rd.readAsText(file);
  }
  function seqSaveLocal() { const list = store.get(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, []).filter((p2) => p2.title !== pat.title); list.unshift(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(pat))); store.set(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, list.slice(0, 50)); toast(`Saved “${pat.title}” in this browser`); renderSeq(); }

  // ---------- community patterns (shared database on claude.ai) ----------
  let dbCap = null, userCap = null, myId = null, canWrite = null, amOwner = false, community = [], commUnsub = null, commState = 'idle', commNames = {};
  Promise.resolve(window.claude && typeof window.claude.use === 'function' ? Promise.all([window.claude.use('db'), window.claude.use('user')]) : [null, null]).then(async ([d, u]) => {
    dbCap = d; userCap = u;
    if (u) { try { myId = await u.id(); canWrite = await u.can('data.write'); amOwner = await u.isOwner(); } catch {} }
    if (state.view === 'seq') renderSeq();
  }).catch(() => {});
  function subscribeCommunity() {
    if (!dbCap || commUnsub) return; commState = 'loading'; const timer = setTimeout(() => { if (commState === 'loading') { commState = 'slow'; renderCommunity(); } }, 10000);
    commUnsub = dbCap.collection('patterns').limit(300).onSnapshot((snap2) => {
      if (!snap2.metadata.fromCache) { clearTimeout(timer); commState = 'ready'; }
      community = snap2.docs.flatMap((d) => { const items = d.data()?.items; return Array.isArray(items) ? items.map((it) => ({ ...it, uid: d.id })) : []; })
        .map((it) => { const c = cleanPattern(it, { allowCustom: false }); return c && c.tracks.length ? { ...c, id: String(it.id || ''), at: Number(it.at) || 0, uid: it.uid } : null; }).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
      renderCommunity(); resolveNames();
    }, () => { clearTimeout(timer); commState = 'error'; renderCommunity(); });
  }
  async function resolveNames() { if (!userCap) return; const ids = [...new Set(community.map((c) => c.uid))].filter((id) => !(id in commNames)); if (!ids.length) return; try { const ps = await userCap.profiles(ids); for (const id of ids) commNames[id] = (ps && ps[id] && ps[id].name) || ''; renderCommunity(); } catch {} }
  function renderCommunity() {
    const list = $('#comm-list'), note = $('#comm-note'); if (!list || !dbCap) return; subscribeCommunity();
    const share = $('#seq-share'); if (share) { share.hidden = canWrite === false || !myId; }
    note.textContent = commState === 'error' ? 'The community board can’t be reached right now.' : commState === 'slow' ? 'Still connecting to the community board…' : commState !== 'ready' ? 'Loading…'
      : community.length ? `${community.length} shared pattern${community.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}. Load one to play it or remix it.` : canWrite === false ? 'No shared patterns yet. You can browse and play patterns others share.' : 'No shared patterns yet. Be the first: press “Share this pattern”.';
    if (canWrite === false && commState === 'ready') note.textContent += ' Sharing is open to people with Contributor access.';
    list.innerHTML = community.slice(0, 100).map((c) => { const mine = c.uid === myId; const by = mine ? 'you' : (commNames[c.uid] || 'a SoundWell member'); const banks = BANKS.filter((b) => c.tracks.some((t) => t.pats[b] && /[xX]/.test(t.pats[b].s))).length;
      return `<li><span class="pt">${esc(c.title)}</span><span class="pm">by ${esc(by)} · ${c.bpm} BPM · ${c.tracks.length} tracks${banks > 1 ? ` · ${banks} patterns` : ''}${c.song ? ' · song' : ''}</span><span class="pa"><button class="btn" type="button" data-comm="load" data-uid="${esc(c.uid)}" data-id="${esc(c.id)}">Load</button>${mine || amOwner ? `<button class="btn" type="button" data-comm="del" data-uid="${esc(c.uid)}" data-id="${esc(c.id)}" aria-label="Remove ${esc(c.title)}">Remove</button>` : ''}</span></li>`; }).join('');
  }
  async function shareCommunity() {
    if (!dbCap || !myId) { toast('Sharing works when SoundWell is opened on claude.ai.'); return; }
    const clean = cleanPattern(pat, { allowCustom: false }); if (!clean.tracks.length) { toast('Add some library sounds first.'); return; }
    if (clean.tracks.length < pat.tracks.length) { toast('Your own creations stay in your browser, so those tracks aren’t shared.'); }
    const btn = $('#seq-share'); btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      const ref = dbCap.doc('patterns/' + myId); const cur = await ref.get(); let items = cur.exists && Array.isArray(cur.data().items) ? cur.data().items.slice() : [];
      const id = 'p' + Date.now().toString(36); items.unshift({ id, at: Date.now(), ...clean }); items = items.slice(0, 40); while (items.length > 1 && JSON.stringify(items).length > 700000) items.pop();
      await ref.set({ items }); toast(`Shared “${clean.title}” with the community`);
    } catch (e) { toast(e && e.code === 'quota_exceeded' ? 'The community board is full right now.' : e && e.code === 'invalid_argument' ? 'Your access level can browse but not share patterns.' : 'Couldn’t share the pattern. Try again.'); if (e && e.code === 'invalid_argument') { canWrite = false; renderCommunity(); } }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  async function removeCommunity(uid, id) {
    try { const ref = dbCap.doc('patterns/' + uid); const cur = await ref.get(); if (!cur.exists) return; const items = (cur.data().items || []).filter((it) => String(it.id) !== id); await ref.set({ items }); toast('Removed from the community board'); }
    catch { toast('Couldn’t remove it. Only the person who shared it, or the page owner, can.'); }
  }

  // sequencer events
  const seqview = $('#seqview');
  const trackOf = (el) => { const tr = el.closest('[data-t]'); return tr ? [pat.tracks[+tr.dataset.t], +tr.dataset.t] : [null, -1]; };
  seqview.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const st = e.target.closest('[data-step]');
    if (st) {
      const [t] = trackOf(st); const k = +st.dataset.step; const L = laneOf(t); const lane = SEQ.lane;
      if (lane === 'steps') { if (e.detail === 0) stepCycle(st, t, k); return; }
      if (L.s[k] === '.') { toast('Turn the step on in the Steps view first.'); return; }
      if (lane === 'cond') { snapSoon(); const i = CONDS.indexOf(laneVal(t, k, 'cond')); setLane(t, k, 'cond', CONDS[(i + (e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1) + CONDS.length) % CONDS.length]); seqCell(st, t, k); seqPersist(); return; }
      if (e.detail === 0) { snapSoon(); const d = LANES[lane]; let v = laneVal(t, k, lane) + d.step * (lane === 'rep' ? 1 : 4); if (v > d.max) v = d.min; setLane(t, k, lane, v); seqCell(st, t, k); seqPersist(); }
      return;
    }
    const ln = e.target.closest('[data-lane]'); if (ln) { SEQ.lane = ln.dataset.lane; renderSeq(); return; }
    const bk = e.target.closest('[data-bank]'); if (bk) { const b = bk.dataset.bank; if (SEQ.playing && !pat.song) { SEQ.queued = b === SEQ.bank ? null : b; renderSeq(); } else { pat.cur = b; seqPersist(); renderSeq(); } return; }
    const pv = e.target.closest('[data-preview]'); if (pv) { seqPreview(pv.dataset.preview); return; }
    const pk = e.target.closest('[data-pickslug]'); if (pk) { seqApplyPick(pk.dataset.pickslug); return; }
    const cm = e.target.closest('[data-comm]'); if (cm) { const c = community.find((x) => x.uid === cm.dataset.uid && x.id === cm.dataset.id); if (!c) return; if (cm.dataset.comm === 'load') { seqLoadPattern(cleanPattern(c), `Loaded “${c.title}”`); seqPlay(); } else removeCommunity(c.uid, c.id); return; }
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-seq]'); if (!b || b.tagName === 'INPUT' || b.tagName === 'SELECT') return; const a = b.dataset.seq; const [t, ti] = trackOf(b);
    const edit = (fn) => { snap(); fn(); seqPersist(); renderSeq(); };
    if (a === 'play') SEQ.playing ? seqStop() : seqPlay();
    else if (a === 'pick') seqPick(ti);
    else if (a === 'add') seqPick(pat.tracks.length);
    else if (a === 'pickclose') { pvStop(); SEQ.picker = null; $('#seqpicker').hidden = true; }
    else if (a === 'mute' || a === 'solo') edit(() => { t[a] = !t[a]; });
    else if (a === 'swap') seqSwap(ti, e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
    else if (a === 'more') { SEQ.menu = SEQ.menu === ti ? -1 : ti; renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'rm') edit(() => { pat.tracks.splice(ti, 1); SEQ.menu = -1; });
    else if (a === 'dup') { if (pat.tracks.length < SEQ_MAX_TRACKS) edit(() => { pat.tracks.splice(ti + 1, 0, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t))); }); }
    else if (a === 'clrrow') edit(() => setRow(t, ''));
    else if (a === 'shl' || a === 'shr') edit(() => shiftRow(t, a === 'shr' ? 1 : -1));
    else if (a === 'rand') edit(() => randomRow(t));
    else if (a === 'euclid') { const tr = b.closest('[data-t]'); const k = +tr.querySelector('[data-seq="eu-k"]').value; const r = +tr.querySelector('[data-seq="eu-r"]').value; edit(() => setRow(t, euclid(k, Math.min(t.len, pat.steps), r))); }
    else if (a === 'copy') { SEQ.clip = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(laneOf(t))); toast('Copied the steps. Paste them into any track or pattern.'); renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'paste' && SEQ.clip) edit(() => { const L = laneOf(t); L.s = (SEQ.clip.s + blankPat(pat.steps)).slice(0, pat.steps); L.p = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(SEQ.clip.p)); });
    else if (a === 'clrbank') edit(() => { for (const x of pat.tracks) delete x.pats[pat.cur]; });
    else if (a === 'song') edit(() => { pat.song = !pat.song; if (SEQ.playing) { seqStop(); seqPlay(); } });
    else if (a === 'rec') { SEQ.rec = !SEQ.rec; if (SEQ.rec && !SEQ.playing) seqPlay(); renderSeq(); if (SEQ.rec) toast('Recording: play keys 1–8 in time. Hits snap to the nearest step.'); }
    else if (a === 'fill') { SEQ.fill = !SEQ.fill; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(SEQ.fill)); }
    else if (a === 'human') humanize();
    else if (a === 'describe') describeBeat();
    else if (a === 'bsec') makeBSection();
    else if (a === 'fill2') addFill();
    else if (a === 'bbopen') { SEQ.bbOpen = !SEQ.bbOpen; SEQ.bbMsg = ''; if (!SEQ.bbOpen && BB.stop) BB.stop(); renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'bbclose') { if (BB.stop) BB.stop(); SEQ.bbOpen = false; renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'bbrec') bbRecord();
    else if (a === 'bbfile') $('#bb-file').click();
    else if (a === 'tap') { const now = performance.now(); SEQ.taps = SEQ.taps.filter((x) => now - x < 2500).concat(now); if (SEQ.taps.length > 1) { const iv = (SEQ.taps.at(-1) - SEQ.taps[0]) / (SEQ.taps.length - 1); snapSoon(); pat.bpm = num(60000 / iv, 60, 200, pat.bpm); $('#seq-bpm').value = pat.bpm; seqPersist(); } }
    else if (a === 'undo' || a === 'redo') seqUndo(a === 'redo');
    else if (a === 'new') { seqLoadPattern(defaultPattern()); pat.title = 'New pattern'; renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'save') seqSaveLocal();
    else if (a === 'file') seqSaveFile();
    else if (a === 'open') $('#seq-file').click();
    else if (a === 'export') seqExport();
    else if (a === 'share') shareCommunity();
    else if (a === 'loadmine') { const p2 = cleanPattern(store.get(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, [])[+b.dataset.i]); if (p2) seqLoadPattern(p2, `Loaded “${p2.title}”`); }
    else if (a === 'delmine') { const list = store.get(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, []); list.splice(+b.dataset.i, 1); store.set(SEQ_SAVED_KEY, list); renderSeq(); }
  });
  // Steps view: a tap cycles off → hit → accent → off; dragging across a row paints hits (or erases, if the drag starts on a hit).
  function stepCycle(btn, t, k) { snapSoon(); const L = laneOf(t); const nxt = { '.': 'x', x: 'X', X: '.' }[L.s[k]]; L.s = L.s.slice(0, k) + nxt + L.s.slice(k + 1); if (nxt === '.') delete L.p[k]; seqCell(btn, t, k); seqPersist(); if (nxt !== '.' && !SEQ.playing) audition(t, (L.p[k] || {}).tune || 0, nxt === 'X' ? 1 : 0.62); }
  let stepPaint = null;
  function paintCell(btn) {
    const t = pat.tracks[stepPaint.ti]; const k = +btn.dataset.step; if (stepPaint.done.has(k)) return; stepPaint.done.add(k); const L = laneOf(t); const c = L.s[k];
    if (stepPaint.on && c === '.') { L.s = L.s.slice(0, k) + 'x' + L.s.slice(k + 1); if (!SEQ.playing) audition(t, 0, 0.62); }
    else if (!stepPaint.on && c !== '.') { L.s = L.s.slice(0, k) + '.' + L.s.slice(k + 1); delete L.p[k]; } else return;
    seqCell(btn, t, k);
  }
  seqview.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('.steps:not(.lanemode) [data-step]'); if (!btn || e.button) return; if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') e.preventDefault();
    const [t, ti] = trackOf(btn); stepPaint = { ti, first: btn, on: laneOf(t).s[+btn.dataset.step] === '.', done: new Set(), moved: false, id: e.pointerId };
    try { btn.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch {}
  });
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    if (!stepPaint || e.pointerId !== stepPaint.id) return;
    // follow the finger along the row it started on, even if it drifts up or down a little
    let el = null, best = Infinity; for (const b of stepPaint.first.parentElement.children) { const r = b.getBoundingClientRect(); if (e.clientX < r.left - 2 || e.clientX > r.right + 2) continue; const d = Math.abs(r.top + r.height / 2 - e.clientY); if (d < best) { best = d; el = b; } }
    if (!el || el === stepPaint.first && !stepPaint.moved) return;
    if (!stepPaint.moved) { stepPaint.moved = true; snap(); paintCell(stepPaint.first); } paintCell(el);
  });
  const paintEnd = (e) => { if (!stepPaint || e.pointerId !== stepPaint.id) return; const p2 = stepPaint; stepPaint = null;
    if (p2.moved) { seqPersist(); return; } if (e.type === 'pointerup') { const [t] = trackOf(p2.first); if (t) stepCycle(p2.first, t, +p2.first.dataset.step); } };
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', paintEnd); window.addEventListener('pointercancel', paintEnd);
  // drag on a step to draw velocity, chance, repeat, nudge or pitch (paints across steps)
  let laneDrag = null;
  const laneAt = (x, y) => { const el = document.elementFromPoint(x, y)?.closest('#seqview .steps.lanemode [data-step]'); return el || null; };
  function laneFromPointer(el, y) { const [t] = trackOf(el); const k = +el.dataset.step; const lane = SEQ.lane; const d = LANES[lane]; if (!d.min && d.min !== 0 || laneOf(t).s[k] === '.') return; const r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); const f = 1 - Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (y - r.top) / r.height)); setLane(t, k, lane, d.min + f * (d.max - d.min)); seqCell(el, t, k); }
  seqview.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => { const el = e.target.closest('.steps.lanemode [data-step]'); if (!el || SEQ.lane === 'cond' || e.button) return; e.preventDefault(); snap(); laneDrag = true; laneFromPointer(el, e.clientY); });
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => { if (!laneDrag) return; const el = laneAt(e.clientX, e.clientY); if (el) laneFromPointer(el, e.clientY); });
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { if (laneDrag) { laneDrag = null; seqPersist(); } });
  seqview.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; const a = el.dataset.seq; const [t] = trackOf(el);
    if (el.id === 'seq-desc') { SEQ.desc = el.value; return; }
    if (el.id === 'seq-q') { seqResults(el.value); if (PV.slug && !document.querySelector(`#seq-results [data-preview="${PV.slug}"]`)) pvStop(); return; }
    if (a === 'title') { snapSoon(); pat.title = el.value.slice(0, 60) || 'Untitled pattern'; }
    else if (a === 'bpm') { const v = Math.round(+el.value); if (v >= 60 && v <= 200) { snapSoon(); pat.bpm = v; } }
    else if (a === 'swing') { snapSoon(); pat.swing = +el.value; el.nextElementSibling.textContent = pat.swing + '%'; }
    else if (a === 'chain') { const v = el.value.toUpperCase().replace(/[^ABCD]/g, ''); if (el.value !== v) el.value = v; if (v) { snapSoon(); pat.chain = v; } }
    else if (t && (a === 'vol' || a === 'pan' || a === 'tone' || a === 'send')) {
      snapSoon(); t[a] = a === 'vol' ? el.value / 100 : +el.value; liveBus(t); const o = el.nextElementSibling;
      if (o && a === 'pan') o.textContent = t.pan === 0 ? 'C' : t.pan < 0 ? 'L' + -t.pan : 'R' + t.pan; else if (o && a === 'tone') o.textContent = t.tone === 0 ? 'Off' : t.tone < 0 ? 'Low-pass' : 'High-pass'; else if (o && a === 'send') o.textContent = t.send + '%';
    }
    else return; seqPersist();
  });
  seqview.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; const a = el.dataset.seq; const [t] = trackOf(el);
    if (el.id === 'seq-file' && el.files[0]) { seqOpenFile(el.files[0]); el.value = ''; return; }
    if (el.id === 'bb-file' && el.files[0]) { bbFromFile(el.files[0]); el.value = ''; return; }
    if (a === 'pitch' && t) { snap(); t.pitch = +el.value; seqPersist(); audition(t); }
    else if ((a === 'choke' || a === 'len') && t) { snap(); t[a] = +el.value; seqPersist(); renderSeq(); }
    else if (a === 'steps') setSteps(+el.value);
    else if (a === 'bpm') { el.value = pat.bpm; }
    else if (a === 'chain') { el.value = pat.chain; }
    else if (a === 'tpl' && el.value !== '') { const tp = TEMPLATES[+el.value]; seqLoadPattern(templatePattern(tp), `Loaded the ${tp.name} template. Undo goes back to your pattern.`); seqPlay(); }
    else if (a === 'copyto' && el.value) { const to = el.value; snap(); for (const x of pat.tracks) x.pats[to] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(laneOf(x))); seqPersist(); toast(`Copied pattern ${pat.cur} to ${to}`); renderSeq(); }
  });
  seqview.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.target.id === 'seq-desc' && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); if (!SEQ.aiBusy) describeBeat(); return; }
    if (e.target.id === 'seq-q' && e.key === 'Enter') { const first = $('#seq-results [data-pickslug]'); if (first) seqApplyPick(first.dataset.pickslug); return; }
    const st = e.target.closest?.('.steps.lanemode [data-step]');
    if (st && (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') && SEQ.lane !== 'cond') { e.preventDefault(); const [t] = trackOf(st); const k = +st.dataset.step; if (laneOf(t).s[k] === '.') return; snapSoon(); setLane(t, k, SEQ.lane, laneVal(t, k, SEQ.lane) + LANES[SEQ.lane].step * (e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? 1 : -1)); seqCell(st, t, k); seqPersist(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (state.view !== 'seq' || e.altKey) return; const typing = /^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement?.tagName) && document.activeElement.type !== 'range'; if (typing) return;
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z' || e.key === 'y')) { e.preventDefault(); seqUndo(e.key === 'y' || e.shiftKey); return; }
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
    const d = /^Digit([1-8])$/.exec(e.code); if (d && !e.repeat) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); seqPad(+d[1] - 1, e.shiftKey); return; }
    if (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); if (!e.repeat) (SEQ.playing ? seqStop() : seqPlay()); return; }
    if ((e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') && !e.repeat) { SEQ.fill = !SEQ.fill; document.querySelector('[data-seq="fill"]')?.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(SEQ.fill)); }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { if (state.view === 'seq' && (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') && !/^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement?.tagName)) e.preventDefault(); }, true);

  // ---------- sound effects for video: place sounds on a video's timeline, preview in sync, export aligned audio ----------
  // The video never leaves the device: it plays from a local object URL, and only the sound placements are saved.
  const VID_KEY = 'vid-project';
  const VID = { file: null, url: '', name: '', size: 0, dur: 0, cues: [], sel: -1, playing: false, nodes: [], zoom: 1, orig: 0.8, picker: false, pickAt: 0, replace: false, raf: 0, drag: null };
  const VID_CHIPS = ['whoosh', 'punch', 'impact', 'door', 'footsteps', 'glitch', 'pop', 'ding', 'boom', 'laugh', 'swoosh', 'riser'];
  const vidEl = () => $('#vid-video');
  const fmtT = (t) => { t = Math.max(0, t || 0); const m = Math.floor(t / 60); return `${m}:${(t - m * 60).toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`; };
  const cleanCue = (c) => ({ id: String(c.id || 'c' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)), slug: c.slug, t: Math.max(0, Math.min(1e5, Number(c.t) || 0)), vol: Math.max(0, Math.min(1.5, Number.isFinite(+c.vol) ? +c.vol : 1)) });
  const cueDur = (c) => { const s = bySlug[c.slug]; return s ? s.dur : 0.5; };
  function vidSave() { store.set(VID_KEY, { name: VID.name, size: VID.size, cues: VID.cues }); }
  const vidBufs = new Map();
  function vidLoad(slug) { const s = bySlug[slug]; if (!s || vidBufs.has(slug)) return; vidBufs.set(slug, null); getBuf(s).then((b) => vidBufs.set(slug, b)).catch(() => { vidBufs.delete(slug); toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); }); }
  function vidOpenFile(file) {
    if (!file) return; if (!/^video\//.test(file.type) && !/\.(mp4|mov|m4v|webm|ogv|mkv)$/i.test(file.name)) { toast('That isn’t a video file. Try .mp4, .mov or .webm.'); return; }
    vidPause(); if (VID.url) URL.revokeObjectURL(VID.url);
    Object.assign(VID, { file, url: URL.createObjectURL(file), name: file.name, size: file.size, dur: 0, cues: [], sel: -1, picker: false });
    const prev = store.get(VID_KEY, null);
    if (prev && prev.name === file.name && prev.size === file.size && Array.isArray(prev.cues)) { VID.cues = prev.cues.filter((c) => c && bySlug[c.slug]).map(cleanCue); if (VID.cues.length) toast(`Restored your ${VID.cues.length} sound${VID.cues.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} for this video.`); }
    for (const c of VID.cues) vidLoad(c.slug); renderVid();
  }
  // playback: the video plays itself; sounds are scheduled on the audio clock from the video's position
  function vidStopNodes() { for (const n of VID.nodes) { try { n.stop(); } catch {} } VID.nodes = []; }
  function vidSchedule() {
    vidStopNodes(); const v = vidEl(); if (!v || v.paused) return; const c = ac(); const now = v.currentTime; const base = c.currentTime + 0.03;
    for (const cue of VID.cues) {
      const buf = vidBufs.get(cue.slug); if (!buf) { vidLoad(cue.slug); continue; } const off = now - cue.t; if (off >= buf.duration) continue;
      const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = cue.vol; src.connect(g); g.connect(c.destination);
      if (off >= 0) src.start(base, off); else src.start(base - off); VID.nodes.push(src);
    }
  }
  function vidPlay() { const v = vidEl(); if (!v) return; ac(); if (P.playing) pPause(); if (SEQ.playing) seqStop(); pvStop(); v.volume = VID.orig; if (v.ended || v.currentTime >= VID.dur - 0.02) v.currentTime = 0; v.play().then(() => { VID.playing = true; vidSchedule(); vidSyncPlay(); vidTick(); }).catch(() => toast('This video can’t play in your browser. Try an .mp4 file.')); }
  function vidPause() { const v = vidEl(); VID.playing = false; vidStopNodes(); cancelAnimationFrame(VID.raf); if (v && !v.paused) v.pause(); vidSyncPlay(); }
  function vidSyncPlay() { for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-v="play"]')) { b.setAttribute('aria-label', VID.playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'); b.innerHTML = `<svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${VID.playing ? 'pause' : 'play'}"/></svg><span>${VID.playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}</span>`; } }
  function vidTick() { const v = vidEl(); if (!v) return; const ph = $('#vid-ph'); if (ph && VID.dur) ph.style.left = (v.currentTime / VID.dur * 100) + '%'; const tt = $('#vid-time'); if (tt) tt.textContent = `${fmtT(v.currentTime)} / ${fmtT(VID.dur)}`; if (VID.playing) VID.raf = requestAnimationFrame(vidTick); }
  function vidSeek(t) { const v = vidEl(); if (!v) return; v.currentTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(VID.dur, t)); vidTick(); if (VID.playing) setTimeout(vidSchedule, 30); }

  function vidLanes() {
    const minSec = VID.dur * 0.07 / VID.zoom; const ends = []; const lane = new Map();
    for (const c of [...VID.cues].sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t)) { const end = c.t + Math.max(cueDur(c), minSec); let i = ends.findIndex((e) => e <= c.t); if (i < 0) { i = ends.length; ends.push(0); } ends[i] = end; lane.set(c.id, i); }
    return { lane, n: Math.max(1, ends.length) };
  }
  function vidTimelineHtml() {
    if (!VID.dur) return '<p class="hint">Loading the video…</p>';
    const { lane, n } = vidLanes(); const span = VID.dur / VID.zoom; const stepT = [0.5, 1, 2, 5, 10, 15, 30, 60, 120, 300].find((x) => span / x <= 10) || 600;
    const ticks = []; for (let t = 0; t <= VID.dur + 1e-6; t += stepT) ticks.push(`<span class="tick" style="left:${t / VID.dur * 100}%">${fmtT(t).replace(/\.\d+$/, '')}</span>`);
    const cues = VID.cues.map((c, i) => { const s = bySlug[c.slug]; return `<button type="button" class="cue${i === VID.sel ? ' sel' : ''}" data-cue="${i}" style="left:${c.t / VID.dur * 100}%;width:${Math.max(0.4, cueDur(c) / VID.dur * 100)}%;top:${lane.get(c.id) * 34 + 26}px" aria-label="${esc(s.title)} at ${fmtT(c.t)}. Drag to move."><span>${esc(s.title)}</span></button>`; }).join('');
    return `<div class="tlscroll"><div class="tlin" id="vid-tlin" style="width:${VID.zoom * 100}%;height:${n * 34 + 34}px"><div class="ruler">${ticks.join('')}</div>${cues}<div class="ph" id="vid-ph" style="left:${(vidEl()?.currentTime || 0) / VID.dur * 100}%"></div></div></div>`;
  }
  function vidInspectorHtml() {
    const c = VID.cues[VID.sel]; if (!c) return `<p class="hint">${VID.cues.length ? 'Select a sound on the timeline to change it. Drag sounds to move them.' : 'Play or scrub to a moment, then press “Add sound here” (or the A key).'}</p>`;
    const s = bySlug[c.slug];
    return `<div class="insp"><b>${esc(s.title)}</b><span class="hint">at ${fmtT(c.t)}</span>
      <button class="btn" type="button" data-v="nudge" data-d="-0.1" aria-label="Move 0.1 seconds earlier">−0.1s</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="nudge" data-d="-0.02" aria-label="Move one frame earlier">−1f</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="nudge" data-d="0.02" aria-label="Move one frame later">+1f</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="nudge" data-d="0.1" aria-label="Move 0.1 seconds later">+0.1s</button>
      <button class="btn" type="button" data-v="tohere">Move to playhead</button>
      <label class="hint">Volume <input type="range" min="0" max="150" value="${Math.round(c.vol * 100)}" data-v="cvol"><output>${Math.round(c.vol * 100)}%</output></label>
      <button class="btn" type="button" data-v="hear">▶ Hear it</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="replace">Replace</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="del">Delete</button></div>`;
  }
  function vidPickerHtml() {
    if (!VID.picker) return '';
    return `<div class="picker" id="vid-picker"><div class="pkhead"><label for="vid-q">${VID.replace ? 'Replace with…' : `Add a sound at ${fmtT(VID.pickAt)}`}</label><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="pickclose">Close</button></div>
      <div class="vchips">${VID_CHIPS.map((w) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-vchip="${w}">${w}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <p class="pknote">Press ▶ to hear a sound first, then tap its name to place it.</p><input id="vid-q" type="search" placeholder="Search whoosh, punch, door slam, glitch…" autocomplete="off"><ul id="vid-results"></ul></div>`;
  }
  function vidResults(q) {
    const toks = words(q || 'whoosh'); const pool = SOUNDS.filter((s) => !s.loop || s.kind === 'sfx');
    const res = pool.map((s) => [s, score(s, toks)]).filter((x) => x[1]).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 40).map((x) => x[0]);
    const box = $('#vid-results'); if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = res.length ? res.map((s) => `<li><button class="pv" type="button" data-preview="${s.slug}" aria-label="Preview ${esc(s.title)}" aria-pressed="${PV.slug === s.slug}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${PV.slug === s.slug ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg></button><button class="pick" type="button" data-vpick="${s.slug}" aria-label="Place ${esc(s.title)}"><span class="art" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})"></span><span class="pt">${esc(s.title)}</span><span class="pm">${fmtDur(s.dur)}</span><span class="use">${VID.replace ? 'Use' : 'Place'}</span></button></li>`).join('') : '<li class="pm" style="padding:6px">No matching sounds.</li>';
  }
  function vidPart(id, html) { const el = $('#' + id); if (el) el.innerHTML = html; }
  function vidRefresh() { vidPart('vid-tl', vidTimelineHtml()); vidPart('vid-insp', vidInspectorHtml()); const n = $('#vid-count'); if (n) n.textContent = `${VID.cues.length} sound${VID.cues.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`; }
  function renderVid() {
    const v = $('#vidview'); if (!v) return;
    if (v.dataset.url === VID.url && VID.url) { vidRefresh(); return; } v.dataset.url = VID.url;
    const head = `<div class="seqhead"><h2>Sound for video</h2><div class="seqtools"><button class="btn${VID.url ? '' : ' primary'}" type="button" data-v="open"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-film"/></svg>${VID.url ? 'Open another video' : 'Open a video'}</button>
      ${VID.url ? '<button class="btn dl" type="button" data-v="saveproj">Save project</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="openproj">Open project</button>' : ''}</div></div>
      <input type="file" id="vid-file" accept="video/*,.mp4,.mov,.m4v,.webm" hidden><input type="file" id="vid-projfile" accept=".json,application/json" hidden>`;
    if (!VID.url) {
      v.innerHTML = head + `<div class="vidempty" id="vid-drop"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-film"/></svg><h3>Add sound effects to a video</h3>
        <p>Open a clip, tap where things happen (a punch, a door, a swipe), pick sounds from the library and export audio that lines up with your video. Drop the WAV into CapCut, iMovie, Premiere or any editor at 0:00.</p>
        <p class="hint">Your video stays on your device. Nothing is uploaded.</p><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-v="open">Open a video</button></div>`;
      return;
    }
    v.innerHTML = head + `<div class="vidwrap"><video id="vid-video" src="${VID.url}" playsinline preload="auto"></video></div>
      <div class="seqbar vidbar"><button class="seqplay withlabel" type="button" data-v="play"></button><span class="vtime" id="vid-time">0:00.00 / 0:00.00</span>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-v="add"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg>Add sound here</button>
        <label>Video's own sound <input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(VID.orig * 100)}" data-v="orig"></label>
        <label>Zoom <input type="range" min="1" max="8" step="0.5" value="${VID.zoom}" data-v="zoom"></label><span class="hint" id="vid-count"></span></div>
      <div class="timeline" id="vid-tl"></div><div id="vid-insp" class="seqbar vidinsp"></div><div id="vid-pickwrap">${vidPickerHtml()}</div>
      <div class="seqbar export dl"><span class="lbl">Export</span><label><input type="checkbox" id="vid-incl"> Include the video's own sound</label>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-v="export"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg>Export audio</button><span class="hint">A WAV as long as your video, plus a cue sheet. Line it up at 0:00 in your editor.</span></div>`;
    const el = vidEl();
    el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => { VID.dur = el.duration || 0; VID.cues = VID.cues.filter((c) => c.t < VID.dur); vidRefresh(); vidTick(); });
    el.addEventListener('pause', () => { if (VID.playing) { VID.playing = false; vidStopNodes(); vidSyncPlay(); } });
    el.addEventListener('ended', () => { VID.playing = false; vidStopNodes(); vidSyncPlay(); vidTick(); });
