    el.addEventListener('seeked', () => { vidTick(); if (VID.playing) vidSchedule(); });
    el.addEventListener('error', () => toast('This video can’t be opened in your browser. Try an .mp4 file.'));
    el.addEventListener('click', () => (VID.playing ? vidPause() : vidPlay()));
    vidSyncPlay(); vidRefresh(); if (VID.picker) vidResults($('#vid-q')?.value);
  }
  function vidOpenPicker(replace) { const v = vidEl(); VID.replace = !!replace; VID.pickAt = v ? v.currentTime : 0; VID.picker = true; if (VID.playing) vidPause(); vidPart('vid-pickwrap', vidPickerHtml()); vidResults(''); $('#vid-q')?.focus(); $('#vid-picker')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' }); }
  function vidClosePicker() { VID.picker = false; pvStop(); vidPart('vid-pickwrap', ''); }
  function vidPick(slug) {
    pvStop(); vidLoad(slug);
    if (VID.replace && VID.cues[VID.sel]) VID.cues[VID.sel].slug = slug;
    else { VID.cues.push(cleanCue({ slug, t: VID.pickAt, vol: 1 })); VID.sel = VID.cues.length - 1; }
    vidSave(); vidClosePicker(); vidRefresh(); toast(`Placed “${bySlug[slug].title}” at ${fmtT(VID.replace ? VID.cues[VID.sel].t : VID.pickAt)}`);
  }
  async function vidExport() {
    if (!VID.cues.length) { toast('Add some sounds first.'); return; } const btn = document.querySelector('[data-v="export"]'); btn.disabled = true; toast('Rendering your soundtrack…');
    const base = (VID.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') || 'video'); const incl = $('#vid-incl')?.checked;
    try {
      const sr = 48000; const oc = new OfflineAudioContext(2, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(VID.dur * sr)), sr); let origNote = '';
      if (incl) { try { const ab = await VID.file.arrayBuffer(); const ob = await new Promise((res, rej) => ac().decodeAudioData(ab, res, rej)); const s = oc.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = ob; const g = oc.createGain(); g.gain.value = VID.orig; s.connect(g); g.connect(oc.destination); s.start(0); } catch { origNote = ' (the video’s own sound couldn’t be read, so it has your effects only)'; } }
      for (const c of VID.cues) { const buf = await getBuf(bySlug[c.slug]); const s = oc.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = buf; const g = oc.createGain(); g.gain.value = c.vol; s.connect(g); g.connect(oc.destination); s.start(c.t); }
      const audio = await oc.startRendering(); const pk = peakOf(audio); if (pk > 0.98) scaleBuf(audio, 0.98 / pk);
      const csv = 'time_seconds,timecode,sound,volume_percent,sound_slug\n' + [...VID.cues].sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t).map((c) => `${c.t.toFixed(3)},${fmtT(c.t)},"${bySlug[c.slug].title.replace(/"/g, '""')}",${Math.round(c.vol * 100)},${c.slug}`).join('\n') + '\n';
      const proj = JSON.stringify({ format: 'soundwell-video', name: VID.name, duration: VID.dur, cues: VID.cues }, null, 2);
      const lic = `Sound for ${VID.name}, made with SoundWell.\n\nPlace ${base}-sound.wav at 0:00 in your video editor. It is exactly as long as the video.\n\nEvery SoundWell sound is CC0 1.0 (public domain): use it for anything, commercial work included. No credit required.\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\n${incl ? '\nThe video’s own sound is yours and is not covered by this note.\n' : ''}`;
      await saveZip(`${base}-sound.zip`, [{ name: `${base}-sound.wav`, data: toWav(audio) }, { name: `${base}-cues.csv`, data: enc(csv) }, { name: `${base}-soundwell.json`, data: enc(proj) }, { name: 'LICENSE.txt', data: enc(lic) }]);
      if (origNote) toast('Exported' + origNote);
    } catch { toast('Couldn’t render the soundtrack. Try a shorter video.'); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  async function vidSaveProject() {
    if (!dlCap) { toast('Saving files isn’t available in this view.'); return; } const base = VID.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'video';
    try { await dlCap.save({ filename: `${base}-soundwell.json`, data: JSON.stringify({ format: 'soundwell-video', name: VID.name, duration: VID.dur, cues: VID.cues }, null, 2) }); } catch (e) { if (e && e.code !== 'declined') toast('Couldn’t save the file in this view.'); }
  }
  function vidOpenProject(file) {
    const rd = new FileReader(); rd.onload = () => { let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(String(rd.result)); } catch {} const cues = j && Array.isArray(j.cues) ? j.cues.filter((c) => c && bySlug[c.slug]).map(cleanCue) : [];
      if (!cues.length) { toast('That file isn’t a SoundWell video project.'); return; } VID.cues = cues; VID.sel = -1; for (const c of cues) vidLoad(c.slug); vidSave(); vidRefresh(); toast(`Opened ${cues.length} sound placements${j.name && j.name !== VID.name ? ` (made for ${j.name})` : ''}.`); };
    rd.readAsText(file);
  }

  // video events
  const vidview = $('#vidview');
  vidview.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const pv = e.target.closest('[data-preview]'); if (pv) { seqPreview(pv.dataset.preview); return; }
    const pk = e.target.closest('[data-vpick]'); if (pk) { vidPick(pk.dataset.vpick); return; }
    const ch = e.target.closest('[data-vchip]'); if (ch) { const q = $('#vid-q'); q.value = ch.dataset.vchip; vidResults(q.value); return; }
    const cue = e.target.closest('[data-cue]'); if (cue) return; // handled by pointer events
    const tl = e.target.closest('#vid-tlin'); if (tl && VID.dur) { const r = tl.getBoundingClientRect(); vidSeek((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * VID.dur); return; }
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-v]'); if (!b || b.tagName === 'INPUT') return; const a = b.dataset.v; const c = VID.cues[VID.sel];
    if (a === 'open') $('#vid-file').click();
    else if (a === 'play') VID.playing ? vidPause() : vidPlay();
    else if (a === 'add') vidOpenPicker(false);
    else if (a === 'pickclose') vidClosePicker();
    else if (a === 'export') vidExport();
    else if (a === 'saveproj') vidSaveProject();
    else if (a === 'openproj') $('#vid-projfile').click();
    else if (c && a === 'nudge') { c.t = Math.max(0, Math.min(VID.dur, c.t + Number(b.dataset.d))); vidSave(); vidRefresh(); vidSeek(c.t); }
    else if (c && a === 'tohere') { c.t = vidEl()?.currentTime || 0; vidSave(); vidRefresh(); }
    else if (c && a === 'hear') { const s = bySlug[c.slug]; getBuf(s).then((buf) => { const x = ac(); const src = x.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; const g = x.createGain(); g.gain.value = c.vol; src.connect(g); g.connect(x.destination); src.start(); }).catch(() => {}); }
    else if (c && a === 'replace') vidOpenPicker(true);
    else if (c && a === 'del') { VID.cues.splice(VID.sel, 1); VID.sel = -1; vidSave(); vidRefresh(); }
  });
  vidview.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
    const cue = e.target.closest('[data-cue]'); if (!cue || e.button) return; e.preventDefault(); const i = +cue.dataset.cue; const tl = $('#vid-tlin'); const r = tl.getBoundingClientRect();
    VID.sel = i; VID.drag = { i, id: e.pointerId, x0: e.clientX, t0: VID.cues[i].t, w: r.width, moved: false }; cue.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    for (const x of document.querySelectorAll('#vid-tlin .cue')) x.classList.toggle('sel', x === cue); vidPart('vid-insp', vidInspectorHtml());
  });
  vidview.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    const d = VID.drag; if (!d || e.pointerId !== d.id) return; const dx = e.clientX - d.x0; if (!d.moved && Math.abs(dx) < 4) return; d.moved = true;
    const c = VID.cues[d.i]; c.t = Math.max(0, Math.min(VID.dur - 0.01, d.t0 + dx / d.w * VID.dur)); const el = document.querySelector(`#vid-tlin [data-cue="${d.i}"]`); if (el) { el.style.left = (c.t / VID.dur * 100) + '%'; el.setAttribute('aria-label', `${bySlug[c.slug].title} at ${fmtT(c.t)}. Drag to move.`); }
  });
  const vidDragEnd = (e) => { const d = VID.drag; if (!d || e.pointerId !== d.id) return; VID.drag = null; if (d.moved) { vidSave(); vidRefresh(); if (VID.playing) vidSchedule(); } else vidSeek(VID.cues[d.i].t); };
  vidview.addEventListener('pointerup', vidDragEnd); vidview.addEventListener('pointercancel', vidDragEnd);
  vidview.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; const a = el.dataset.v;
    if (el.id === 'vid-q') { vidResults(el.value); return; }
    if (a === 'orig') { VID.orig = el.value / 100; const v = vidEl(); if (v) v.volume = VID.orig; }
    else if (a === 'zoom') { VID.zoom = +el.value; vidPart('vid-tl', vidTimelineHtml()); }
    else if (a === 'cvol') { const c = VID.cues[VID.sel]; if (c) { c.vol = el.value / 100; el.nextElementSibling.textContent = el.value + '%'; vidSave(); } }
  });
  vidview.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; if (el.id === 'vid-file' && el.files[0]) { vidOpenFile(el.files[0]); el.value = ''; } else if (el.id === 'vid-projfile' && el.files[0]) { vidOpenProject(el.files[0]); el.value = ''; }
  });
  vidview.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.target.id === 'vid-q' && e.key === 'Enter') { const first = $('#vid-results [data-vpick]'); if (first) vidPick(first.dataset.vpick); } });
  vidview.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { if (e.dataTransfer?.types?.includes('Files')) { e.preventDefault(); } });
  vidview.addEventListener('drop', (e) => { const f = e.dataTransfer?.files?.[0]; if (f) { e.preventDefault(); vidOpenFile(f); } });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (state.view !== 'video' || e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return; const typing = /^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement?.tagName) && document.activeElement.type !== 'range'; if (typing || !VID.url) return;
    if (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); if (!e.repeat) (VID.playing ? vidPause() : vidPlay()); }
    else if (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') { e.preventDefault(); vidOpenPicker(false); }
    else if ((e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') && VID.cues[VID.sel]) { e.preventDefault(); VID.cues.splice(VID.sel, 1); VID.sel = -1; vidSave(); vidRefresh(); }
    else if ((e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') && VID.cues[VID.sel]) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); const c = VID.cues[VID.sel]; c.t = Math.max(0, Math.min(VID.dur, c.t + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1) * (e.shiftKey ? 0.1 : 0.02))); vidSave(); vidRefresh(); }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { if (state.view === 'video' && (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') && !/^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement?.tagName)) e.preventDefault(); }, true);

  // ---------- game audio: engine-ready packs, and a procedural runtime that ships recipes instead of audio ----------
  const GAME_KEY = 'game-pack';
  const GAME = { name: store.get('game-pack-name', 'my-game-sounds'), items: (store.get(GAME_KEY, []) || []).map((x) => (x && x.slug ? { ...x, slug: canon(x.slug) } : x)).filter((x) => x && bySlug[x.slug]), picker: false, busy: false };
  const gameSave = () => { store.set(GAME_KEY, GAME.items); store.set('game-pack-name', GAME.name); };
  const gameId = (t) => t.toLowerCase().replace(/·/g, ' ').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '').slice(0, 40) || 'sound';
  /** The recipe behind a sound, if it can run in the procedural runtime (generated sounds, and creations made from them). */
  function gameRecipe(s) {
    if (s.g) return { g: s.g, p: s.p, m: {}, rate: 1, vol: 1, loop: !!s.loop };
    const b = s.custom && bySlug[s.custom.base]; if (b && b.g) { const o = s.custom.o || {}; return { g: b.g, p: b.p, m: modOf(s.custom.m) || {}, rate: o.speed || 1, vol: 10 ** ((o.gain || 0) / 20), loop: !!s.loop }; }
    return null;
  }
  function gameAdd(s, quiet) {
    if (GAME.items.some((x) => x.slug === s.slug)) { if (!quiet) toast(`“${s.title}” is already in your game pack.`); return false; }
    if (GAME.items.length >= 60) { toast('A game pack holds 60 sounds.'); return false; }
    let id = gameId(s.title); const used = new Set(GAME.items.map((x) => x.name)); for (let k = 2; used.has(id); k++) id = gameId(s.title) + '_' + k;
    GAME.items.push({ slug: s.slug, name: id, takes: s.loop ? 1 : 4, pitch: s.loop ? 0 : 0.03, vol: 1 }); gameSave(); if (!quiet) toast(`Added “${s.title}” to your game pack.`); return true;
  }
  /** One take of a pack item: take 0 is the sound as chosen; later takes are new renders of the recipe (or small nudges for recordings). */
  async function gameTake(s, k) {
    if (s.g) return modBuf(s, k ? { seed: 1000 + k } : null);
    const b = s.custom && bySlug[s.custom.base];
    if (b && b.g) return processBuf(b, s.custom.o, { ...(s.custom.m || {}), ...(k ? { seed: 1000 + k } : {}) });
    if (!k) return getBuf(s); const r = rng(k * 7919); return processBuf(s.custom ? bySlug[s.custom.base] : s, { ...(s.custom ? s.custom.o : defaults()), speed: (s.custom ? s.custom.o.speed : 1) * (1 + (r() - 0.5) * 0.08), gain: (r() - 0.5) * 3 });
  }
  async function gameHearTakes(i) {
    const it = GAME.items[i]; const s = bySlug[it.slug]; const c = ac(); let t = c.currentTime + 0.05; const n = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, it.takes));
    try { for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const buf = await gameTake(s, k); const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.playbackRate.value = 1 + (k ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * it.pitch : 0); const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = it.vol; src.connect(g); g.connect(c.destination); t = Math.max(t, c.currentTime + 0.05); src.start(t); t += Math.min(buf.duration, 1.2) + 0.15; } } catch { toast(`Couldn't load “${s.title}”.`); }
  }
  const fmtMB = (b) => b > 1e6 ? (b / 1e6).toFixed(1) + ' MB' : Math.max(1, Math.round(b / 1000)) + ' KB';
  function gameSizes() {
    const wav = GAME.items.reduce((a, it) => a + (bySlug[it.slug].dur * 44100 * 4 + 44) * it.takes, 0);
    const proc = GAME.items.filter((it) => gameRecipe(bySlug[it.slug])); const rt = proc.length ? SoundwellEngine.toString().length + soundwellRuntime.toString().length + 400 + proc.length * 120 : 0;
    return { wav, rt, nProc: proc.length };
  }
  function gameListHtml() {
    const { wav, rt, nProc } = gameSizes(); const n = GAME.items.length;
    const opt = (val, cur, label) => `<option value="${val}"${String(val) === String(cur) ? ' selected' : ''}>${label}</option>`;
    return `${n ? `<div class="gtable" role="table" aria-label="Sounds in your game pack"><div class="grow ghead" role="row"><span role="columnheader">Sound</span><span role="columnheader">Name in code</span><span role="columnheader">Takes</span><span role="columnheader">Pitch</span><span role="columnheader">Volume</span><span role="columnheader"><span class="sr">Actions</span></span></div>
        ${GAME.items.map((it, i) => { const s = bySlug[it.slug]; const proc = !!gameRecipe(s); return `<div class="grow" role="row" data-gi="${i}"><span class="gsound" role="cell"><span class="art" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})"></span><span><b>${esc(s.title)}</b><small>${fmtDur(s.dur)}${s.loop ? ' · loop' : ''} · ${proc ? 'recipe' : 'recording'}</small></span></span>
          <span role="cell" data-l="Name in code"><input type="text" value="${esc(it.name)}" data-g="name" aria-label="Name in code for ${esc(s.title)}" spellcheck="false"></span>
          <span role="cell" data-l="Takes"><select data-g="takes" aria-label="Takes for ${esc(s.title)}">${[1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8].map((k) => opt(k, it.takes, k)).join('')}</select></span>
          <span role="cell" data-l="Pitch spread"><select data-g="pitch" aria-label="Random pitch for ${esc(s.title)}">${[[0, 'None'], [0.02, '±2%'], [0.03, '±3%'], [0.05, '±5%'], [0.1, '±10%']].map(([a, b]) => opt(a, it.pitch, b)).join('')}</select></span>
          <span role="cell" data-l="Volume"><input type="range" min="10" max="150" value="${Math.round(it.vol * 100)}" data-g="vol" aria-label="Volume for ${esc(s.title)}"></span>
          <span class="gact" role="cell"><button class="btn" type="button" data-g="hear" title="Plays up to four takes in a row">▶ Takes</button><button class="ib" type="button" data-g="rm" aria-label="Remove ${esc(s.title)}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-x"/></svg></button></span></div>`; }).join('')}</div>
      <div class="gexport dl"><div><h3>Export</h3>
          <label class="gcheck"><input type="checkbox" id="game-wav" checked> <span><b>Audio files for any engine</b> · ${fmtMB(wav)}<small>${GAME.items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.takes, 0)} WAV takes, a manifest, Godot randomizer resources, a Unity script and Unreal steps.</small></span></label>
          <label class="gcheck"><input type="checkbox" id="game-rt" ${nProc ? 'checked' : 'disabled'}> <span><b>Procedural runtime for web games</b> · ${nProc ? `${fmtMB(rt)} (about ${fmtMB(rt * 0.3)} compressed)` : 'no recipe sounds yet'}<small>${nProc ? `soundwell.js generates ${nProc} of these sounds inside the browser, with a new take every time you ask. No audio files to download.${nProc < n ? ' Recordings aren’t included.' : ''}` : 'Add generated sounds (marked “recipe”) to use it.'}</small></span></label></div>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-g="export"${GAME.busy ? ' disabled' : ''}><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-down"/></svg>Export game pack</button></div>`
      : '<div class="vidempty"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-game"/></svg><h3>Your game pack is empty</h3><p>Add jumps, coins, footsteps, lasers, hits and menu sounds. Each one gets several takes so your game never sounds repetitive.</p></div>'}`;
  }
  function renderGame() {
    const v = $('#gameview'); if (!v) return; const { wav, rt, nProc } = gameSizes(); const n = GAME.items.length;
    const opt = (val, cur, label) => `<option value="${val}"${String(val) === String(cur) ? ' selected' : ''}>${label}</option>`;
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="seqhead"><h2>Game audio</h2><div class="seqtools"><label class="hint" for="game-name">Pack name</label><input id="game-name" type="text" maxlength="40" value="${esc(GAME.name)}" spellcheck="false"></div></div>
      <p class="gintro">Build a sound pack for your game. Every sound comes with several <b>takes</b>, so a footstep or a gunshot never repeats exactly. Export it ready for <b>Godot</b>, <b>Unity</b>, <b>Unreal</b> or the web, or ship the <b>recipes</b> instead of audio files: a small script that generates fresh takes inside your web game.</p>
      <div class="seqtools"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-g="add"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-layers"/></svg>Add sounds</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-g="addsaved">Add my saved sounds</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-g="addmine">Add Your creations</button>${n ? '<button class="btn" type="button" data-g="clear">Clear pack</button>' : ''}<span class="hint">Tip: “+ Game pack” on any sound adds it too.</span></div>
      <div id="game-pickwrap">${GAME.picker ? `<div class="picker" id="game-picker"><div class="pkhead"><label for="game-q">Add sounds to your pack</label><button class="btn" type="button" data-g="pickclose">Close</button></div><div class="vchips">${['footsteps', 'jump', 'coin', 'laser', 'explosion', 'hit', 'ui click', 'power up', 'door', 'magic'].map((w) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-gchip="${w}">${w}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="pknote">Press ▶ to hear a sound first, then tap its name to add it.</p><input id="game-q" type="search" value="${esc(GAME.q || '')}" placeholder="Search jump, coin, footsteps, laser…" autocomplete="off"><ul id="game-results"></ul></div>` : ''}</div>
      <div id="game-list">${gameListHtml()}</div>`;
    if (GAME.picker) gameResults(GAME.q || '');
  }
  function gameResults(q) {
    const toks = words(q || 'game'); const res = SOUNDS.map((s) => [s, score(s, toks)]).filter((x) => x[1]).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 40).map((x) => x[0]); const box = $('#game-results'); if (!box) return; const inPack = new Set(GAME.items.map((x) => x.slug));
    box.innerHTML = res.length ? res.map((s) => `<li><button class="pv" type="button" data-preview="${s.slug}" aria-label="Preview ${esc(s.title)}" aria-pressed="${PV.slug === s.slug}"><svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-${PV.slug === s.slug ? 'stop' : 'play'}"/></svg></button><button class="pick" type="button" data-gadd="${s.slug}" aria-label="Add ${esc(s.title)}"${inPack.has(s.slug) ? ' disabled' : ''}><span class="art" style="background-image:url(${soundArt(s)})"></span><span class="pt">${esc(s.title)}</span><span class="pm">${fmtDur(s.dur)}</span><span class="use">${inPack.has(s.slug) ? 'Added' : 'Add'}</span></button></li>`).join('') : '<li class="pm" style="padding:6px">No matching sounds.</li>';
  }

  /** The runtime shipped in soundwell.js. Kept as a real function so it is tested here; exported with toString(). */
  function soundwellRuntime(Engine, RECIPES) {
    return function createSoundWell(opts) {
      opts = opts || {};
      const ctx = opts.context || new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const out = ctx.createGain(); out.connect(opts.destination || ctx.destination);
      let worker = null; let wid = 0; const pend = new Map(); let local = null;
      try {
        const src = Engine.toString() + '\nconst E = ' + Engine.name + '();\nonmessage = function (e) { var d = e.data; try { var o = E.renderEntry(d.g, d.p, d.m); postMessage({ id: d.id, l: o.l, r: o.r }, o.r !== o.l ? [o.l.buffer, o.r.buffer] : [o.l.buffer]); } catch (err) { postMessage({ id: d.id, err: String(err) }); } };';
        worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([src], { type: 'text/javascript' })));
        worker.onmessage = function (e) { const r = pend.get(e.data.id); if (!r) return; pend.delete(e.data.id); if (e.data.err) r.rej(new Error(e.data.err)); else r.res(e.data); };
        worker.onerror = function () { worker = null; pend.forEach(function (r) { r.retry(); }); pend.clear(); };
      } catch (e) { worker = null; }
      function onMain(g, p, m) { return new Promise(function (res, rej) { setTimeout(function () { try { local = local || Engine(); res(local.renderEntry(g, p, m)); } catch (e) { rej(e); } }, 0); }); }
      function raw(g, p, m) { if (!worker) return onMain(g, p, m); return new Promise(function (res, rej) { const id = ++wid; pend.set(id, { res: res, rej: rej, retry: function () { onMain(g, p, m).then(res, rej); } }); worker.postMessage({ id: id, g: g, p: p, m: m }); }); }
      const takes = new Map(); const last = new Map(); let seedBase = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000);
      /** Render one take of a sound. seed 0 is the take chosen in SoundWell; any other number is a new take. */
      async function render(name, seed) {
        const r = RECIPES[name]; if (!r) throw new Error('SoundWell: there is no sound named "' + name + '"');
        const o = await raw(r.g, r.p, Object.assign({}, r.m, seed ? { seed: seed } : {}));
        const b = ctx.createBuffer(2, o.l.length, 44100); b.copyToChannel(o.l, 0); b.copyToChannel(o.r || o.l, 1); return b;
      }
      /** Pre-render takes (by default every sound, with the number of takes chosen in SoundWell). Call once while loading. */
      async function load(names, count) {
        names = names || Object.keys(RECIPES);
        await Promise.all(names.map(async function (name) { const k = count || RECIPES[name].takes || 3; const list = []; for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) list.push(await render(name, i === 0 ? 0 : seedBase + i)); takes.set(name, list); }));
      }
      /** Play a random take (never the same one twice in a row). Returns {stop()}; loops keep playing until stopped. */
      function play(name, o) {
        o = o || {}; const r = RECIPES[name]; const list = takes.get(name);
        if (!r) throw new Error('SoundWell: there is no sound named "' + name + '"');
        if (!list || !list.length) { load([name]); return null; }
        let i = Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length); if (list.length > 1 && i === last.get(name)) i = (i + 1) % list.length; last.set(name, i);
        const src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = list[i]; src.loop = o.loop != null ? !!o.loop : !!r.loop;
        src.playbackRate.value = (o.rate || 1) * (r.rate || 1) * (1 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (r.pitch || 0));
        const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = (o.volume != null ? o.volume : 1) * (r.vol != null ? r.vol : 1); src.connect(g);
        let node = g; if (o.pan && ctx.createStereoPanner) { const pn = ctx.createStereoPanner(); pn.pan.value = o.pan; g.connect(pn); node = pn; } node.connect(out);
        if (ctx.state !== 'running') ctx.resume(); src.start(); return { source: src, stop: function () { try { src.stop(); } catch (e) { /* already stopped */ } } };
      }
      /** Replace a sound's takes with brand-new ones, in the background. */
      async function refresh(name) { const k = (takes.get(name) || []).length || RECIPES[name].takes || 3; seedBase += 1000; const list = []; for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) list.push(await render(name, seedBase + i)); takes.set(name, list); }
      return { context: ctx, output: out, names: Object.keys(RECIPES), load: load, play: play, render: render, refresh: refresh, ready: function (name) { return takes.has(name); } };
    };
  }
  function gameRuntimeJs(items) {
    const rec = {}; for (const it of items) { const s = bySlug[it.slug]; const r = gameRecipe(s); if (!r) continue; const m = { ...r.m }; rec[it.name] = { g: r.g, p: r.p, m, loop: r.loop, takes: it.takes, pitch: it.pitch, vol: Math.round(it.vol * r.vol * 1000) / 1000, rate: r.rate, title: s.title }; }
    return `/*!\n * soundwell.js: procedural game sounds from SoundWell (https://claude.ai). Every sound is generated in the browser from its recipe.\n * Sounds: ${Object.keys(rec).join(', ')}\n * License: CC0 1.0 (public domain). Use it for anything; no credit required.\n *\n *   const sw = SoundWell();            // optional: SoundWell({ context: myAudioContext })\n *   await sw.load();                   // renders the takes (in a worker when available)\n *   sw.play('${Object.keys(rec)[0] || 'name'}');               // random take, random pitch spread; also { volume, pan, rate, loop }\n *   sw.refresh('${Object.keys(rec)[0] || 'name'}');            // brand-new takes in the background\n */\n(function (global) {\n'use strict';\nconst RECIPES = ${JSON.stringify(rec, null, 1)};\n${SoundwellEngine.toString()}\nconst createSoundWell = (${soundwellRuntime.toString()})(SoundwellEngine, RECIPES);\nglobal.SoundWell = createSoundWell;\nif (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = createSoundWell;\n})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);\n`;
  }
  function gameDemoHtml(items) {
    const names = items.filter((it) => gameRecipe(bySlug[it.slug])).map((it) => it.name);
    return `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>SoundWell runtime demo</title>
<style>body{font:16px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;margin:0;padding:24px;background:#0b1020;color:#e8ecf6}h1{margin:0 0 6px}p{color:#9aa6c2}button{font:600 15px system-ui;margin:4px;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid #2a3554;background:#141c33;color:#e8ecf6;cursor:pointer}button:hover{border-color:#3fa9ff}#s{color:#3fa9ff}</style></head>
<body><h1>soundwell.js demo</h1><p>Each click plays a different take, generated in your browser. <span id="s">Loading…</span></p><div id="b"></div>
<script src="soundwell.js"><\/script><script>
const sw = SoundWell(); const names = ${JSON.stringify(names)};
document.getElementById('b').innerHTML = names.map((n) => '<button data-n="' + n + '">' + n + '</button>').join('') + '<button id="r">New takes</button>';
sw.load().then(() => { document.getElementById('s').textContent = 'Ready. Click a sound.'; });
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { const n = e.target.dataset && e.target.dataset.n; if (n) sw.play(n); if (e.target.id === 'r') names.forEach((x) => sw.refresh(x)); });
<\/script></body></html>`;
  }
  function gameGodotTres(it, files) {
    const ext = files.map((f, i) => `[ext_resource type="AudioStream" path="res://soundwell/sounds/${it.name}/${f}" id="${i + 1}"]`).join('\n');
    const streams = files.map((_, i) => `stream_${i}/stream = ExtResource("${i + 1}")\nstream_${i}/weight = 1.0`).join('\n');
    return `[gd_resource type="AudioStreamRandomizer" load_steps=${files.length + 1} format=3]\n\n${ext}\n\n[resource]\nplayback_mode = 0\nrandom_pitch = ${(1 + it.pitch).toFixed(3)}\nrandom_volume_offset_db = ${it.pitch ? '1.0' : '0.0'}\nstreams_count = ${files.length}\n${streams}\n`;
  }
  function gameUnityCs(items) {
    const pitch = items.map((it) => `        { "${it.name}", ${it.pitch.toFixed(3)}f }`).join(',\n'); const vol = items.map((it) => `        { "${it.name}", ${it.vol.toFixed(3)}f }`).join(',\n');
    return `// SoundWell for Unity. Copy the pack's "sounds" folder to Assets/Resources/SoundWell, and this file anywhere under Assets.
// Usage:  SoundWell.Play(audioSource, "${items[0]?.name || 'name'}");   or   SoundWell.PlayAt("${items[0]?.name || 'name'}", transform.position);
// Every call picks a random take (never the same twice in a row) with a little pitch spread. Sounds are CC0 (public domain).
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

public static class SoundWell
{
    static readonly Dictionary<string, AudioClip[]> clips = new Dictionary<string, AudioClip[]>();
    static readonly Dictionary<string, int> last = new Dictionary<string, int>();
    static readonly Dictionary<string, float> pitchSpread = new Dictionary<string, float>
    {
${pitch}
    };
    static readonly Dictionary<string, float> volume = new Dictionary<string, float>
    {
${vol}
    };

    public static AudioClip Clip(string name)
    {
        if (!clips.TryGetValue(name, out var list)) { list = Resources.LoadAll<AudioClip>("SoundWell/" + name); clips[name] = list; }
        if (list.Length == 0) { Debug.LogWarning("SoundWell: no clips for " + name); return null; }
        int i = Random.Range(0, list.Length);
        if (list.Length > 1 && last.TryGetValue(name, out var prev) && prev == i) i = (i + 1) % list.Length;
        last[name] = i;
        return list[i];
    }

    public static void Play(AudioSource source, string name, float volumeScale = 1f)
    {
        var clip = Clip(name); if (clip == null) return;
        float spread = pitchSpread.TryGetValue(name, out var p) ? p : 0f;
        source.pitch = 1f + Random.Range(-spread, spread);
        source.PlayOneShot(clip, volumeScale * (volume.TryGetValue(name, out var v) ? v : 1f));
    }

    public static void PlayAt(string name, Vector3 position, float volumeScale = 1f)
    {
        var clip = Clip(name); if (clip == null) return;
        AudioSource.PlayClipAtPoint(clip, position, volumeScale * (volume.TryGetValue(name, out var v) ? v : 1f));
    }
}
`;
  }
  async function gameExport() {
    const wantWav = $('#game-wav')?.checked; const wantRt = $('#game-rt')?.checked && !$('#game-rt').disabled; if (!wantWav && !wantRt) { toast('Choose at least one export.'); return; }
    const items = GAME.items.map((it) => ({ ...it, name: gameId(it.name) })); const dup = items.map((x) => x.name).find((nm, i, a) => a.indexOf(nm) !== i); if (dup) { toast(`Two sounds are both named “${dup}”. Rename one.`); return; }
    const root = gameId(GAME.name).replace(/_/g, '-') || 'game-sounds'; GAME.busy = true; renderGame(); const files = []; const manifest = { format: 'soundwell-game-pack', name: GAME.name, sampleRate: 44100, sounds: [] };
    try {
      if (wantWav) {
        let done = 0; const total = items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.takes, 0);
        for (const it of items) {
          const s = bySlug[it.slug]; const names = [];
          for (let k = 0; k < it.takes; k++) { const buf = await gameTake(s, k); const f = `${it.name}-${k + 1}.wav`; names.push(f); files.push({ name: `${root}/sounds/${it.name}/${f}`, data: toWav(buf) }); done++; if (done % 4 === 0) toast(`Rendering takes… ${done} of ${total}`); }
          files.push({ name: `${root}/godot/${it.name}.tres`, data: enc(gameGodotTres(it, names)) });
          manifest.sounds.push({ name: it.name, title: s.title, loop: !!s.loop, takes: names.map((f) => `sounds/${it.name}/${f}`), pitchSpread: it.pitch, volume: it.vol, source: s.slug });
        }
        files.push({ name: `${root}/unity/SoundWell.cs`, data: enc(gameUnityCs(items)) });
      }
      if (wantRt) { files.push({ name: `${root}/web/soundwell.js`, data: enc(gameRuntimeJs(items)) }, { name: `${root}/web/demo.html`, data: enc(gameDemoHtml(items)) }); }
      if (wantWav) files.push({ name: `${root}/manifest.json`, data: enc(JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2)) });
      const first = items[0]?.name || 'name';
      files.push({ name: `${root}/README.md`, data: enc(`# ${GAME.name}\n\nGame sounds made with SoundWell. Every sound is CC0 1.0 (public domain): use them in any game, commercial or not. No credit required.\n\n${wantWav ? `Each sound has several takes in \`sounds/<name>/\`. Play a random one each time (and vary the pitch a little) so repeated sounds never feel robotic. \`manifest.json\` lists every take, its pitch spread and volume.\n\n## Godot 4\n\n1. Copy this whole folder into your project as \`res://soundwell/\`.\n2. Each \`godot/<name>.tres\` is an AudioStreamRandomizer that already holds the takes and pitch spread.\n3. Set it as the \`stream\` of an AudioStreamPlayer (or 2D/3D) and call \`play()\`.\n\n## Unity\n\n1. Copy the \`sounds\` folder to \`Assets/Resources/\` and rename it \`SoundWell\`.\n2. Copy \`unity/SoundWell.cs\` anywhere under \`Assets\`.\n3. Call \`SoundWell.Play(audioSource, "${first}")\` or \`SoundWell.PlayAt("${first}", transform.position)\`.\n\n## Unreal Engine 5\n\n1. Drag a sound's takes into the Content Browser to import them.\n2. Create a Sound Cue (or a MetaSound), add a **Random** node with all the takes, then a **Modulator** node for the pitch spread listed in \`manifest.json\`.\n3. Play the Sound Cue as usual.\n\n## Anything else\n\nThe WAVs are 16-bit, 44.1 kHz stereo and work in GameMaker, Defold, Phaser, FMOD, Wwise and any other tool.\n` : ''}${wantRt ? `\n## Web games: the procedural runtime\n\n\`web/soundwell.js\` doesn't contain audio. It contains the recipes, and generates the sounds in the player's browser, with a new take whenever you ask. Open \`web/demo.html\` to try it.\n\n\`\`\`html\n<script src="soundwell.js"><\/script>\n<script>\n  const sw = SoundWell();\n  await sw.load();            // render the takes once while your game loads\n  sw.play('${first}');           // a random take with a little pitch spread\n  sw.play('${first}', { volume: 0.5, pan: -0.3 });\n  sw.refresh('${first}');        // swap in brand-new takes in the background\n<\/script>\n\`\`\`\n\nIt works with Phaser, PixiJS, three.js or plain canvas. Pass your own context with \`SoundWell({ context: yourAudioContext })\`.\n` : ''}`) });
      files.push({ name: `${root}/LICENSE.txt`, data: enc(`Sounds in this pack were made with SoundWell and are dedicated to the public domain under CC0 1.0.\nhttps://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\nUse them for anything, commercial work included. No credit required.\n`) });
      await saveZip(`${root}.zip`, files);
    } catch { toast('Couldn’t build the pack. Try fewer takes.'); }
    GAME.busy = false; renderGame();
  }

  // game audio events
  const gameview = $('#gameview');
  gameview.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const pv = e.target.closest('[data-preview]'); if (pv) { seqPreview(pv.dataset.preview); return; }
    const ga = e.target.closest('[data-gadd]'); if (ga) { if (gameAdd(bySlug[ga.dataset.gadd])) { ga.disabled = true; ga.querySelector('.use').textContent = 'Added'; $('#game-list').innerHTML = gameListHtml(); } return; }
    const ch = e.target.closest('[data-gchip]'); if (ch) { const q = $('#game-q'); q.value = GAME.q = ch.dataset.gchip; gameResults(q.value); return; }
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-g]'); if (!b || b.tagName === 'INPUT' || b.tagName === 'SELECT') return; const a = b.dataset.g; const row = b.closest('[data-gi]'); const i = row ? +row.dataset.gi : -1;
    if (a === 'add') { GAME.picker = true; renderGame(); $('#game-q')?.focus(); }
    else if (a === 'pickclose') { GAME.picker = false; pvStop(); renderGame(); }
    else if (a === 'addsaved' || a === 'addmine') { const pool = a === 'addsaved' ? [...saved].map((x) => bySlug[x]).filter(Boolean) : SOUNDS.filter((s) => s.custom); let k = 0; for (const s of pool) if (gameAdd(s, true)) k++; toast(k ? `Added ${k} sound${k === 1 ? '' : 's'}.` : a === 'addsaved' ? 'No new saved sounds. Tap ♥ on sounds to save them.' : 'No new creations. Make one with the recipe knobs or “Describe a sound”.'); renderGame(); }
    else if (a === 'clear') { GAME.items = []; gameSave(); renderGame(); }
    else if (a === 'rm') { GAME.items.splice(i, 1); gameSave(); renderGame(); }
    else if (a === 'hear') gameHearTakes(i);
    else if (a === 'export') gameExport();
  });
  gameview.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; if (el.id === 'game-q') { GAME.q = el.value; gameResults(el.value); return; } if (el.id === 'game-name') { GAME.name = el.value.slice(0, 40) || 'my-game-sounds'; gameSave(); return; }
    const row = el.closest('[data-gi]'); if (!row) return; const it = GAME.items[+row.dataset.gi]; const a = el.dataset.g;
    if (a === 'name') { it.name = el.value; gameSave(); } else if (a === 'vol') { it.vol = el.value / 100; gameSave(); }
  });
  gameview.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const el = e.target; const row = el.closest('[data-gi]'); if (!row) return; const it = GAME.items[+row.dataset.gi]; const a = el.dataset.g;
    if (a === 'name') { it.name = gameId(el.value); el.value = it.name; gameSave(); } else if (a === 'takes') { it.takes = +el.value; gameSave(); renderGame(); } else if (a === 'pitch') { it.pitch = +el.value; gameSave(); }
  });
  gameview.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.target.id === 'game-q' && e.key === 'Enter') { const first = $('#game-results [data-gadd]:not([disabled])'); if (first && gameAdd(bySlug[first.dataset.gadd])) { gameResults(GAME.q || ''); $('#game-list').innerHTML = gameListHtml(); } } });

  // ---------- events ----------
  list.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const tag = e.target.closest('[data-tag]'); if (tag) { state.q = tag.dataset.tag; $('#q').value = state.q; state.sort = 'best'; $('#sort').value = 'best'; state.open = null; render(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0 }); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('#morebtn')) { showMore(); return; }
    const g = e.target.closest('[data-goto]'); if (g) { goto(g.dataset.goto); return; }
    const kb = e.target.closest('[data-kit]'); if (kb) { loadKit(kb.dataset.kit); return; }
    const el = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); if (!el) return;
    const s = bySlug[el.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; const act = el.dataset.act;
    if (act === 'play') toggle(s);
    else if (act === 'open') open(s.slug);
    else if (act === 'seek') { const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect(); const x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width)); if (cur === s && P.buf) { if (P.playing) pSeek(x * P.buf.duration); else pStartAt(x * P.buf.duration); } else load(s, x); }
    else if (act === 'save') { saved.has(s.slug) ? saved.delete(s.slug) : saved.add(s.slug); store.set('saved', [...saved]); const on = saved.has(s.slug); el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on)); el.setAttribute('aria-label', `${on ? 'Remove from saved' : 'Save'}: ${s.title}`); toast(on ? 'Saved to this browser' : 'Removed from saved'); if (state.saved) render(); else chips(); }
    else if (act === 'dl') download(s);
    else if (act === 'copy') copy(s, el);
    else if (act === 'seq') addToSeq(s);
    else if (act === 'game') gameAdd(s);
    else if (act === 'mix') { addToMix(s); if (state.open === s.slug) { state.open = null; open(s.slug); } }
    else if (act === 'edit') { state.edit = state.edit === s.slug ? null : s.slug; state.open = null; open(s.slug); if (state.edit) document.getElementById('e-' + s.slug)?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' }); }
    else if (act === 'delmine') { const i = SOUNDS.indexOf(s); if (i >= 0) SOUNDS.splice(i, 1); delete bySlug[s.slug]; removeFromMix(s.slug); saveMine(); state.open = null; toast(`Deleted “${s.title}”`); render(); }
    else if (act === 'eplay') previewEdit(s);
    else if (act === 'kdice') { const m = km(s); m.seed = (m.seed || 0) + 1; const t = el.closest('.knobs').querySelector('[data-ko="seed"]'); if (t) t.textContent = 'Take ' + (m.seed + 1); previewEdit(s); }
    else if (act === 'kreset') { knobs.delete(s.slug); state.open = null; open(s.slug); }
    else if (act === 'ksave') {
      const m = { ...km(s) }; const o = { ...eo(s) }; const what = modDesc(m);
      const c = { slug: 'my-' + Date.now().toString(36), title: `${s.title} (my take)`.slice(0, 60), text: s.title, base: s.slug, o, m, desc: `Your take on ${s.title}${what ? ` (${what})` : ''}. Made with SoundWell's recipe knobs.` };
      const mine = addCustom(c); saveMine(); toast(`Saved “${mine.title}” to Your creations. Add it to the sequencer like any sound.`);
    }
    else if (act === 'edl') downloadEdit(s, false);
    else if (act === 'evar') downloadEdit(s, true);
    else if (act === 'ereset') { edits.delete(s.slug); state.open = null; open(s.slug); }
  });
  list.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const kn = e.target.closest('[data-k]'); if (kn) { const s = bySlug[kn.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; const d = KNOBS.find((x) => x.k === kn.dataset.k); km(s)[d.k] = +kn.value; const o = kn.closest('.knobs').querySelector(`[data-ko="${d.k}"]`); if (o) o.textContent = d.fmt(+kn.value); return; }
    const el = e.target.closest('[data-e]'); if (!el) return; const s = bySlug[el.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; const o = eo(s); const k = el.dataset.e; const v = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : +el.value;
    const out = (txt) => { const x = el.closest('.editor').querySelector(`[data-o="${k}"]`); if (x) x.textContent = txt; };
    if (k === 'speed') { o.speed = v / 100; out(`${o.speed.toFixed(2)}×`); }
    else if (k === 'gain') { o.gain = v; out(`${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v} dB`); }
    else if (k === 'start') { o.start = Math.min(v / 1000, o.end - 0.01); out(`${(o.start * s.dur).toFixed(2)} s`); }
    else if (k === 'end') { o.end = Math.max(v / 1000, o.start + 0.01); out(`${(o.end * s.dur).toFixed(2)} s`); }
    else if (k === 'fin') { o.fin = v / 100 * fadeMax(s); out(`${o.fin.toFixed(1)} s`); }
    else if (k === 'fout') { o.fout = v / 100 * fadeMax(s); out(`${o.fout.toFixed(1)} s`); }
    else if (k === 'rev') o.rev = v;
    drawEdit(s);
  });
  list.addEventListener('change', (e) => { const kn = e.target.closest('[data-k]'); if (kn) { previewEdit(bySlug[kn.closest('.row').dataset.slug]); return; } });
  list.addEventListener('change', (e) => { if (e.target.matches('[data-e="rev"]')) { const s = bySlug[e.target.closest('.row').dataset.slug]; eo(s).rev = e.target.checked; drawEdit(s); } });
  home.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const k = e.target.closest('[data-kit]'); if (k) { loadKit(k.dataset.kit); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('#ss-go')) { songStarter(); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('#desc-go')) { createSound($('#desc-in').value); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('#desc-retry')) { createSound($('#desc-in').value, { retry: true }); return; }
    const ex = e.target.closest('[data-ex]'); if (ex) { $('#desc-in').value = ex.dataset.ex; createSound(ex.dataset.ex); return; }
    const mp = e.target.closest('[data-mine-play]'); if (mp) { toggle(bySlug[mp.dataset.minePlay]); return; }
    const mo = e.target.closest('[data-mine-open]'); if (mo) { goto(mo.dataset.mineOpen); return; }
    const q = e.target.closest('[data-kitq]'); if (q) { state.q = q.dataset.kitq; $('#q').value = state.q; render(); return; }
    if (e.target.closest('#scene2go')) { const t = $('#scene2').value; $('#scene').value = t; openMixer(); buildScene(t); }
  });
  $('#mixbtn').addEventListener('click', () => ($('#mixer').hidden ? openMixer() : closeMixer()));
  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const nb = e.target.closest('[data-nav]'); if (nb) { e.preventDefault(); nav(nb.dataset.nav); return; }
    const pb = e.target.closest('[data-pack]'); if (pb) { e.preventDefault(); openPack(pb.dataset.pack); }
  });
  let heroT = 0; window.addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(heroT); heroT = setTimeout(drawHero, 150); });
  $('#mixclose').addEventListener('click', closeMixer);
  $('#mixplay').addEventListener('click', () => (mixOn ? mixStop() : mixStart()));
  $('#mixclear').addEventListener('click', () => { mixStop(); mix = []; saveMix(); renderMixer(); syncMixButtons(); });
  $('#mixvol').addEventListener('input', (e) => { mixVol = e.target.value / 100; store.set('mixvol', mixVol); if (master) master.gain.setTargetAtTime(mixVol, actx.currentTime, 0.02); });
  $('#mixexport').addEventListener('click', exportMix);
  $('#scenego').addEventListener('click', () => buildScene($('#scene').value));
  $('#scene').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); buildScene(e.target.value); } });
  $('#layers').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-m]'); if (!b || b.dataset.m === 'vol') return; const slug = b.closest('.layer').dataset.slug; const l = mix.find((x) => x.slug === slug); if (!l) return;
    if (b.dataset.m === 'trig') trigger(l);
    else if (b.dataset.m === 'rm') removeFromMix(slug);
    else if (b.dataset.m === 'mute') { l.mute = !l.mute; saveMix(); const x = live.get(slug); if (x) x.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(l.mute ? 0 : l.vol, actx.currentTime, 0.02); renderMixer(); }
  });
  $('#layers').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    if (e.target.dataset.m !== 'vol') return; const slug = e.target.closest('.layer').dataset.slug; const l = mix.find((x) => x.slug === slug); if (!l) return;
    l.vol = e.target.value / 100; saveMix(); const x = live.get(slug); if (x && !l.mute) x.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(l.vol, actx.currentTime, 0.02);
  });
  let qT = 0;
  $('#q').addEventListener('input', (e) => { clearTimeout(qT); qT = setTimeout(() => { state.q = e.target.value.trim(); state.open = null; if (state.view === 'packs' || state.view === 'seq' || state.view === 'video' || state.view === 'game') state.view = 'list'; if (state.q && state.sort !== 'best') { /* keep user's sort */ } render(); }, 120); });
  $('#q').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.target.value = ''; state.q = ''; render(); } if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); e.target.blur(); } });
  document.querySelector('.seg').addEventListener('click', (e) => { const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return; state.kind = b.dataset.kind; state.cats.clear(); state.open = null; syncSeg(); render(); });
  $('#sort').addEventListener('change', (e) => { state.sort = e.target.value; render(); });
  $('#keyf').addEventListener('change', (e) => { state.key = e.target.value; state.open = null; render(); });
  $('#bpmf').addEventListener('change', (e) => { state.bpm = +e.target.value; state.open = null; render(); });
  $('#chips').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b || b.disabled) return; state.open = null;
    if (b.dataset.opt) state[b.dataset.opt] = !state[b.dataset.opt];
    else { const c = b.dataset.cat; state.cats.has(c) ? state.cats.delete(c) : state.cats.add(c); }
    render();
  });
  $('#clear').addEventListener('click', () => { resetFilters(); state.browse = true; render(); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;
    const typing = /^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement?.tagName) && document.activeElement.type !== 'range';
    if (e.key === '/' && !typing) { e.preventDefault(); $('#q').focus(); return; }
    if (typing) return;
    const onButton = document.activeElement?.tagName === 'BUTTON';
    if (e.key === ' ' && !onButton) { e.preventDefault(); if (state.view === 'seq') (SEQ.playing ? seqStop() : seqPlay()); else toggle(); }
    else if (e.key === 'j' || e.key === 'J') step(1);
    else if (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K') step(-1);
    else if ((e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') && cur && P.buf && document.activeElement?.type !== 'range') pSeek(pTime() + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 5 : -5));
    else if (e.key === 'l' || e.key === 'L') $('#loopbtn').click();
    else if (e.key === 'm' || e.key === 'M') $('#mixbtn').click();
    else if (e.key === 'Escape' && !$('#mixer').hidden) closeMixer();
    else if (/^[1-9]$/.test(e.key)) { const pads = mix.filter((l) => !bySlug[l.slug].loop); const l = pads[+e.key - 1]; if (l) trigger(l); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => { const slug = location.hash.slice(1); if (bySlug[slug] && state.open !== slug) goto(slug); });

  // ---------- boot ----------
  for (const c of store.get(MINE_KEY, []).reverse()) if (c && c.slug && c.base) addCustom(c);
  readColors(); statsLine(); render(); renderMixer();
  const h = location.hash.slice(1); if (bySlug[h]) goto(h);
})();
</script>
